Method: 引入计算神经科学中的统计决策置信度（SDC）规范框架，将答案对数几率差（LD）视为潜在决策变量的候选读数，在三个感知辨别任务、一个基于记忆的决策任务以及复杂视觉推理任务中，跨多个多模态非推理模型和一个推理模型，检验SDC预测的定性特征（如正确/错误的折叠X模式）。

Result: 在感知和记忆任务中，LD满足SDC的所有定性特征，表明答案对数几率在此类设置中表现为潜在决策变量的单调读数而非启发式偏好分数。在复杂视觉推理中，LD仍能预测正确性（超越客观任务难度），但完整的SDC几何特征缺失。

Conclusion: 答案对数几率在一定条件下可作为潜在决策变量的可靠读数，SDC框架为理解生物和人工智能中的置信度提供了统一的理论基础，同时也揭示了当前框架在缺乏显式规范过程模型时的边界。

Abstract: Reliable confidence -- the probability that a model's own answer is correct -- is essential for the trustworthy deployment of language models. Existing work has largely evaluated confidence by how well it predicts correctness and whether it is calibrated, leaving open a more fundamental question: what does the confidence signal itself represent? Answer logits may reflect a latent decision variable sufficient to compute normative confidence, or instead a heuristic preference signal that combines the available evidence in a non-Bayesian manner. We address this using statistical decision confidence (SDC), a normative framework from computational neuroscience. Treating the answer-logit difference (LD) as a candidate readout of the latent decision variable, we test the qualitative signatures predicted by SDC. Across three perceptual discrimination tasks and a memory-based decision task, spanning three multimodal non-reasoning models and one reasoning model, LD satisfied these signatures -- including the diagnostic correct/error folded-X pattern -- showing that, in these settings, answer logits behave as monotonic readouts of a latent decision variable rather than heuristic preference scores. In complex visual reasoning, LD continued to predict correctness beyond objective task difficulty, but the full geometric signatures of SDC were absent, illustrating the current boundary of the framework when explicit normative process models are unavailable. These results provide a computational account of confidence in multimodal language models, delineate when answer logits behave as readouts of a latent decision variable, and establish SDC as a unifying framework for studying confidence across biological and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [73] [Exploring Zero-Shot Foundation Models for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12454)
*Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Roland Kwitt,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究了将单变量时间序列预测基础模型TimesFM零样本应用于多变量时间序列异常检测（MTSAD）的效果。在SWaT基准测试中，无论是将该基础模型用作逐特征预测器还是嵌入器，其性能均未达到现有基线水平。原因是模型过于擅长捕捉时间动态，导致异常窗口内的预测误差过低，无法区分正常与异常。但模型在异常边界处产生高误差，表明其适用于变化点检测而非MTSAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MTSAD方法依赖特定应用模型，训练成本高且难以扩展。近期出现的大规模预训练基础模型在单变量时间序列预测上展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，因此作者探究这类模型能否直接用于MTSAD而无需任务特定训练。

Method: 在SWaT基准上零样本应用单变量预测基础模型TimesFM，采用两种策略：(1)作为逐特征预测器，基于阈值化预测误差进行异常检测；(2)作为嵌入器，提取中间表示输入标准异常检测器。

Result: 两种策略均未达到现有基线性能。嵌入表示仅部分分离正常与异常片段，不足以实现可靠检测。模型对异常窗口的预测误差过低，导致持续异常与正常行为难以区分。但模型在异常边界处产生预测误差峰值。

Conclusion: 所提出的朴素零样本基础模型不适合用于MTSAD，但其在异常边界处的高误差表明这类模型在变化点检测任务上具有潜力。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) is essential for reliability and safety in domains such as industrial process monitoring and financial risk management, yet conventional approaches rely on application-specific models that are costly to train and hard to scale. Foundation Models (FMs), pre-trained on broad data with strong zero-shot generalization, have recently become available for univariate time series forecasting, raising the question of whether they can address MTSAD without task-specific training. We investigate the zero-shot application of a univariate forecasting FM, TimesFM, to industrial MTSAD on the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT) benchmark, evaluating two strategies: treating the FM as a per-feature forecaster with thresholded prediction errors, and as an embedder whose intermediate representations feed standard outlier detectors. Neither of our proposed setups is competitive with established baselines; embeddings reveal only partial separation between normal and anomalous segments, insufficient for reliable detection. The cause is that the FM is too effective at capturing temporal dynamics, yielding low error even within fully anomalous windows, so persistent anomalies become indistinguishable from normal behavior. However, these observations yield valuable insights: the error peaks at anomaly boundaries, indicating FMs reliably detect distribution changes. We conclude that the proposed naive zero-shot FMs are unsuitable for MTSAD but promising for change-point detection.

</details>


### [74] [Sample Efficient Generative Optimization for Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12488)
*Sarina Kopf,Cristina Nevado,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEGO是一个样本高效的分子优化框架，通过贝叶斯优化与自适应生成模型结合，仅用其他方法十分之一的oracle调用次数即可达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现、材料设计和催化中的分子优化需要在大化学空间中搜索，但高保真oracle和实验测量成本高昂，因此亟需样本高效的优化方法。

Method: SEGO框架包含四个核心步骤：概率代理模型假设化学空间中高奖励区域；生成模型在该区域定向生成候选分子；通过采集函数选择最有希望的候选；使用oracle调用结果更新代理模型并锚定生成器。

Result: 在PMO基准上仅用其他方法十分之一的oracle调用达到最先进性能；在多参数对接任务中，约以现有方法一半的oracle调用次数达到十个命中。

Conclusion: SEGO的样本效率提升使分子优化更接近直接由实验反馈驱动的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Molecular optimization in drug discovery, materials design, and catalysis requires searching vast chemical spaces under tight evaluation budgets, since high-fidelity oracles and experimental measurements are costly. The practical impact of an optimization method therefore hinges on its sample efficiency: how few evaluations it needs to find strong candidates. We introduce Sample Efficient Generative Optimization (SEGO), a framework for Bayesian optimization on adaptively generated molecules. In SEGO, a probabilistic surrogate model forms a hypothesis about where hits lie in chemical space, a generative model is steered to propose candidates in that region, the most promising candidate is selected via an acquisition function, and the resulting oracle call is used both to sharpen the surrogate and to anchor the generator in real reward. SEGO attains state-of-the-art performance on the practical molecular optimization (PMO) benchmark using only one tenth of the oracle calls consumed by other methods, and on a multiparameter docking task it reaches ten hits in roughly half the oracle calls of existing approaches. These gains move molecular optimization closer to campaigns driven by direct experimental feedback.

</details>


### [75] [Adversarial Attacks on Online Handwriting using Salience-based Temporal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12500)
*Yataro Tamura,Brian Kenji Iwana,Jiseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于显著性引导的时间编辑对抗攻击方法，通过在时间序列上插入和删除关键点而非添加噪声，生成对抗样本，在保持手写轨迹平滑性和视觉结构的同时，实现了更强的单次黑盒迁移攻击性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击方法主要针对图像输入设计，依赖加性空间扰动，应用到在线手写识别（时间序列笔迹轨迹）时会产生高频抖动和明显不自然的笔画伪影，亟需一种适配时序特性的对抗攻击方法。

Method: 提出基于显著性引导的时间编辑框架：利用梯度激活映射估计时间显著性，选择对原始类别预测贡献最大的时间步，在这些步上进行点的插入和删除操作，而非添加噪声扰动，从而保持笔迹的形状和平滑性。

Result: 在Unipen和CASIA-OLHWDB数据集上，传统图像攻击在白盒设置下表现强但迁移性差；所提时间编辑攻击在保持手写视觉结构的同时，实现了更强的单次黑盒迁移攻击性能。

Conclusion: 时间编辑攻击对在线手写识别构成了现实威胁，尤其在单次黑盒迁移场景下，表明时序对抗攻击是值得关注的研究方向。

Abstract: Deep learning models for online handwriting recognition have been shown effective and are increasingly deployed in practical applications. However, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks is still a challenge. Existing adversarial methods are predominantly designed for image-based inputs and typically rely on additive spatial perturbations. When applied to online handwriting, which is inherently represented as a time series of pen trajectories, such perturbations often introduce high-frequency jitter and visibly unnatural stroke artifacts. In this work, we propose a novel adversarial attack framework for online handwriting recognition based on salience-guided temporal editing. Instead of adding noise, the proposed method generates adversarial examples by inserting and deleting points at time steps selected according to temporal salience, preserving the shape and smoothness of the original handwriting. Temporal salience is estimated using gradient-based activation mapping, which guides edits toward time steps that strongly support the original class prediction. We evaluate the proposed approach on the Unipen and CASIA-OLHWDB datasets under both white-box and one-shot black-box attack settings. Experimental results demonstrate that while conventional image-based attacks achieve strong white-box performance, they exhibit poor transferability across models. In contrast, the proposed temporal editing attack achieves stronger one-shot black-box transferability while preserving the visual structure of the handwriting. These results indicate that temporal editing is a relevant threat model for online handwriting recognition, particularly in one-shot black-box transfer settings.

</details>


### [76] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从显式生成模型的角度重新解释了Forward-Forward算法，证明其平方 goodness 是尺度差异下似然比检验的充分统计量，FF阈值是决策边界，并推广到各向异性、重尾等分布，阐明了层间归一化必须去除长度而保留坐标能量，以及成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward算法中平方goodness和层间归一化通常被视为启发式方法，缺乏理论基础，本文旨在建立其显式生成模型解释。

Method: 将FF算法的平方goodness解释为两个零均值尺度差异总体间似然比检验的充分统计量，推广到各向异性（马氏距离）、重尾（饱和统计量，斜率为后验精度）情况，并分析层间归一化必须去除长度而保留坐标能量，以及成对目标中的尺度膨胀捷径。

Result: 平方goodness是似然比检验充分统计量；推广到马氏goodness和饱和统计量（后验精度即除法归一化）；层间归一化需去除长度保留坐标能量；成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，白化goodness可消除。

Conclusion: FF算法的启发式设计在显式生成模型下具有统计理论基础，为理解局部学习算法提供了新视角。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary. It generalizes: anisotropic populations yield a Mahalanobis goodness, the plain square being its isotropic case; heavy-tailed populations yield a saturating statistic whose slope is a posterior precision - divisive normalization - with bounded evidence and an advantage only under aggregation. The same lens characterizes the inter-layer normalization: it must remove the length while preserving per-coordinate energy, explaining a depth collapse we observe under unit-norm normalization; and the pairwise objective admits a scale-inflation shortcut that a whitened goodness removes.

</details>


### [77] [OOD-RL-Bench: A Benchmark Framework for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12523)
*Emil Mittag,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OOD-RL-Bench框架，用于评估强化学习轨迹中的分布外检测方法，实验发现观测扰动和机制切换检测准确率高，而观测延迟和动作条件动力学检测困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测基准主要评估图像分类器或静态低维数据集，未能考虑RL轨迹中复杂的、动作依赖的时序结构，缺乏专门针对RL轨迹异常检测的评估框架。

Method: 构建了OOD-RL-Bench框架，通过共享接口和配置集成检测器与异常注入器；在LunarLander-v3环境中使用DQN策略进行评估，采用匹配时间AUROC、AUPRC、误报率、检测延迟和分段发作等指标。

Result: 观测扰动和机制切换可被多种方法高准确率识别；观测延迟和动作条件动力学即使在相同时间步比较异常分数与干净分数时仍然难以检测；不同异常类型间检测性能差异显著。

Conclusion: OOD-RL-Bench作为可扩展的基准框架，揭示了RL中OOD检测的挑战，尤其是指出了当前方法在处理时序依赖型异常方面的不足，并公开了框架、策略检查点和完整结果供复现。

Abstract: Reliable reinforcement learning (RL) agents must maintain operational integrity amidst sensor malfunctions, dynamic disturbances, and slow environmental shifts. The detection of out-of-distribution conditions is pivotal to determining when an agent's observations, transitions, or trajectory dynamics deviate from the assumptions underpinning its policy training. Current out-of-distribution (OOD) detection benchmarks typically evaluate image classifiers or static low-dimensional datasets, failing to account for the complex, action-dependent temporal structure inherent in RL trajectories. To address this gap, we present OOD-RL-Bench, a comprehensive and extensible framework designed to evaluate OOD detectors against categories of anomalies injected into RL trajectories. Detectors and anomaly injectors are integrated through shared interfaces and configuration, which allows new scoring methods and perturbation families to be evaluated without modification of the core benchmark loop. We evaluate the utility of the framework using a Deep Q-Network policy within the LunarLander-v3 environment. We assess the performance of each detector across a suite of anomaly types using matched-time AUROC, matched-time AUPRC, matched-time false-positive rate, detection delay, and segmented-onset metrics. Our analysis reveals significant performance variance across anomaly types: observation perturbations and regime switches are identified with high accuracy by several methods, while observation delay and action-conditioned dynamics remain difficult even when post-onset anomaly scores are compared against clean scores from the same timesteps. We make the framework, trained policy checkpoint, and complete results publicly available as a reproducible artefact.

</details>


### [78] [From Preimage Search To Source-Grounded Feature Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12526)
*Kaixiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于源的（source-grounded）特征反演方法，通过局部修复反向传播信号（使用维纳映射），在单次反向传播中实现对神经网络内部特征的可解释可视化，无需逐查询优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统特征反演方法只匹配目标特征，忽略了生成该特征的原始输入（源）信息，导致反演结果不唯一且缺乏与具体样本的关联。

Method: 在计算图边界处，用封闭形式的维纳映射修复反向传播信号：第一个映射从均值种子VJP修复到上游状态，第二个映射修正JVP前向一致性残差；将修复后的状态沿同一DAG单次反向传播组合，形成零截距映射族，支持跨架构、通道和视觉分布的新输入。

Result: 对标配的目标与源控制实验验证了反演结果依赖于所选特征和待解释样本的局部算子，而非与目标无关的图像模板；预测条件特征图谱与对内部特征的独立干预结果一致。

Conclusion: 基于源的特征反演使模型隐藏的特征层次结构在单层和单通道级别上可检视，将网络从输入中提取的特征与塑造其决策的内部证据联系起来。

Abstract: Interpreting a neural network requires understanding what its internal features extract from a particular input. Feature inversion seeks to express a selected feature in the input domain, but canonical iterative methods search for an input whose re-encoded representation matches the target. Because many inputs can satisfy this constraint, target matching alone does not specify the inverse associated with the sample that generated the feature. We formulate source-grounded feature inversion by conditioning the inverse on the source-local network geometry at the target-generating input. At each boundary of the computational DAG, backpropagation provides the correct reverse dependencies but transports an adjoint signal rather than an upstream-state estimate. We locally repair this signal with a closed-form matrix Wiener map from a mean-seed VJP to the upstream state, followed by a second Wiener map for the JVP forward-consistency residual, and compose the repaired states through the same DAG in one finite reverse pass. One calibrated zero-intercept map family supports new inputs, depths, channels, and channel groups across diverse CNN and Transformer architectures, tensor components, and visual distributions without query-specific optimisation. Matched target and source controls verify that each inverse depends on the selected feature and the local operators of the sample being explained, rather than a target-independent image template. Prediction-conditioned feature atlases align these visualisations with independent interventions on the corresponding internal features. Together, source-grounded feature inversion opens the model's hidden feature hierarchy to inspection at the level of individual layers and channels, linking what the network extracts from an input to the internal evidence that shapes its decision.

</details>


### [79] [A JoLT for the KV Cache: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression via Joint Tucker and JL-Residual Allocation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12550)
*Rahul Krishnan,Volker Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JoLT 提出了一种基于三阶张量视角的 KV 缓存压缩方法，通过部分 Tucker 分解压缩 token 和特征轴，并用 Johnson-Lindenstrauss 旋转低比特残差恢复截断能量，在单字节预算下用对偶变量联合分配 Tucker 秩和残差比特宽度，实现了近无损的 2-3 倍压缩，在 Mistral-7B 和 LLaMA-2-13B 上困惑度、GSM8K 精度和 RULER 检索均保持与未压缩基线相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV 缓存随着批次大小、上下文长度和深度增长，成为 Transformer 推理的主要内存开销，尤其在长上下文场景下限制了吞吐量。现有低秩方法和量化方法分别压缩缓存的二维切片或降低比特宽度，但均未利用缓存天然是三维张量（头轴、token 轴、特征轴）且三个轴携带不同冗余量的特性。

Method: 将 KV 缓存视为三维张量，对 token 轴和特征轴进行部分 Tucker 分解压缩，保留头轴和层轴不变；对截断丢弃的能量用 Johnson-Lindenstrauss 旋转后的低比特残差进行补偿；通过单个拉格朗日对偶变量，在每层组内为键和值分别联合分配 Tucker 秩和残差比特宽度，满足单字节预算约束。

Result: 在 Mistral-7B 和 LLaMA-2-13B 上实现 2-3 倍压缩，困惑度、GSM8K 准确率和 RULER 检索性能均在统计噪声范围内。2 倍压缩时键和值的相对 Frobenius 误差分别为 0.009 和 0.006，比跨层 SVD 和 4-bit 量化低约一个数量级。FlashJoLT 变体实现 5-13 倍压缩加速。

Conclusion: JoLT 通过张量分解和 JL 旋转残差的结合，充分利用了 KV 缓存的多轴冗余结构，显著优于传统低秩和量化方法，且对多种注意力架构均有效。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become the dominant memory cost of transformer inference. It grows with batch size, context length, and depth, and at long context it, rather than the model weights, sets the ceiling on throughput. Two families of methods reduce it. Low-rank methods factor two-dimensional slices of the cache, either per-head matrices or cross-layer feature blocks, and quantization methods lower the bit-width of every entry. Neither family exploits the fact that the cache at a layer is naturally a third-order tensor whose three axes, the heads, the tokens, and the features, carry very different amounts of redundancy. We take this tensor view directly. Our method, JoLT, applies a partial Tucker decomposition that compresses only the token and feature axes while leaving the head and layer axes intact, and then restores the energy that truncation discards with a Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) rotated low-bit residual. A single Lagrangian dual allocates the Tucker ranks and the residual bit-widths together, per layer group and separately for keys and values, under one byte budget. The result is a near-lossless 2-3x compression: perplexity, GSM8K accuracy, and RULER needle-in-a-haystack retrieval all stay at or within statistical noise of the uncompressed baseline on both a grouped-query-attention model (Mistral-7B-v0.3) and a multi-head-attention model (LLaMA-2-13B). At 2x, JoLT reconstructs the cache to relative Frobenius error 0.009 (K) and 0.006 (V) on both architectures, roughly an order of magnitude below cross-layer SVD and 4-bit quantization. A randomized-SVD variant, FlashJoLT, delivers a 5-13x compression-time speedup at matched quality.

</details>


### [80] [Lightweight Multi-Scale Anomaly Detection for Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12599)
*Raheen Junaid Wani,Smruti R. Sarangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级多尺度自编码器（LMSAE），利用离散小波变换提取多尺度特征，在不到500KB模型体积下实现高效时间序列异常检测，在Jetson Nano上延迟降低9倍、功耗降低2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度时间序列异常检测方法计算开销大，对细微异常的多尺度敏感性不足，难以满足物联网、传感器网络和边缘监控场景对内存、延迟和功耗的严格约束。

Method: 基于轻量级自编码器架构，引入离散小波变换（DWT）进行多尺度特征提取，并设计多尺度损失函数以增强对细微或隐藏异常的敏感性。

Result: 在基准数据集上以不到500KB的模型体积达到有竞争力或更优的检测性能；在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上实现推理延迟降低9倍、功耗降低2倍。

Conclusion: LMSAE是一种兼具高检测性能与极低计算资源消耗的轻量级时间序列异常检测方案，非常适合边缘部署场景。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is increasingly important in IoT systems, sensor networks, and edge monitoring applications, where models must operate under strict constraints on memory, latency, and power consumption. While recent deep-learning approaches have improved detection accuracy, many remain computationally expensive and often fail to capture subtle anomalies due to limited multi-scale sensitivity. Autoencoders are widely used for anomaly detection because they reconstruct normal patterns well, leading to elevated reconstruction errors for anomalous inputs. Their simplicity and efficiency also make them suitable lightweight backbones for handling multi-scale inputs. To address these challenges, we propose a Lightweight MultiScale AutoEncoder (LMSAE) network for univariate time-series anomaly detection, designed to be compact and computationally efficient. LMSAE leverages the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to extract multi-scale features and employs a multi-scale loss function to improve sensitivity to subtle or hidden anomalies. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive or superior detection performance despite using significantly fewer parameters and a model size of less than 500 KB. LMSAE also achieves low-latency, low-power inference on the NVIDIA Jetson Nano, with 9x reduction in inference latency and 2x reduction in power consumption, making it ideal for edge deployment.

</details>


### [81] [The Geometry of Memorization: Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity as a Diagnostic for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12616)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限时间谱灵敏度(FTSS)指标g(t)，通过追踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值来揭示连续时间生成框架中的流动几何结构，并发现过拟合会导致谱坍塌，基于此开发了一种无需外部查询的内部几何审计方法来检测生成记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成框架虽然理论上偏好直线轨迹，但经验网络会发展出复杂的路径变形，现有方法难以有效揭示这种流动几何结构。同时，数据稀缺场景下的过拟合检测缺乏基于内部轨迹动力学的无梯度方法。

Method: 提出FTSS g(t)指标，通过追踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值作为稳定秩的连续代理，实现无梯度、前向传播的流几何结构分析。基于g(t)在数据稀缺时表现出的谱坍塌现象，构建内部几何审计框架。

Result: 发现泛化模型能维持稳定的有效维度，而过拟合会导致谱坍塌（光谱崩溃），利用这一结构现象可检测生成记忆。

Conclusion: 基于g(t)的内部几何审计框架能够仅使用内部轨迹动力学检测生成记忆，无需外部成员查询或基线数据对比。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative frameworks construct probability paths between base and target domains by optimizing time-dependent velocity fields. While theoretical targets favor straight trajectories, empirical networks develop complex path deformations. This paper presents the Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity (FTSS) g(t), a gradient-free, forward-pass metric that exposes flow geometry by tracking the root-mean-square singular value of the state-transition matrix. Serving as a continuous proxy for stable rank, g(t) reveals a distinct geometric pathology under data scarcity: while generalizing models maintain stable effective dimensions, overfitting causes a spectral collapse. We leverage this structural phenomenon to develop an internal geometric audit based on g(t). Our framework detects generative memorization using purely internal trajectory dynamics, removing the need for external membership queries or baseline data comparison.

</details>


### [82] [Learning Forced Multibody Dynamics on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12627)
*Martine Dyring Hansen,Marta Ghirardelli,Elena Celledoni,David Martin de Diego,Brynjulf Owren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程的机械系统动力学学习架构，仅使用位置数据并在李群上运行，自然地保留了系统的几何结构和守恒律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要速度数据或忽略系统的几何结构，而真实场景中速度数据可能不可用或充满噪声。该工作旨在仅利用位置数据学习机械系统动力学，同时保持几何不变量和守恒律。

Method: 基于离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程，在李群流形上直接构建动力学模型，仅使用位置测量数据，并扩展到多体系统和外部控制输入场景。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上均表现出良好的性能，验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为仅依赖位置数据的机械系统动力学学习提供了一种几何保结构的方法，适用于速度数据不可靠或缺失的实际场景，具有良好的扩展性。

Abstract: We propose an architecture for learning the dynamics of mechanical systems based on discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations on Lie groups using only position data. By formulating the dynamics directly on manifold-valued configuration spaces, the method naturally respects the geometric structure of the systems and preserves geometric invariants and conservation laws. The reliance on position measurements alone makes the framework applicable in settings where velocity data are unavailable or noisy. The approach extends naturally to multibody systems, accommodates external control inputs, and demonstrates strong performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [83] [AdaPCLA: Adaptive Prior-Calibrated Logit Adjustment for Long-Tailed Longitudinal EHR Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12645)
*Shuai Cui,Chen Wenxuan,Wenjie Du,Jian Lou,Dan Li,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaPCLA框架通过数据分布感知的模拟退火训练策略，使生成模型能够更好地适应纵向电子健康记录中的罕见事件共现结构，同时支持零样本跨群体自适应生成，显著提升了尾部事件的生成质量和下游任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归模型在生成纵向EHR数据时，倾向于低估尾部事件（如罕见疾病、症状）的共现结构，导致对稀有亚群的数据生成保真度不足，影响隐私保护研究的可靠性。

Method: 提出AdaPCLA框架：(1) 数据分布感知的训练策略，通过模拟退火训练内化数据知识参数；(2) 零样本分布控制，支持训练-free的跨临床群体自适应生成；(3) 基于标签经验NTK的理论分析，刻画罕见码logit更新及退火速度与NTK条件数对先验信号保留的影响。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，TailPairSeen指标较HALO分别提升114.2%和65.1%；零样本跨群体适应中F1较GPT式生成提升3.5%。

Conclusion: AdaPCLA在尾部事件合理性、下游任务效用和零样本控制方面均取得一致提升，验证了分布感知训练策略对罕见子群体生成质量的有效性。

Abstract: Generative modeling of longitudinal Electronic Health Records is increasingly important for privacy-preserving research, yet standard autoregressive models tend to underrepresent the co-occurrence structure of tail events (i.e., diseases, symptoms), reducing the fidelity and faithfulness of generated data for rare subpopulations. To this end, we propose AdaPCLA framework, which enables generative models to adaptively fit and generate EHR data through a data distribution-aware training strategy; this is achieved by internalizing data knowledge parameters by simulated annealing training. It also supports training-free adaptation to a diverse clinical population for generation through zero-shot distribution control. Moreover, our theoretical analysis characterizes rare-code logit updates through the label-wise empirical NTK and derives a prior-internalization bound for how annealing speed and NTK conditioning affect retained prior signals. Experiments on real-world data show that AdaPCLA achieves consistent gains in tail plausibility, downstream utility, and zero-shot control; in particular, it improves TailPairSeen over HALO by 114.2% on MIMIC-III and 65.1% on MIMIC-IV, outperforms GPT-style generation by 3.5% F1 for zero-shot cross-population adaptation.

</details>


### [84] [Extractable Memorization From First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12649)
*A. Feder Cooper,Marika Swanberg,Jamie Hayes,Lea Duesterwald,Christopher De Sa,Daniel E. Ho,Mark A. Lemley,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过匹配比较（matched comparison）来严格验证LLM中的可提取记忆（extractable memorization），解决了现有研究中普遍存在的有效性偏差问题，并基于此重新定义了“可提取记忆”的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM可提取记忆的研究存在两种相反的效度问题：部分研究高估了记忆提取（将短序列的可预测性误认为记忆），另一些研究则低估了其可靠性（模型可能复现未训练过的真实文本）。两种观点都忽视了有效提取主张的核心——模型必须以足够高的概率生成训练序列以表明记忆。

Method: 形式化匹配比较框架：（1）**共形检验（Conformal Test）**：在训练序列和非训练序列来自总体时，校准阈值到指定假阳性率（FPR）；（2）**普查（Census）**：当对象为单个文档（如书籍）时，与匹配的非训练文档进行对照校准。

Result: 在Wikipedia上，OLMo 2 32B复现非训练10-token后缀的频率约为训练后缀的24%，表明大量所谓的“记忆”实为假阳性。对于Llama 3.1 70B在书籍上的实验，校准后的阈值低至1e-27，可通过极低概率支持记忆主张，但实际采样预算几乎不可能提取。

Conclusion: 匹配比较能够实现严格、校准的记忆主张，揭示了先前实验设置的效度问题。基于此，论文将“可提取记忆”重新定义为：同时满足有效的记忆主张和在现实采样预算内近乎确定的生成能力。

Abstract: Recent work on extractable memorization in LLMs suffers from two contrasting validity problems. Some studies overstate extraction, e.g., relying on sequences too short to distinguish memorization from predictability. Others imply that extraction is unreliable evidence of memorization, since models can also reproduce real-world text they weren't explicitly trained on. In different ways, both overlook what makes a valid extraction claim: the model must generate a training sequence with high enough probability to indicate memorization. To determine what's high enough, one has to perform a matched comparison: measuring the generation probabilities of both the training sequences of interest and comparable non-training sequences. Because non-training sequences cannot have been memorized, their probabilities provide a baseline for predictability; a training sequence exceeding this baseline provides evidence of memorization. We formalize matched comparisons in two ways: (1) a conformal test that calibrates a threshold to a chosen FPR when training and non-training sequences are sampled from populations, and (2) a census that calibrates against a matched non-training document when the object is a single document (e.g., a book). We show that matched comparisons enable rigorous, calibrated memorization claims, and reveal where prior setups have validity issues. For instance, on Wikipedia OLMo 2 32B reproduces non-training 10-token suffixes roughly 24% as often as training ones: that share of the training generation rate reflects false positives, not memorization. For Llama 3.1 70B on books, the thresholds we calibrate are as low as 1e-27, supporting memorization claims for sequences that no feasible sampling budget would extract. Based on these results, we refine "extractable memorization" to require a valid memorization claim and near-certain generation within a realistic budget.

</details>


### [85] [Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning: A Path Toward Eliminating LLM Hallucination in Empirical Inference via Tool-Attested Kernel Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12650)
*Junyu Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EG-VAR，一种基于Lean 4的架构，通过将Lean内核作为已验证声明的唯一铸造者，确保每一个验证输出在结构上都源自被证明的工具调用和内核检查的有效推理链，未通过的输出则诚实声明"Abstain"并提供可重放的审计轨迹。在TableBench数值推理子集上达到120/120，反事实压力测试中保持100%来源忠实，残留语义形式化错误仅1.7%-3.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭工具访问不足以使LLM经验推理可治理：接受输出不必源于被证明的证据，接受演绎不必经得起形式审查。现有框架无法保证LLM输出的推理链在形式上可验证和可审计。

Method: 提出EG-VAR架构，基于Lean 4实现：(1) Lean内核作为已验证声明的唯一铸造者，通过工具证明公理和声明源提升实现；(2) 每个验证输出在结构上源于被证明的工具调用（定理3.1）和内核检查的有效推理链（定理3.2）；(3) 未通过输出输出"Abstain"并提供可重放审计轨迹；(4) LLM作为部署时形式化器。

Result: 在TableBench数值推理子集(n=120)上达到120/120，基线为95%；反事实压力测试（5个领域×2个模型）中EG-VAR保持100%来源忠实，而同工具基线降至80-90%，无工具基线降至50-80%；语义形式化错误率Sonnet为3.3%，Opus为1.7%。

Conclusion: EG-VAR可作为高风险经验声明技术治理接口：形式化旁路使目标命题、源范围、证据边界、证明义务和弃权条件可审计，消除无支持的验证输出。未来可将类型化旁路嵌入数据集、API、公共记录和AI生成文档中，将形式化负担摊销为可复用基础设施。

Abstract: Tool access alone does not make LLM empirical reasoning governable: accepted outputs need not descend from attested evidence, and accepted deductions need not hold up under formal scrutiny. We present EG-VAR (Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning), a Lean 4-based tool-calling architecture in which the Lean kernel is the sole minter of Verified claims via tool-attestation axioms and declared source lifts. Every verified output structurally descends from an attested tool call (Thm. 3.1) and a kernel-checked chain of valid inference (Thm. 3.2); residual outputs are honest Abstain with a replayable audit trail. On a subcollection of TableBench numerical reasoning (n=120), EG-VAR attains 120/120 versus a 95% same-tool baseline; on counterfactual stress tests (5 domains x 2 models), EG-VAR stays 100% source-faithful while same-tool drops to 80-90% (no-tool 50-80%). With the LLM as deployment-time formalizer, residual semantic-formalization error is 3.3% on Sonnet and 1.7% on Opus. We position EG-VAR as a technical-governance interface for high-stakes empirical claims: a formal sidecar makes the target proposition, source scope, evidence boundary, proof obligation, and abstention condition auditable, eliminating unsupported Verified outputs today while turning formalization errors, lift and source-authority disputes, ambiguities, and abstentions into explicit audit targets. Over time, typed sidecars in datasets, APIs, public records, and AI-generated documents can amortize this formalization burden into reusable infrastructure.

</details>


### [86] [Learning-based Probabilistic Load Forecasting with Post-hoc and In-model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12730)
*Sarah Al-Shareeda,Gulcihan Ozdemir,Heung Seok Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能建筑负荷预测中，当模型部署时输入特征不完整需重建的情况下，不确定性应置于何处的问题。比较了模块化事后残差分位数方案与集成式模型内分位数学习方案，发现不确定性放置方式依赖网络骨架，集成式分位数学习在TFT模型上表现最佳，而重建输入会导致分位数评分增加106%，说明模型不会自动吸收重建带来的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能建筑负荷预测模型通常在密集、多变量、高频数据上离线训练，但部署时可能仅提供小时级、特征有限的输入。缺失特征需要重建，其误差可能通过模型传播，若输入不确定性未被反映，预测区间可能校准不良，影响需求响应调度。因此需要研究推理输入重建后不确定性应置于何处。

Method: 开发了一个统一的前一天概率预测框架，对齐时间分辨率、重建不可用输入并导出因果特征。比较了模块化事后残差分位数方案与集成式模型内分位数学习方案，使用三种深度学习骨干（循环、混合循环、基于注意力的TFT），在相同输入、预测范围、预处理规则和训练预算下进行对比。

Result: 不确定性放置方式依赖网络骨架。集成式分位数学习在TFT上最可靠，在标记测试窗口上MAPE为2.2-3.6%，RMSE为28-83W，区间宽度比模块化区间窄约5倍。重建输入使分位数评分增加106%，而区间宽度几乎不变。Diebold-Mariano检验支持TFT排名和循环骨干的混合表现。

Conclusion: 重建输入增加了分位数评分但区间宽度几乎不变，表明模型不会自动吸收重建带来的不确定性。事后残差分位数在推理依赖重建输入时存在局限性。鲁棒性检验支持上述排名。

Abstract: Smart-building load forecasters are often trained offline on dense, multivariate, high-frequency data, but deployment may provide only hourly, feature-limited inputs. Missing features must then be reconstructed, and their errors can propagate through the model. If this input uncertainty is not reflected, prediction intervals may become miscalibrated, affecting demand-response scheduling. Our work examines where uncertainty should be placed once inference inputs are reconstructed. We develop a unified one-day-ahead probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns temporal resolution, reconstructs the unavailable inputs, and derives causal features, and we compare a modular post-hoc residual-quantile scheme with an integrated in-model quantile-learning scheme. The comparison uses three mid-scale Deep Learning (DL) backbones: recurrent, hybrid recurrent, and attention-based Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) models, under identical inputs, forecasting horizon, preprocessing rules, and training budgets. Results show that uncertainty placement is backbone-dependent. Integrated quantile learning is most reliable with the TFT, yielding 2.2-3.6% MAPE and 28-83W RMSE on the labeled test window, while producing intervals about 5x narrower than the modular intervals at the closest-to-nominal coverage level. Diebold-Mariano tests support the TFT ranking and the mixed behavior of the recurrent backbones. A reconstruction-sensitivity test shows that reconstructed inputs increase the Quantile Score (QS) by 106% while interval width remains nearly unchanged, indicating that the model does not automatically absorb reconstruction-induced uncertainty. Robustness checks against non-DL baselines and seasonal hold-out weeks support this ranking. Our results expose the limits of post-hoc residual quantiles when inference depends on reconstructed inputs.

</details>


### [87] [What Makes a Representational Prior Work? Feature Families, Label-Free Invariances, and Critical Windows in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12735)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过188组新实验，系统研究了对比先验在grokking现象中加速泛化的四个关键轴：内容（特征族对齐决定先验是否允许泛化）、监督（无标签不变性先验可靠加速）、时机（仅需早期短暂施加）和设置（跨多种任务和架构复现）。核心发现：特征族对齐是泛化的必要条件，无标签的交换不变性先验即可可靠加速，且只需在训练早期（4%预算）施加就能实现大部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明grokking延迟本质上是形成任务结构化表示所需的时间，且可通过对比先验注入。本文旨在系统刻画什么因素使得这样的先验有效，从四个维度（内容、监督、时机、设置）进行大规模实验验证。

Method: 在188组新实验中，沿四个轴系统操控：1) 内容轴：比较相同特征族（幅度带）的连贯可学习先验与随机划分先验的效果；2) 监督轴：对比全无标签的交换不变性先验（仅用(a,b)~(b,a)作为正对）与标签监督先验；3) 时机轴：将先验限制在不同时间窗口（早期2000轮 vs. 连续 vs. 后期窗口）；4) 设置轴：在模乘任务、不同深度和归一化变体上验证，并扫描权重范数钳位。

Result: (1) 内容：来自错误特征族的先验（幅度带）与随机划分无显著差异（p=0.43），证实特征族对齐是关键；(2) 监督：无标签交换不变性先验在15/15次运行中泛化，中位数2.7倍加速，比标签监督先验更可靠（p=0.038）；与权重范数钳位组合达到最强效果（中位数17倍，5/5）；(3) 时机：仅早期2000轮（4%预算）即可10/10泛化且2.7倍加速，优于连续施加（8/10，1.25倍）和后期窗口（2.1倍）；(4) 设置：跨模乘、深度和归一化变体复现，先验使权重范数延迟律指数降低约17倍。

Conclusion: 特征族对齐是决定先验能否允许泛化的关键；无标签的交换不变性内容足以实现加速；短暂的早期窗口即可捕获几乎所有收益。对于在记忆前就已泛化的任务，则不存在延迟需要控制。

Abstract: Companion work showed the grokking delay is causally the time to form task-structured representations, injectable via a contrastive prior. Here we characterize what makes such a prior work, across four axes, in 188 new runs. Content: a coherent, learnable prior built from the wrong feature family (magnitude bands) blocks generalization like a random partition (1/15 vs 0/20 grok; $p=0.43$ between them), confirming the companion's prediction that priors act at the level of the circuit's features. Supervision: a fully label-free invariance prior -- positives are commuted pairs $(a,b)\sim(b,a)$ only -- generalizes in 15/15 runs at a median $2.7\times$ speedup, more reliably than the label-supervised prior itself ($p=0.038$), and combined with a weight-norm clamp yields the strongest method we test (median $17\times$, 5/5) -- strongest meaning reliably fast: plain cross-entropy with a clamp matches this speed only at the exact critical norm, while the prior keeps it fast across the entire clamp range. Timing: the prior is only needed early -- applied solely during the first 2000 epochs (4% of budget) it generalizes 10/10 at $2.7\times$, beating continuous application (8/10, $1.25\times$) and a duration-matched later window ($2.1\times$). Setting: the dissociation replicates on modular multiplication and across depths and normalization variants, and a clamp sweep quantifies the companion's central claim: structure injection flattens the weight-norm delay-law exponent about 17-fold (plain cross-entropy slows $31\times$ per +10 norm units, a lower bound as higher cells are censored, versus $1.22\times$ with the prior). Honest boundary: tasks that generalize before memorizing have no delay to control. Feature-family alignment decides whether a prior permits generalization; invariance content suffices for acceleration without labels; a brief early window captures nearly all of the benefit.

</details>


### [88] [Constraint-Aware Aggregation for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Microgrid Energy Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12763)
*Usman Haider,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦强化学习中的约束感知聚合方法，提出基于惩罚的简单聚合规则 $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$，在分布式能源协调场景中相比FedAvg显著降低违规并提升奖励，无需双重优化或修改本地训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦强化学习聚合方法（如FedAvg）未考虑系统级约束，导致全局行为不安全。现有方法需要双重优化或修改本地训练，复杂度高。需要一种轻量级、兼容标准通信协议的约束感知聚合方法。

Method: 提出惩罚基聚合规则 $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$，在服务器端更新时同时考虑局部性能（奖励$R_i$）和估计的约束违规（$V_i$），通过惩罚项实现奖励与安全性的权衡，无需修改本地训练或进行对偶优化。

Result: 在DairyGridEnv基准（多农场电池储能协调，共享电网容量约束）及芬兰真实负载数据和德国FIELD数据集上，基于惩罚的聚合相比FedAvg显著减少违规并提升奖励。组合奖励-违规方案（通过$λ$调节）虽可权衡但稳定性较差。

Conclusion: 轻量级的聚合策略能够在保持标准通信协议的前提下，显著提升联邦强化学习的经验安全性，其中简单的惩罚基聚合方法在奖励与安全性之间提供了最可靠的折衷。

Abstract: Federated Reinforcement Learning (FedRL) enables coordination of distributed energy resources without sharing raw local data, but standard aggregation methods such as FedAvg do not account for system-level constraints, often leading to unsafe global behavior. In this work, we study constraint-aware aggregation for federated reinforcement learning in distributed energy coordination. We propose aggregation rules that incorporate both local performance and estimated constraint violation into the server-side update. Among these, a simple penalty-based rule, $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$, consistently provides the most reliable trade-off between reward and safety, without requiring dual optimization or modifications to local training. \textcolor{black}{We evaluate our approach on DairyGridEnv, a benchmark modeling multiple farms coordinating battery storage under stochastic demand and a shared grid capacity constraint, and further assess robustness using real load-driven demand profiles from Finland and the German FIELD dataset. Across multiple seeds, penalty-based aggregation substantially reduces violations while improving reward relative to FedAvg in both synthetic and real load-driven settings.} A combined reward-violation scheme exposes a tunable trade-off via $λ$, but is less stable. These results demonstrate that lightweight aggregation strategies can substantially improve empirical safety in federated reinforcement learning while preserving standard communication protocols.

</details>


### [89] [Learning Mechanistic Reasoning for Chemical Reactions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12771)
*Xingyu Dang,Haocheng Tang,Junmei Wang,Yanjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模反应机理推理数据集，并在FukuyamaBench基准上微调Qwen3-30B-A3B模型，实现了8.3%的精确路径匹配，超越专用FlowER模型（5.1%），证明机理感知训练能显著增强语言模型的化学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学大语言模型主要关注粗粒度的命名反应进行产物预测和逆向合成，导致物理不一致性和幻觉；而专用小规模机理推理模型泛化能力受限。

Method: 构建了大规模反应机理推理数据集，并创建了基于Fukuyama《高等有机反应机理》的FukuyamaBench基准；微调Qwen3-30B-A3B模型进行层次化机理推理。

Result: 在FukuyamaBench Set A上，微调后的Qwen3-30B-A3B达到8.3%的精确路径匹配，超越FlowER模型（5.1%）。

Conclusion: 机理感知训练能显著增强语言模型的化学推理能力，为将推理型LLM应用于化学反应机理学习提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Reaction mechanisms consist of the step-by-step sequences of elementary reactions that explain chemical transformations. Learning the mechanism logic is therefore essential for enhancing the fundamental chemical intelligence of large language models (LLMs). The stepwise deduction of reaction mechanism aligns naturally with the reasoning paradigms of reasoning LLMs. However, current chemical LLMs primarily emphasize coarse-grained name reactions for product prediction and retrosynthesis, often leading to physical inconsistencies and hallucinations. In contrast, specialized small-scale generative models for mechanism inference typically suffer from restricted generalization capacity across diverse chemical spaces. To overcome these limitations, we built a novel, large-scale reasoning dataset of reaction mechanisms. Furthermore, we established the FukuyamaBench, a difficult benchmark derived from Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism book, to rigorously evaluate model performance on hierarchical mechanism reasoning. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves 8.3% exact pathway match on FukuyamaBench Set~A, surpassing the specialized FlowER model (5.1%), demonstrating that mechanism-aware training substantially enhances chemical reasoning in language models.

</details>


### [90] [AVQ-Attention: Adaptive Vector-Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12789)
*Winfried van den dool,Patrick Forré,Amir Habibian,Yuki M. Asano,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应向量量化注意力（AVQ Attention），通过自适应分配码本容量来优化注意力机制，在保持O(MN)复杂度的同时提升精度效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制O(N²)计算复杂度过高，现有VQ注意力虽将其降至O(MN)但存在码本容量分配不均的问题：高注意力区域可能被粗粒度量化，而低注意力区域浪费表征能力。

Method: 从少量码字开始，在前向传播中识别最重要的码字，用预学习的子码字进行细化，实现重要区域细粒度量化、非重要区域粗粒度量化；通过自定义Triton内核在Flash Attention的平铺计算范式内完成完整自适应细化过程。

Result: 在保持O(MN)复杂度的前提下，相比固定码本VQ注意力取得了更好的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: AVQ注意力通过自适应码本容量分配，在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下有效解决了VQ注意力中码本资源分配不均的问题。

Abstract: The $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ complexity of attention over $N$ tokens remains a computational bottleneck in transformer models. Vector-Quantized (VQ) attention reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ by representing keys with $M$ codewords, but applies uniform codebook capacity regardless of where attention mass concentrates: high-attention regions of key space may be coarsely approximated while low-attention regions waste representational capacity. We propose Adaptive Vector-Quantized (AVQ) Attention, which adaptively allocates codebook capacity based on attention importance. Starting from a small set of codewords, our method identifies the most important codes during the forward pass and refines them with pre-learned child codewords, achieving fine-grained quantization where it matters most while maintaining coarse quantization elsewhere. We develop an implementation using custom Triton kernels that enables the full adaptive refinement process, including importance scoring, child codeword insertion, and parent contribution replacement, to be carried out within the tiled computation paradigm of Flash Attention with minimal overhead. Our approach maintains $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ complexity while achieving improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to fixed-codebook VQ-attention.

</details>


### [91] [Accelerating Masked Diffusion Large Language Models: A Survey of Efficient Inference Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12829)
*Daehoon Gwak,Minhyung Lee,Junwoo Park,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的延迟分解框架，用于分析扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的推理效率，并从算法、架构/系统和推理时扩展三个维度系统梳理了加速技术，最后给出了可复现基准测试的指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型虽具有并行生成的理论优势，但仅靠并行生成无法保证实际加速，需要专门的推理机制（如扩散感知缓存和重用）才能提升效率。然而，端到端延迟来源于算法、架构和系统层面因素的复杂权衡，现有基准测试常混淆这些因素，导致严格比较困难，因此需要一个统一的延迟分解框架来解耦这些因素。

Method: 本文首先提出一个统一的延迟分解框架，将dLLMs的推理延迟拆解为不同因素；然后基于该框架，从三个维度（算法创新、架构与系统优化、推理时扩展）对现有加速技术进行分类和分析；最后提供可复现基准测试的指南。

Result: 通过延迟分解框架，本文揭示了影响dLLMs实际推理速度的关键因素及其权衡关系，系统梳理了三类加速技术，并指出了并行生成潜力尚未完全发挥的开放挑战。

Conclusion: dLLMs的推理效率是实现实际部署的前提，但当前加速技术仍需进一步整合与优化；未来的研究方向包括更精细的延迟分解、可复现的基准测试标准，以及更充分地释放并行生成在推理阶段的优势。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a theoretical advantage in parallel generation over standard autoregressive models. However, parallel generation alone does not guarantee practical speedups. Realizing this efficiency requires specialized inference mechanisms, such as diffusion-aware caching and reuse. Consequently, as inference efficiency becomes a prerequisite for practical deployment, recent research has actively explored acceleration techniques across algorithms, architectures, and systems. However, rigorous comparisons remain difficult, as end-to-end latency stems from intricate trade-offs between algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors that are often conflated in existing benchmarks. In this survey, we introduce a unified latency decomposition framework for dLLMs to disentangle these factors and analyze their impact on inference speed in real deployments. Guided by this framework, we categorize acceleration techniques along three axes covering algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling. Finally, we provide guidelines for reproducible benchmarking and highlight open challenges for realizing the full potential of parallel generation.

</details>


### [92] [Verifier-Based Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Reasoning Models for Thermal Energy Storage Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12856)
*Takumi Shioda,Kohei Terashima,Tatsuo Nagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）微调开源推理模型，使其在建筑热能存储调度中接近动态规划最优解（排放61.2 vs 60.8 kg-CO2），并证明推理时间推理能力对任务的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑需响应电网条件转移冷却负荷，热储能（TES）可实现此目标，但现有方法（MPC、强化学习）难以跨建筑规模化部署，亟需一种可扩展的调度方案。

Method: 将精确离线动态规划（DP）动作值转换为密集奖励信号，通过强化微调（RFT）训练开源推理模型作为上层调度器，从文本状态和预测输出每小时热泵设定点。

Result: RFT将排放从70.5降至61.2 kg-CO₂，接近DP最优值60.8 kg-CO₂；GPT-5未经任务训练即接近DP和MPC；GPT-4o（非推理LLM）排放高于无储能基线，表明推理时推理能力至关重要。

Conclusion: 基于DP的可验证奖励为适配开源推理模型到建筑储能调度提供实用途径，主要稳定可观察的规划模式（候选比较、前瞻、可行性检查），并展示了一定的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Buildings are expected to shift cooling loads in response to grid conditions. Thermal energy storage (TES) enables this shift, but scheduling it well requires planning hours ahead under storage constraints. Model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning are difficult to scale across buildings. This study instead adapts an open-weight reasoning model through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We convert exact offline dynamic-programming (DP) action values into dense rewards for every candidate action. Using only 30 training prompts, reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) trains the model as an upper-level scheduler that outputs hourly heat-pump setpoints from text-based states and forecasts. Evaluation uses a deliberately simple office-building TES benchmark where exact DP is tractable and the optimum is known. RFT reduces the open-weight model's emissions from 70.5 to 61.2 kg-CO2, close to the DP optimum of 60.8 kg-CO2. GPT-5 nearly matches DP and MPC without task-specific training, while GPT-4o, a non-reasoning LLM, produces higher emissions than the no-storage baseline, so inference-time reasoning appears important. Trace analysis shows that RFT mainly stabilizes observable planning patterns (candidate comparison, look-ahead, and feasibility checking) rather than creating a new strategy. Robustness and generalization tests clarify what transfers: the reinforced planning patterns persist under forecast errors and an unseen TES condition and carry over to a battery task, but its different structure limits the gains. DP-based verifiable rewards offer a practical way to adapt open-weight reasoning models to building storage scheduling. These results motivate higher-fidelity tests of whole-building control and scalable verifiers for city-scale energy management.

</details>


### [93] [Energy-Based Physics-Informed Form Finding for Clustered Tensegrity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12888)
*Jing Qin,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于能量的学习框架，用于张拉整体结构找形与物理属性预测，通过将总势能最小化与本构关系融入训练目标，同时预测平衡节点构型及内力/力密度，在棱柱与着陆器系统中展现出良好的可扩展性与预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体找形与物理属性预测是结构力学中的逆问题，因几何与力的强非线性耦合、结构稳定性保障及边界/对称约束施加而极具挑战，传统方法对噪声和离群值鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出基于能量的学习框架，将总势能最小化和本构关系作为物理损失项整合到训练目标中，实现平衡节点构型与成员力/力密度等物理量的同步预测。

Result: 在张拉整体棱柱与着陆器系统上的数值实验表明，该方法具有良好的可扩展性，能准确预测结构属性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将能量基物理损失直接融入学习过程，提升了物理一致性、鲁棒性和数据效率，为张拉整体结构的找形与属性预测提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Tensegrity form-finding and physical property prediction are fundamental inverse problems in structural mechanics, which aim to determine equilibrium configurations and internal force distributions. These problems are challenging due to strong nonlinearity arising from the coupling between geometry and forces, the need to ensure structural stability, and the enforcement of constraints such as boundary conditions and symmetry. Moreover, traditional methods often lack robustness to noise and outliers. This paper proposes an energy-based learning framework for clustered tensegrity form finding and physical property prediction. The proposed approach incorporates total potential energy minimization and constitutive relations into the training objective, enabling the simultaneous prediction of equilibrium nodal configurations and associated physical quantities, including member forces and force densities. By incorporating energy-based physical losses directly into the learning process, the framework improves physical consistency, robustness, and data efficiency. Numerical experiments on tensegrity structures, including prism and lander systems, show the great potential of the proposed approach and demonstrate its capability for scalable form finding and accurate prediction of structural properties.

</details>


### [94] [Contrastive-Collapsed Loss for Flexible and Geometrically Optimal Embeddings and Faster Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12916)
*Blanca Cano-Camarero,Ángela Fernández-Pascual,José R. Dorronsoro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo损失函数，通过促进类内坍塌与类间对比来学习归一化且结构良好的表征，在OpenML-CC18表格数据集上达到与SOTA方法相当的竞争力，并实现更紧致的类聚类和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有损失函数（如点回归、交叉熵）在学习归一化且结构良好的表征时存在初始化距离最优配置较远、梯度信息不足、类级坍塌激励不够等问题，需要一种能兼顾类内紧凑与类间分离的新损失函数。

Method: 提出CoCo损失函数，同时鼓励类内坍塌（intra-class collapse）与类间对比（inter-class contrast），保留神经网络逼近几何最优嵌入（大角度类间分离）的灵活性，并从理论上分析了其与点回归、交叉熵的关系。

Result: 在OpenML-CC18基准的多样化表格数据集上，CoCo与核SVM、随机森林、点回归和基于交叉熵的神经网络等SOTA方法性能相当；理论分析与实验均证明CoCo能促进更紧致的类聚类和更快收敛。

Conclusion: CoCo损失是一种有效的目标函数，能够在保持竞争力的预测性能的同时学习判别性表征。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CoCo, a loss function aimed at learning normalized and well-structured representations. The proposed loss encourages intra-class collapse and inter-class contrast while preserving sufficient flexibility for neural networks to approximate geometrically optimal embeddings with large angular separation between classes. We provide a theoretical analysis positioning CoCo with respect to related objectives such as dot regression and cross-entropy, showing that the new proposed loss benefits from closer initialization to the optimal configuration, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise representation collapse. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular datasets from the OpenML-CC18 benchmark show that CoCo achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, including kernel SVM, Random Forest, dot regression, and cross-entropy-based neural networks. In addition, both theoretical arguments and empirical analyses demonstrate that the proposal promotes tighter class clustering and faster convergence. These results highlight CoCo loss as an effective objective for learning discriminative representations while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [95] [Efficient Sequential Calibration with $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12928)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效随机预测器，结合SPR-Calibration过程与外部Blackwell风格修正层，将在线二值序列校准问题的期望校准误差突破至O(T^{2/3-ε})，优于经典的T^{2/3}界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线序列校准问题中，经典校准误差下界为T^{2/3}，近期Dagan等人突破了这一屏障。本文旨在进一步改进，实现更优的校准误差上界。

Method: 将SPR-Calibration过程（用于控制代理条件均值序列的校准误差）与外部Blackwell风格修正层（用于校正代理序列与真实结果之间的残差）相结合。总校准误差分解为代理校准误差和残差，前者由SPR-Calibration保证界定，后者通过二次势函数论证及SPR-Calibration预测器的稀疏性控制。

Result: 实现了期望校准误差O(T^{2/3-ε})，其中ε>0为某常数。

Conclusion: 通过双层架构设计，成功进一步降低了在线校准误差的上界，展示了SPR-Calibration过程与修正层结合的有效性。

Abstract: We study the online binary sequential calibration problem. A recent breakthrough by \citet{dagan2024breaking} overcomes the classical \(T^{2/3}\) barrier for calibration error. Building on this result, we present an efficient randomized forecaster that achieves an expected calibration error \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\) for some constant \(\varepsilon>0\).
  Our forecaster combines the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure \citep{dagan2024breaking} with an outer Blackwell-style correction layer. The \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure controls calibration with respect to a surrogate sequence of conditional-mean estimates, while the correction layer controls the additional error incurred when these surrogates are used to approximate the true outcomes. The analysis decomposes the total calibration error into the surrogate calibration error and the residual discrepancy between the surrogate sequence and the true outcomes. The former is bounded by the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} guarantee in \citet{dagan2024breaking}, and the latter is controlled using a quadratic potential argument together with the sparsity of the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} forecaster.

</details>


### [96] [The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13006)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个根本性问题：现有基于频谱的可预测性指标实际衡量的是序列的二阶统计特性，而非"添加上下文（如长回溯窗口、检索插件或预训练模型）是否有助于预测"这一实践者真正关心的问题。作者通过相位随机化证明了任何频谱指标在超越二阶的预测价值上存在不可能性结果，并提出了覆盖率赤字（coverage deficit）这一无标签诊断指标，该指标通过对比线性预测与类比预测的增益来量化超越频谱的结构性信息。在七个基准上验证了核心预测：窗口键控检索的价值在代理对之间完全崩塌（ECL中位数从+33%降至-35%，p<10^-40），而所有频谱指标保持不变；基础模型的价值分裂为可存活的二阶部分和崩塌的超越线性边际；长线性窗口的价值则完全存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频谱可预测性指数被实践者错误地解读为"是否应该添加上下文（长回溯、检索插件、预训练模型）"的答案，但这两者本质上是不同的问题。上下文的价值是工作点的属性而非序列本身的属性，而任何基于功率谱构建的指数在相位随机化下保持不变，但超越二阶的价值（如检索和基础模型提供的）却会被相位随机化破坏。作者旨在揭示这一混淆并提供一个正确的诊断方法。

Method: 1. 提出不可能性结果：任何基于功率谱的指数在相位随机化下不变，而相位随机化后的序列渐进高斯化，破坏了超越二阶的结构信息。2. 构建代理对（surrogate pairs）：固定频谱和边缘分布，但改变相位结构。3. 提出覆盖率赤字（coverage deficit）：一种无标签、配置级别的诊断指标，其主项通过测量类比预测相对于线性预测的增益来量化超越频谱的结构信息。4. 在七个基准上进行验证，比较频谱指数与结构项在不同预测方法（窗口键控检索、基础模型、长线性窗口）下的表现。

Result: 1. 窗口键控检索的价值在代理对之间完全崩塌（ECL中位数从+33%降至-35%，p<10^-40），而所有频谱指数保持不变。2. 基础模型的价值分裂为可存活的二阶部分和崩塌的超越线性边际。3. 长线性窗口的价值完全存活。4. 留一数据集实验：结构项在频谱指数无法预测的地方成功预测了超越频谱价值的符号，而二阶机制则反向成立。

Conclusion: 频谱可预测性指数和上下文价值是两回事，不能混为一谈。本文的贡献不在于提出新的预测器，而在于做出这一关键区分、提供受控比较方法以及给出部署决策的诊断工具。覆盖率赤字作为无标签诊断指标，能有效分离二阶和超越二阶的预测增益，为实践者判断是否应引入检索或预训练模型提供理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE.

</details>


### [97] [TerraZero: Procedural Driving Simulation for Zero-Demonstration Self-Play at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13028)
*Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Weixin Li,Wei-Jer Chang,Zachary Lee,Tim Wang,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraZero 是一个程序化驾驶模拟器与自博弈训练框架，通过零拷贝流水线实现每秒130万智能体步数的高速仿真，仅利用真实地图几何数据生成无限多样化场景，从零开始通过强化学习训练策略，无需人类演示或推理时的后备规划器，在 InterPlan 长尾基准上首次超越所有学习方法，在常规驾驶基准上取得最佳碰撞安全指标，并同时支持驾驶策略与仿真智能体双重角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶模拟器在速度、真实性和多样性三者之间存在矛盾：日志数据驱动的模拟器难以覆盖安全关键的长尾场景，而纯程序化模拟器往往缺乏真实地图结构和异构智能体等保真度要素，且现有学习方法在长尾基准上未超越传统规划器。

Method: 采用可配置 C 引擎在 CPU 上运行仿真、GPU 上执行策略推理，通过零拷贝路径消除数据传输开销。仅使用日志数据提取真实地图几何信息，每个地图注入随机化规则驱动的道路使用者、信号控制器以及随机化的动力学、奖励和智能体尺寸，实现无限场景生成。采用纯强化学习的自博弈训练，跨 GPU 分布式执行，无人类演示也无推理时后备规划器。

Result: 单服务器级 GPU 上达到 130 万智能体步数/秒；在 InterPlan 长尾基准上首次有完全学习的策略超越所有更大规模的学习规划器；在常规驾驶 val14 基准上碰撞率和碰撞时间指标最优；在 Waymo Open Sim Agents  realism 上优于其他无演示方法，与最强的参考锚定自博弈方法相当。策略零样本泛化至不同城市和数据集，包括无监督学习左侧行驶。

Conclusion: TerraZero 证明纯强化学习结合程序化多样化场景生成足以训练出在长尾安全场景和常规驾驶上均领先的驾驶策略，且同一框架可同时服务于自动驾驶策略和仿真智能体生成，为自动驾驶提供高效、安全且泛化能力强的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Training robust autonomous driving agents requires a simulator that is fast enough for reinforcement learning at scale, realistic enough to ground behavior in real-world map structure, and diverse enough to cover the safety-critical long tail that logged data rarely contains. We present TerraZero, a procedural driving simulator and self-play training stack. A configurable C engine runs simulation on the CPU and policy inference on the GPU over a zero-copy path, sustaining 1.3M agent-steps per second on a single server-grade GPU, far faster than existing object-level simulators, while keeping fidelity lighter single-agent systems omit: heterogeneous agents, multiple dynamics models, and full traffic-rule enforcement. TerraZero treats logged data only as a source of real-world map geometry, populating each map with randomized rule-based road users and signal controllers and randomizing agent dynamics, rewards, and sizes per episode, so a map yields an unbounded set of scenarios. Every reported policy trains from scratch by reinforcement learning alone on a compute-efficient self-play recipe across GPUs, with zero human demonstrations and no fallback planner at inference. Policies generalize zero-shot across cities and datasets, including emergent left-hand-traffic driving without explicit supervision. As an ego policy, TerraZero is the first fully learned policy to top the InterPlan long-tail benchmark, ahead of larger learned planners; on routine-driving val14 it ranks among the best approaches and is the safest, posting the best collision and time-to-collision scores. On Waymo Open Sim Agents realism the same recipe outperforms other demonstration-free methods and is competitive with the strongest reference-anchored self-play method. One stack serves both roles: driving policies across dynamics for cars and trucks, and sim agents that jointly control vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists.

</details>


### [98] [The Seriality Gap in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13031)
*Jorge Diaz Chao,Konpat Preechakul,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在硬球多体碰撞的因果链预测任务中，标准双向视频扩散模型的性能随因果链增长而下降，而单球控制实验表明这是由依赖事件结构而非视频长度导致。研究发现存在"序列性差距"：视频扩散模型的去噪循环无法提供可扩展的串行计算，与任务的串行推理需求不匹配，并证明了去噪步骤不会增加超越骨干网络的串行计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视频扩散模型在需要串行推理的物理模拟任务（如多球碰撞因果链预测）中的性能限制，探究模型在处理长因果链任务时的退化原因。

Method: 设计多球硬球碰撞实验（长因果链）与单球控制实验（长度匹配但无球-球交互），进行干预研究比较不同模型架构（自回归/逐块生成、增加网络深度）的效果，并进行理论证明。

Result: 标准双向视频扩散模型在长因果链任务上性能显著退化，而单球控制实验退化基本消失；增加串行计算的方法可不成比例地提升性能。

Conclusion: 视频扩散模型存在序列性差距（seriality gap），其去噪循环无法提供任务所需的可扩展串行计算，这一结构性障碍限制了其在串行推理和模拟任务上的应用。

Abstract: When one ball strikes another, then another, video models should predict the consequences of each bounce. In controlled experiments on multi-ball hard-sphere dynamics, we find that the performance of standard bidirectional video diffusion degrades as the causal chain lengthens, even when provided more denoising steps. In a length-matched single-ball control, where ball-ball interactions are absent, the degradation largely disappears, isolating dependent-event structure rather than video length as the cause. Across intervention studies, methods that increase effective serial computation improve performance disproportionately, including autoregressive/blockwise generation and architectural depth. We identify this pattern as the seriality gap: a mismatch between tasks requiring growing serial computation and video diffusion models whose denoising loop does not provide scalable serial compute. We then prove that, for deterministic video prediction, denoising steps do not add serial computation beyond the backbone, indicating a structural obstacle for video diffusion on serial reasoning and simulation tasks.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

### [99] [A Quantum Computing Approach to Track Reconstruction in Strip-Type Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12821)
*Seungyeob Jwa,Hyunyong Kim,Jangho Kim,Minseok Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用量子退火技术进行条状气体探测器中的粒子径迹重建，将两个子问题（单光子径迹的击中点选择和跨层的簇三联体选择）建模为QUBO问题，并在DAMSA探测器模拟数据上验证了该方法，结果表明QPU重构在位置和角度分辨率上接近卡尔曼滤波方法，能够有效提取有效三联体并构建径迹候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在条状气体探测器中，幽灵击中点和多重击中组合使得模式识别转化为组合优化问题，传统方法面临计算复杂度挑战，因此需要探索量子退火等新型计算范式来提升径迹重建效率。

Method: 将两个重建子问题（单径迹击中点选择和跨层簇三联体选择）建模为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，使用量子退火处理单元（QPU）求解，并通过基于图连通性的关联规则连接提取到的有效簇三联体。

Result: 在DAMSA探测器模拟事件上，单径迹击中选择任务的QPU重构在位置和角度分辨率上接近基于卡尔曼滤波的方法；在同时关联任务中，成功从QPU样本中提取有效簇三联体并构建径迹候选，验证了QUBO公式能够复现局部重建决策。

Conclusion: 该研究为在更复杂的追踪环境中结合量子计算和经典计算的径迹重建方法提供了实践基础，证明了量子退火在粒子物理重建领域的可行性。

Abstract: This study investigates the use of quantum annealing for particle track reconstruction in strip-type gaseous detectors. In such detectors, ghost hits and multiple hit combinations can turn pattern recognition into a combinatorial optimization problem. We formulate two reconstruction subproblems as quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problems. The first subproblem selects detector hits associated with a single photon track inside a localized candidate region. The second subproblem selects cluster triplets from different detector layers so that multiple track candidates can be handled within a single quantum processing unit(QPU) submission. The proposed formulations are tested using simulated DAMSA detector events. For the single track hit selection task, the QPU based reconstruction gives position and angular resolutions close to those obtained with a Kalman based reconstruction. In the simultaneous association task, valid cluster triplets are first extracted from the QPU samples and then connected using an association rule based on graph connectivity to construct track candidates. The DAMSA event topology studied here has low pileup and is dominated by the two photon signal from axion-like particle(ALP) decay. In this setting, the results show that the QUBO formulations can reproduce local reconstruction decisions. This provides a practical basis for further studies of reconstruction methods that combine quantum and classical computing in more complex tracking environments.

</details>


### [100] [A Non-Commutative Voronovskaya Theorem for Quantum Neural Network Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11907)
*Rômulo Damasclin Chaves dos Santos,Delvonei Alves de Andrade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子神经网络算子逼近任意量子通道的完全渐近展开，包含整数幂项、分数阶项和纯量子对易子项，并建立了严格的误差界。基于此展开，获得了量子中心极限定理、最优插值测地线构造方法，以及分数阶光滑性对量子神经网络加速的制约机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Voronovskaya定理是逼近论的核心结果，但其非对易（量子）版本尚缺失。量子神经网络的逼近行为缺乏严格的渐近理论支撑，特别是逼近误差中量子特有的贡献尚未被刻画。

Method: (1) 推导量子神经网络算子的完全渐近展开，将误差分解为三项：整数幂项（普通Fréchet导数）、分数幂项（Marchaud分数阶导数刻画Hölder光滑性）、纯量子对易子项。(2) 给出余项的界：‖R_{m,n}(Φ,·)‖_⋄ ≤ C_{m,γ,d} ‖Φ‖_{C^{m,γ}} n^{-(m+γ)} (log n)^{3m/2}。(3) 通过经典模拟数值实验验证收敛速率和对数修正。

Result: (1) 建立了量子神经网络算子的完全渐近展开，揭示了逼近误差的三类组成部分；(2) 获得了量子中心极限定理；(3) 提出了基于Kubo-Ando均值的量子通道间最优插值测地线构造方法；(4) 阐明了分数阶光滑性对量子神经网络加速的理论限制；(5) 数值实验验证了理论收敛速率的最优性以及对数修正的不可避免性。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了经典逼近论、分数阶微积分和量子机器学习之间的严格桥梁，为有限维量子神经网络的设计与分析提供了理论洞见和实用工具。

Abstract: We prove a complete asymptotic expansion for quantum neural network operators when they approximate arbitrary quantum channels. This is the non-commutative analogue of the classical Voronovskaya theorem. The expansion reveals that the approximation error splits into three fundamentally different parts: integer powers of \(1/n\) involving ordinary Fréchet derivatives; fractional powers governed by Marchaud fractional derivatives, which capture the Hölder smoothness of the channel; and purely quantum commutator terms that have no classical counterpart. The remainder is bounded sharply by an explicit constant:
  \[
  \norm{R_{m,n}(Φ,\bullet)}_\diamond \le C_{m,γ,d} \|Φ\|_{\cC^{m,γ}} \, n^{-(m+γ)} (\log n)^{3m/2}.
  \]
  We present a numerical test for a classical analogue that confirms the predicted convergence rate and the logarithmic correction, directly validating the asymptotic theory. Based on this expansion, we obtain three major advances: a quantum central limit theorem for the fluctuations of quantum neural network operators, a method to construct optimal interpolation geodesics between quantum channels via Kubo-Ando means, and a systematic understanding of how fractional smoothness limits the acceleration of quantum neural network approximations. The numerical test further demonstrates that the theoretical rates are sharp and that logarithmic enhancements are unavoidable. Altogether, our work builds a rigorous bridge between classical approximation theory, fractional calculus, and quantum machine learning, offering both theoretical insight and practical tools for designing and analyzing quantum neural networks in finite dimensions.

</details>


### [101] [What It Is Like To Be A Quantum Computer: A Closed Quantum Global Workspace -- Conscious Access and Integration as Correlation Dynamics in Hilbert Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11909)
*Libby Heaney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于艺术驱动的好奇心，将全局神经元工作空间（GNW）理论翻译到封闭量子系统中，构建了一个量子Hopfield模型，证明在纯酉动力学下，意识访问和记忆表现为全局纠缠态中的相关结构和相位关系，而非经典表征或记录，并提出了一种以理论模型本身作为艺术-科学协作媒介的新模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受思辨性艺术探究的驱动，探索量子形式的体验如何与表征和具身性相关联，试图澄清工作空间理论中哪些方面依赖于经典假设。

Method: 以经典GNW为架构参考，构建量子Hopfield哈密顿量模型，通过约化得到可处理的玩具模型和GHZ型纠缠，在封闭量子系统框架下分析全局可用性和记忆的实现机制。

Result: 全局信息可用性通过保持相干性的关联分布实现；记忆是持续的内部关联结构；点火成为全局纠缠的转变而非向稳定吸引子收敛；相对相位可通过联合内部操作全局访问，但所有局部子系统均无法获取。

Conclusion: 封闭量子系统方法揭示了非表征性机制，访问和记忆完全是关系性的而非副本或记录；同时提出一种新的艺术-科学协作模式，即通过理论模型本身而非插图或数据进行思辨性艺术探究。

Abstract: Motivated by speculative artistic inquiry into quantum-only forms of experience in relation to representation and embodiment, this paper develops a translation of the Global Neuronal Workspace (GNW) theory for closed quantum systems. The work treats classical GNW as an architectural reference and asks how conscious access and memory change under unitary dynamics.
  We reference a quantum Hopfield-style Hamiltonian that mirrors key organisational features of GNW, such as a distributed workspace and tunable large-scale integration. Within this framework, global availability of information is realised through coherence-preserving correlation distributions and memory is a persistent internal correlation structure. Likewise, ignition becomes a transition in global entanglement as opposed to convergence towards a stable attractor. Reductions of the Hopfield-style Hamiltonian lead to a tractable toy model and GHZ-type entanglement, explicitly showing how relative phase is globally accessible through joint internal operations yet absent from all local subsystems.
  This closed quantum system approach clarifies which aspects of workspace theories are contingent on classical assumptions. Our model highlights a non-representational regime in which access and memory are entirely relational rather than copies or records.
  Beyond its scientific contribution, the paper proposes a mode of art-science collaboration where speculative artistic inquiry is pursued through theoretical models rather than illustration or data.

</details>


### [102] [Comment on Temperature change can solve the Deutsch-Jozsa problem: An exploration of thermodynamic query complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11911)
*Ridha Horchani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出Phys. Rev. A 113, 012420 (2026)中的读出分析存在错误：CNOT扇出操作无法产生独立副本，因此一次热查询无法提供多个统计有效的探针样本；同时Pinsker不等式给出的样本下界推导有误，约为116个样本的结论需要修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正现有文献中关于一次热查询后多次探针样本读出的资源核算错误，澄清量子热查询协议中的统计独立性条件。

Method: 通过分析CNOT扇出操作后寄存器的关联性，证明其只能产生完美相关的寄存器而非独立副本；通过重新审视Pinsker不等式的方向性，指出原始论文中样本下界推导的数学错误。

Result: 证明对角后查询探针的CNOT扇出仅广播单量子比特边际，但产生完美相关的寄存器，迹距离和相对熵不增加；重复测量辅助比特仅提供一次伯努利观测；Pinsker不等式给出的样本下界方向与原始论文相反。

Conclusion: 原始论文中一次查询多次统计有用样本的读出主张以及约116个样本的必要性结论需要修正，热反冲机制仍可能将决策编码在探针温度中，但资源核算需重新进行。

Abstract: We show that the readout analysis in Phys. Rev. A 113, 012420 (2026) does not establish the claimed resource accounting of one thermal query followed by many statistically useful probe samples. A CNOT fanout of a diagonal post-query probe broadcasts its one-qubit marginal but produces perfectly correlated registers rather than independent copies. Consequently, neither the trace distance nor the relative entropy between the balanced and constant hypotheses increases, and repeated measurements of the ancillas provide only one Bernoulli observation. Independent samples instead require repeated preparation and heat exchange, which are repeated queries under the definition adopted in the original paper. We also show that the sample lower bound stated there does not follow from Pinsker's inequality: the inequality gives the opposite ordering for the reciprocal relative entropy. The thermal-kickback mechanism may still encode the decision in the probe temperature, but the one-query readout claim and the quoted necessity of approximately 116 samples require correction.

</details>


### [103] [Comment on Statistical mechanics from quantum envariance and exchange symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11912)
*Ridha Horchani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出Ojha等人提出的通过追溯粒子排列的环境记录得到熵kB ln N!从而解释Gibbs因子的方案存在根本性错误：约化密度矩阵并非部分迹，熵等式仅在正交条件下成立，且该条件不限制玻色/费米对称性；其Saha平衡推导中态因子化导致零纠缠，提出的阶乘在稀薄极限下不趋于1，且双重计数了不可区分性。作者给出了修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Ojha等人关于Gibbs佯谬和Saha平衡中粒子不可区分性处理的错误，纠正其对统计力学基本概念的误用。

Method: 通过分析Ojha等人论文中的关键方程（如Eq.(32)的约化密度矩阵、Eq.(52)的Saha态），指出其数学错误和物理不一致性，并用两粒子示例显式展示矛盾。给出正确的正则系综和逸度公式表述。

Result: Ojha等人的核心结论不成立：熵kB ln N!仅当系统分支态相互正交时才成立，但该条件既不一般地满足，也不自动对应玻色子或费米子对称性；Saha推导中态因子化导致零纠缠，其阶乘因子在热力学极限下发散且双重计数。

Conclusion: Ojha等人提出的通过环境记录和部分迹来导出Gibbs因子的方案是错误的，其修正需要重新审视不可区分粒子的正确统计处理。

Abstract: Ojha, Sardana, and Ghosh [Phys. Rev. A 113, 042221 (2026)] propose that tracing environmental records of particle permutations produces an entropy kB ln N!, thereby explaining the Gibbs factor, and use the same construction to multiply the Saha equilibrium relation by 1/(Ne!Np!). We show that these conclusions do not follow. The reduced density matrix printed in their Eq. (32) is not a partial trace, and even under the natural corrected interpretation the entropy equals kB ln N! only when the system branch states are mutually orthogonal. That condition is not generally satisfied and, when imposed on labeled permutation branches, does not by itself restrict the particle state to one bosonic or fermionic symmetry sector. A two-particle calculation makes the contradiction explicit. Their Gibbs-paradox calculation also starts from a distinguishable-particle entropy while calling it the Sackur-Tetrode entropy. In the Saha section, the state in Eq. (52), as written, factorizes and has zero system-environment entanglement. The proposed factorial does not approach unity in the claimed dilute-gas limit, does not yield a finite nonzero intensive thermodynamic limit, and counts indistinguishability twice. We give the corrected canonical and fugacity-based formulations and identify which standard results of the paper remain unaffected.

</details>


### [104] [Double Covariance Model for Entangled Quantum States: Gaussian Reduction to Second Order Covariances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11968)
*Andrei Khrennikov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出双协方差模型(DCM)，通过将量子纠缠态的密度算子解释为四阶经典统计量（随机协方差算子的协方差），利用高斯过程的二阶矩决定高阶矩的性质，将纠缠态生成问题约化为二阶经典统计，并阐明了并发性（concurrence）的经典概率意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典概率形式与量子概率形式之间的互联是物理学基础问题中极具挑战性的方面，特别是经典概率性生成纠缠态这一问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出双协方差模型(DCM)，将复合量子系统的密度算子对应为四阶经典统计量；利用高斯过程的二阶矩决定高阶矩的性质，将四阶统计约化为二阶统计，从而简化并泛化DCM构造。

Result: DCM框架成功将纠缠态的经典概率生成问题约化为二阶经典统计，并利用（圆）高斯过程使构造数学上严谨且具泛化性；同时阐明了并发性这一纠缠度量在经典概率框架下的含义。

Conclusion: 双协方差模型为经典概率与量子概率的互联提供了新的数学框架，高斯约化使其可操作化，并为理解纠缠度量提供了经典统计视角。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to the problem of interconnecting the probabilistic formalisms of classical and quantum physics, focusing on its most challenging aspect: the classical probabilistic generation of entangled states. We show that the statistics encoded in the density operators of composite quantum systems correspond to fourth-order classical statistics. Specifically, to generate a density operator, one must consider the covariance of a random covariance operator. We term this framework the Double Covariance Model (DCM). This double covariance possesses a non-trivial internal structure that arises from the interplay between two distinct time scales, combining temporal and statistical covariances. In this article, we exploit a well-known property of Gaussian processes: the second-order moment determines the moments of higher orders, specifically the fourth-order moment. This Gaussian reduction simplifies the DCM by reducing it to second-order statistics. Utilizing (circular) Gaussian processes simplifies and generalizes the DCM construction for entangled states, rendering it mathematically rigorous. Furthermore, it clarifies the classical probabilistic meaning of concurrence, a foundational quantitative measure of entanglement.

</details>


### [105] [On the Experimental Determination of Nonlocal Characteristics of Two-Qubit Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11973)
*M. Karthick Selvan,S. Balakrishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于两量子比特门的纠缠能力和门典型性的解析表达式，构建了用于测量这些非局域特性的量子电路。这些电路由su(4) Cartan子代数元素生成，代表多种量子处理器的原生相互作用，每个电路将原生门应用两次并包含至少一个CNOT门。测量纠缠功率需要六（或三）个电路，而测量门典型性需要三个电路，后者需要更多非局域资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验确定两量子比特门的非局域特性，即纠缠能力和门典型性，以表征量子处理器的原生门性能。

Method: 基于su(4) Cartan子代数推导的纠缠能力和门典型性表达式，构建两量子比特量子电路。每个电路将原生门应用两次，包含至少一个CNOT门。通过六（或三）个电路测量纠缠功率，通过三个电路测量门典型性。

Result: 构建了一套测量两量子比特门非局域特性的量子电路方案：纠缠功率测量需6个电路（可缩减至3个，需增加CNOT门数量），门典型性测量需3个电路且需要更多非局域资源。

Conclusion: 所构建的电路可有效测定量子处理器中原生两量子比特门的非局域特性，为量子门表征提供了实验方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we discuss the experimental determination of the nonlocal characteristics of two-qubit gates. Based on the recently derived expressions for the entangling power and gate typicality of two-qubit gates, we construct two-qubit quantum circuits to measure the entangling power and gate typicality of the two-qubit gates, which are generated by the elements of the su(4) Cartan subalgebra. These elements describe the native interactions in many quantum processors. Hence, these circuits can be used to determine the nonlocal characteristics of native gates of many quantum processors. In each circuit, the native gate is applied twice. In addition, each circuit consists of at least one CNOT gate. A set of six two-qubit circuits is constructed to measure the entangling power. The number of circuits is further reduced to three by increasing the nonlocal resources (the number of CNOT gates). To measure the gate typicality, a set of three two-qubit circuits is constructed. Measurement of gate typicality requires more nonlocal resources than the measurement of entangling power.

</details>


### [106] [VQCSim: When Does Compile-Once Statevector Simulation Beat Generic Quantum Frameworks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11985)
*Anton Firc,Martin Perešíni,Vojtěch Mrázek,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Zbyněk Lička,Nouhaila Innan,Walid El Maouaki,Alberto Marchisio,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: VQCSim 是一种编译一次、PyTorch 原生的态矢量执行路径，带有原生自动求导。在 MQT Bench 测试中，VQCSim 编译了所有静态电路并提供 87.7% 的端到端语义验证。在五 GPU 评估中，推理中位加速 4.49 倍，训练中位加速 26.78 倍（有限差分下仍保持 3.31 倍优势）。消融实验显示原生自动求导是主要加速源（27.6 倍）。发布了开源后端选择器 vqcsim-oracle，top-1 一致率达 91.1%-97.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典机器学习工作流中，框架调度与编排开销常主导运行时，而现有模拟器虽能加速执行，但未回答"编译一次的特化策略何时是静态变分电路的正确选择"这一问题。

Method: 设计并实现 VQCSim——一种编译一次（compile-once）、PyTorch 原生的态矢量执行路径，集成原生自动求导（native autograd）。在 MQT Bench 上进行系统化研究，通过消融实验分析编译缓存、批量化向量化和原生自动求导各自的贡献，并推导硬件感知的映射图（regime map）。

Result: VQCSim 编译所有测试静态电路，87.7% 端到端语义验证通过；五 GPU 上推理中位加速 4.49x、训练中位加速 26.78x（有限差分下 3.31x）；原生自动求导贡献 27.6x 加速；发布 vqcsim-oracle 后端选择器，top-1 一致率 91.1%-97.7%。

Conclusion: 编译一次的特化策略在静态变分电路场景下能显著加速量子-经典混合工作流，但以更高的 GPU 内存为代价。vqcsim-oracle 可实现自动后端选择，助力 QML 设计循环。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical machine learning workflows repeatedly evaluate many small parametrized circuits during training and model exploration. In this regime, framework dispatch and orchestration overhead often dominate runtime. Prior simulators accelerate execution but leave open the question of when compile-once specialization is the right choice for static variational circuits. We answer this question with VQCSim, a compile-once, PyTorch-native statevector execution path with native autograd. In a systematic MQT Bench study, VQCSim compiles all tested static circuits and provides 87.7% end-to-end semantic validation. Across a five-GPU evaluation set, VQCSim delivers pooled median speedups of 4.49x for native inference and 26.78x for native training, while retaining a 3.31x advantage under matched finite-difference training. Ablation identifies native autograd as the dominant source of acceleration (27.6x), with compile-once caching and batch vectorization contributing additional gains. The speedup trades higher GPU memory (VQCSim is memory-limited at the high end) for lower runtime. We derive a hardware-aware regime map and release vqcsim-oracle, an open-source backend selector with 91.1%-97.7% top-1 agreement (including cross-GPU transfers), enabling automatic simulator selection in QML design loops.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum-classical neurons upgraded with optical spin qubits for advanced computing and networking architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11992)
*Osama M. Nayfeh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验测量了集成光学自旋量子比特的光电量子-经典神经元，研究了神经元尖峰序列驱动光致发光振荡并调制量子自旋跃迁的机制，建立了考虑神经元与光学自旋相互作用的量子模型，为实现具有量子光学能力的硬件神经元奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件神经元是实现大规模神经网络的关键技术，现有神经元缺乏量子信息处理能力。为突破这一限制，需要开发集成光学自旋量子比特的神经元，使其具备混合量子-经典运算能力，从而解决复杂的量子力学问题。

Method: 采用集成光学自旋量子比特（基于碳化硅中带负电的硅空位及铌/铌氧化物膜中的钕稀土离子）的光电量子-经典神经元，通过脉冲激光激发生理尖峰序列，测量光致发光振荡并调制量子自旋跃迁，建立考虑神经元与光学自旋相互作用的理论模型。

Result: 实验验证了定制化的神经尖峰序列能够驱动光致发光振荡并调制量子自旋跃迁；理论计算表明量子计算操作可在量子-经典神经元与自旋量子比特系统的状态空间上扩展，且量子态强度可通过动态记忆变化调节。

Conclusion: 这些结果对实现具有量子光学能力的硬件神经元至关重要，为受生物光子启发的量子神经形态计算提供了实验和理论基础。

Abstract: Hardware neurons incorporating built-in memory components are critical technologies for implementing large scale neural networks that exhibit adaptive-itinerant behavior and produce biological-inspired spiking patterns that match neuroscience known operations (1). Moreover, neurons capable of full quantum information processing through their qubit states and quantum trajectory provides capability for hybrid quantum-classical operations through the level of coherence/entanglement and non-Markovianity (2,3).
