These attributes further contribute to solving complex quantum mechanical problems by providing a rich and diverse group for expressing quantum neural states. Upgrading these hardware neurons now with optical spin qubits excitable with pulsed laser excitation in the near infrared and designed from negatively charged silicon vacancies in semiconductor silicon carbide (4) and Neodymium rare earth ions in the superconducting Niobium/Niobium oxide film (5) that form the memory provides access to the unique spin Hamiltonian that considers electronic and nuclear interactions. In this manuscript, we experimentally measure opto-electronic quantum-classical neurons integrated with a optical spin qubit and examine how tailored neuronal spiking sequences drive the photoluminescence oscillations and modulate the quantum spin transitions. We derive a quantum model for the system that considers the interaction between the neuron and optical qubit spin and perform calculations to project how quantum computing operations are expandable across the state space in the quantum-classical neuron and spin qubit system. For example, the resulting impact on the neuron quantum states where the strength is adjustable due to dynamical memory changes. These results are critical for realizing hardware neurons with quantum optical capabilities and inspired by the existence in biology of biophotons.

</details>


### [108] [Remote Entanglement of Solid-State Spin Qubits Integrated in Broadband Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12002)
*Christopher Waas,Timo Dolné,Hans K. C. Beukers,Alexander M. Stramma,Nina Codreanu,Noé Mathieu,Ronald Hanson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在独立芯片波导中演示了金刚石锡空位自旋量子比特之间的远程纠缠，利用宽带波导架构实现了高器件良率和无需腔模光谱匹配的纠缠生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔增强系统中量子发射器的制造良率和光谱匹配是集成量子网络扩展的主要障碍，亟需一种无需精确腔模匹配的替代方案。

Method: 采用独立片上波导嵌入锡空位色心，结合固有高效光子发射与宽带波导架构，实现光子介导的纠缠生成及实时前馈控制。

Result: 实现了相干光学与自旋控制、高可见度双光子干涉，并通过实时前馈产生一致纠缠态，无需依赖宣布图案。

Conclusion: 波导集成的锡空位中心成为可扩展量子网络节点的有前景平台。

Abstract: Solid-state spin-photon interfaces promise to scale quantum networks through on-chip photonic integration and multiplexed entanglement generation. To date, remote entanglement between integrated emitters has been realized only in cavity-enhanced systems, where fabrication yield and spectral matching remain major obstacles. Here we demonstrate heralded remote entanglement between diamond tin-vacancy spin qubits embedded in separate on-chip waveguides. Combining intrinsically efficient photon emission with a broadband waveguide architecture provides high device yield and obviates the need for spectral matching to cavity modes. We realize coherent optical and spin control and achieve high-visibility two-photon interference. By combining photon-mediated entanglement generation with real-time feedforward, we produce a consistent entangled state independent of the heralding pattern. These results establish waveguide-integrated tin-vacancy centers as a compelling platform for scalable quantum network nodes.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum incompatibility of Born probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12032)
*Esteban Castro-Ruiz,Nathan Cohen,Luis C. Barbado,Časlav Brukner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证了当使用非理想量子参考系进行测量时，即便在无限次实验下，测量结果的相对频率仍存在不可约的量子不确定性，并证明了关于相对频率的贝尔定理，挑战了量子态本身具有确定性的传统假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子理论假设量子态（所有可能测量的概率目录）是良好定义的，但这一假设隐含地依赖于理想化、无限资源的参考系。作者旨在揭示当参考系具有有限资源时，概率本身会变得不确定，从而推动量子态概念向有限资源场景的扩展。

Method: 通过理论分析证明当测量相对于非理想量子参考系进行时，即使在大量实验下相对频率仍保持不确定；证明关于相对频率的贝尔型定理以表明这种不确定性的量子力学本质不可约；提出基于脉冲零差检测的量子光学实验方案来实现这类关系性测量。

Result: 证明了在非理想量子参考系下，概率本身会变得不确定，且这种不确定性在量子力学意义上是不可约的（通过贝尔定理证明）；提出了可实验实现的量子光学方案。

Conclusion: 研究结果激励将量子态概念扩展到受有限资源约束的场景，预期在量子理论与广义相对论的交界处（时空有界区域中资源和信息本质有限）具有特别重要的意义。

Abstract: Quantum theory challenges the view that individual measurement outcomes are predefined and independent of the measurement context. Yet the quantum state itself -- the catalogue of probabilities for all possible measurements -- is usually assumed to be well defined. We argue that this assumption tacitly relies on measurements being performed relative to ideal, infinitely-resourceful reference frames. We show that, when measurements are made relative to non-ideal quantum reference frames, the probabilities themselves become indefinite: even in the limit of arbitrarily large number of runs, the relative frequencies may remain uncertain. The uncertainty is irreducible in a quantum-mechanical sense, as we show by proving a Bell-type theorem for relative frequencies. We further propose a quantum-optical implementation of these relational measurements based on pulsed homodyne detection. Our findings motivate an extension of the notion of the quantum state to regimes constrained by finite resources. We expect them to be especially relevant at the interface between quantum theory and general relativity, where the resources and information available in a bounded region of spacetime are fundamentally limited.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum magic and non-commutativity as computational resources in quantum reservoir computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12035)
*Wei Xia,Shuaifan Cao,Xingze Qiu,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子储备池计算（QRC）在Pauli-Liouville空间中的统一理论框架，揭示了量子魔法是有效计算的必要条件而非回声状态属性（ESP），证明了qubit-resetting架构存在非线性与记忆容量之间的固有权衡，并提出了哈密顿编码架构作为突破性方案，其中ESP由Liouvillian谱隙天然保证，且离散时间更新映射呈现超越函数阶的非线性依赖，非对易性生成核驱动输入历史的高度非分离处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量子储备池计算在实验上取得快速进展，但对其可扩展量子增强性能所需的结构条件缺乏严格理解。

Method: 在Pauli-Liouville空间中建立理论框架，统一分析回声状态属性、非线性表达能力和量子资源。分别分析了qubit-resetting架构和Hamiltonian编码架构的数学性质。

Result: 量子魔法是有效计算的必要条件；qubit-resetting架构中所有非线性处理仅源于经典编码映射，存在非线性与记忆容量的固有权衡；Hamiltonian编码架构中ESP由Liouvillian谱隙保证，离散更新映射呈现超越函数阶非线性，非对易性驱动输入历史的高度非分离处理。

Conclusion: 建立了QRC架构的严格理论层次结构，为实验实现真正的量子优势提供了指导性设计原则。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing (QRC) provides a hardware-efficient paradigm for temporal information processing on near-term quantum devices. Despite rapid experimental progress, a rigorous understanding of the structural conditions required for its scalable quantum-enhanced performance remains lacking. Here, we develop a theoretical framework in Pauli-Liouville space that provides a unified analytical treatment of the echo state property (ESP), nonlinear expressive power, and quantum resources. We first analyze the widely used qubit-resetting scheme and establish that quantum magic generated by reservoir dynamics is a necessary condition for effective computation, a requirement more fundamental than ESP. However, we prove that this architecture faces inherent expressivity limitations: all nonlinear processing originates exclusively from the classical encoding map, imposing an unavoidable trade-off between nonlinearity and memory capacity. To circumvent this structural bottleneck, we rigorously analyze Hamiltonian encoding, in which temporal inputs are embedded directly into the continuous dynamics generator. We show that the ESP is natively guaranteed by the Liouvillian spectral gap, decoupling it from quantum magic. Crucially, for any non-trivial drive Hamiltonian, the discrete-time update map exhibits a transcendental, infinite-order nonlinear dependence on the instantaneous input. Moreover, the intrinsic non-commutativity of the open-system generators governs the temporal coupling of these nonlinearities, producing highly non-separable processing of the input history. Our results establish a rigorous theoretical hierarchy of QRC architectures and provide prescriptive design principles for experiments targeting genuine quantum advantages in temporal processing.

</details>


### [111] [Dissipative phase transitions and chaos in two-photon driven quantum optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12044)
*Giovanni Bragadin,Filippo Ferrari,Lorenzo Fioroni,and Matteo Seclì,Vincenzo Macrì,Vincenzo Savona,Alberto Mercurio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两光子驱动光力学系统中的非平衡临界性与混沌现象，发现该系统同时支持一阶和二阶耗散相变、对称性破缺、亚稳定性、极限环、混沌吸引子以及量子混沌轨迹，证明其是一个集耗散临界性、对称性破缺和量子混沌于同一实验平台的理想系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索两光子驱动光力学系统中非平衡临界性与混沌行为的物理机制，揭示其在单一实验体系中同时实现耗散相变、对称性破缺和量子混沌的可行性。

Method: 结合半经典稳定性分析、精确Liouvillian谱和随机量子轨迹方法，对两光子驱动光力学系统进行理论和数值研究。

Result: 负失谐下存在二阶相变导致空腔宇称对称性自发破缺；正失谐下对称性破缺与一阶相变共存，表现为亚稳态和额外的对称Liouvillian模；强泵浦下平均场动力学失去稳定不动点，出现极限环和具有正Lyapunov指数的混沌吸引子；量子轨迹呈现类混沌运动、增强的稳态熵和在多个熵Liouvillian模上的离域化。

Conclusion: 两光子驱动光力学系统为在同一实验可及平台上同时研究耗散临界性、对称性破缺和量子混沌提供了理想平台。

Abstract: We investigate nonequilibrium criticality and chaos in a two-photon-driven optomechanical system. The parametric drive preserves a discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry of the optical field, while radiation-pressure coupling transfers the resulting nonlinear dynamics to a mechanical oscillator. Combining semiclassical stability analysis, exact Liouvillian spectra, and stochastic quantum trajectories, we show that this driven-dissipative optomechanical model supports both first- and second-order dissipative phase transitions. At negative detuning a second-order transition yields spontaneous breaking of the cavity-parity symmetry in the thermodynamic limit. At positive detuning the same symmetry breaking coexists with a first-order transition, signaled by metastability and by an additional symmetric Liouvillian mode. At stronger pump power the mean-field dynamics loses all stable fixed points and develops limit cycles and chaotic attractors with positive Lyapunov exponent. Quantum trajectories in this regime display chaotic-like motion, enhanced steady-state entropy, and delocalization over many entropic Liouvillian modes. These results establish two-photon-driven optomechanics as a platform where dissipative criticality, symmetry breaking, and quantum signatures of chaos emerge within the same experimentally accessible setting.

</details>


### [112] [Observation of gravity-like signatures in holographic codes on a quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12047)
*Debopriyo Biswas,Gong Cheng,Krishnanand Karthikeyan,Diana Muñoz-Valencia,Vincent P. Su,Hrant Gharibyan,Daiwei Zhu,Grant Salton,Evgeny Epifanovsky,Martin Roetteler,Christopher Monroe,John Preskill,Norbert M. Linke,ChunJun Cao,Crystal Noel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 trapped-ion 量子计算机上实现了 AdS/CFT 对偶的 HaPPY 码张量网络模型，首次实验验证了 Faulkner-Lewkowycz-Maldacena 公式，并通过引入非稳定化（magic）观察到涌现引力的熵前兆，最后构造并测量了具有虫洞熵行为的量子码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学与广义相对论的统一是理论物理的重大未解问题，AdS/CFT 对偶提供了一个全息二元性的理论框架，但缺乏实验验证手段。

Method: 在 trapped-ion 量子计算机上实现 HaPPY 码（一种具有双曲纠缠模式的张量网络量子纠错码），实验测量 FLM 公式，引入非稳定化资源（magic）并观测熵行为，构造类虫洞量子码。

Result: 首次实验验证了 Faulkner-Lewkowycz-Maldacena 公式；引入 magic 后观测到涌现引力的熵前兆；构建并测量了具有高量子虫洞特征的码结构。

Conclusion: 量子计算机可作为涌现时空建模的实验平台，AdS/CFT 对偶的 HaPPY 码能在当前量子硬件上实现并检验全息对偶的关键预测。

Abstract: The unification of quantum mechanics and general relativity remains one of the major open problems of theoretical physics. The Anti-de Sitter/Conformal Field Theory (AdS/CFT) correspondence provides a valuable theoretical framework for this effort via a holographic duality between a theory of quantum gravity in asymptotically AdS spacetime and a conformal quantum field theory on the lower-dimensional boundary. Here, we implement a toy model of this duality called the HaPPY code, a quantum error-correcting code in the form of a tensor network with hyperbolic entanglement patterns, on a trapped-ion quantum computer. We present the first experimental confirmation of the Faulkner-Lewkowycz-Maldacena formula in this model - a key test of the holographic correspondence. We then enrich it with non-stabilizerness, or magic, and observe entropic precursors expected of emergent gravity. Finally, we present and measure a code construction whose entropic behavior is reminiscent of a highly quantum wormhole. Our experiments illustrate how quantum computers can serve as testbeds for modeling the emergence of spacetime.

</details>


### [113] [Entangling Power and Symmetries in the Quantum Rabi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12053)
*Ian Low,Jens Koch,Sahel Ashhab*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子Rabi模型族中对称性对纠缠功率的影响。通过时间平均纠缠功率作为诊断工具，发现非对称量子Rabi模型（AQRM）在整数偏置点（隐藏对称性位置）出现峰值，而Jaynes-Cummings模型（显式U(1)对称性）则呈现弱谷值。这证明纠缠功率可以有效揭示光-物质系统中隐藏和显式对称性的存在与性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否利用时间平均纠缠功率作为算符级诊断工具，揭示量子Rabi模型族中隐藏对称性和显式对称性的存在及其性质。

Method: 选取Rabi模型族中两个特例：具有显式U(1)对称性的Jaynes-Cummings模型和仅在整数偏置ε/ω∈ℤ时存在参数依赖隐藏对称性的非对称量子Rabi模型。使用两种有限输入系综作为比较：Fock空间截断后的Haar平均和固定平均占据数的相干态平均。

Result: 在AQRM的整数偏置点（隐藏对称性所在处），两个诊断指标均呈现峰值；而在JC点（显式U(1)对称性），则呈现弱谷值。这表明时间平均纠缠功率对两种对称性均有响应，且响应的正负指示了对称性重整谱展开的方式。

Conclusion: 时间平均纠缠功率可以作为有效的算符诊断工具，揭示光-物质系统中隐藏和显式对称性的存在及其性质。

Abstract: The quantum Rabi model is a standard effective Hamiltonian in studies of light-matter interaction, capturing the simplest nontrivial setting in which a qubit couples to a single harmonic oscillator. Within the broader Rabi family, we focus on two special cases: the Jaynes-Cummings (JC) model, which carries an explicit $U(1)$ symmetry, and the asymmetric quantum Rabi model (AQRM), which possesses a parameter-dependent "hidden'' symmetry that appears only at integer bias, $\varepsilon/ω\in\mathbb{Z}$, and is not manifest in the Hamiltonian. We use the time-averaged entangling power as an operator-level diagnostic of these symmetry structures. Since the oscillator Hilbert space is infinite-dimensional, we compare two finite input ensembles: a Haar average after Fock-space truncation and a coherent-state average at fixed mean occupation $\bar{n}$. Both diagnostics show peaks at the integer-bias points of the AQRM, where the hidden symmetry resides. In contrast, the manifest $U(1)$ symmetry at the JC point instead gives a weak dip. Thus, the time-averaged entangling power responds to both the hidden symmetry and the $U(1)$ symmetry in the Rabi family, with the sign of the response indicating how the symmetry reorganizes the spectral expansion. These results demonstrate that the entangling power can serve as an operator diagnostic to reveal the presence and properties of hidden and manifest symmetries in light-matter systems.

</details>


### [114] [HarmQ: Harmonic Backdoor Attacks Against Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12055)
*Junrui Zhang,Zemin Chen,Chunsheng Xin,Hongyi Wu,Rui Ning*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出HarmQ，一种针对量子神经网络的量子原生后门攻击方法，通过利用参数化量子电路的谐波模式偏差，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上实现超过99%的攻击成功率，同时保持90%以上的干净准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子神经网络继承了经典神经网络的安全漏洞，特别是后门攻击的脆弱性。现有针对经典系统的攻击方法由于量子特有的限制（如低采样率破坏触发器、参数化量子电路的谱学习偏差限制可学习模式）而无法有效攻击QNNs。

Method: HarmQ利用参数化量子电路的傅里叶分解偏差，通过谐波触发模式实现攻击。具体采用块均匀结构的正弦扰动作用于粗粒度网格，确保触发器在低采样过程中存活，同时与PQCs偏好低频分量的特性对齐。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上，HarmQ的攻击成功率超过99%，干净准确率保持在90%以上，显著优于BadNets(2.77% ASR)、Watermark(7.96% ASR)、Q-FGSM(44.32% ASR)和QUAP(3.40% ASR)等方法。

Conclusion: HarmQ通过谐波触发模式成功实现了对QNNs的高效后门攻击，t-SNE可视化证实谐波触发生成了明显分离的量子态簇，标志着QNNs面临根本性的安全威胁。

Abstract: Quantum Neural Networks (QNNs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for quantum machine learning in the Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era, leveraging quantum phenomena such as superposition and entanglement to process information in exponentially large Hilbert spaces. However, QNNs inherit critical security vulnerabilities from classical neural networks, particularly susceptibility to backdoor attacks. Existing attack methods designed for classical systems fail against QNNs due to quantum-specific constraints: aggressive downsampling required by limited qubit resources destroys conventional triggers, while the spectral learning bias of parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs) restricts learnable patterns. To tackle this, we present HarmQ, a quantum-native backdoor attack that exploits PQCs' inherent Fourier decomposition bias through harmonic trigger patterns. Our approach employs sinusoidal perturbations on coarse grids with block-uniform structure, ensuring survival through downsampling while aligning with PQCs' preference for low-frequency components. This enables effective backdoor injection under realistic black-box conditions where attackers access only training data. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST demonstrate that HarmQ achieves attack success rates exceeding 99% while maintaining over 90% clean accuracy, significantly outperforming existing methods including BadNets (2.77% ASR), Watermark (7.96% ASR), Q-FGSM (44.32% ASR) and QUAP (3.40% ASR). Parametric t-SNE visualizations of quantum state representations confirm that harmonic triggers create distinctly separated clusters, evidencing HarmQ as a fundamental security threat for QNNs.

</details>


### [115] [Molecular Docking with Quantum Circuit Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12060)
*Gleydson Fernandes de Jesus,Bruno Oziel Fernandez,Marcelo A. Moret*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子电路演化（QCE）的门控量子进化方法用于分子对接，该方法在更少的步骤内找到了最优解，表现出快速且稳定的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子对接计算成本高，限制了实验精度和问题规模，现有量子方法（如高斯玻色采样和门控量子算法）仍有改进空间。

Method: 采用量子电路演化（QCE），一种基于门控量子计算机的无梯度量子进化方法，通过随机应用酉操作驱动量子电路演化来求解分子对接问题。

Result: 该算法在比先前研究更少的步骤内找到最优解，展现出快速且稳定的收敛性能。

Conclusion: QCE方法在分子对接问题上优于已有量子方法，有望提升分子对接的实用性和计算效率。

Abstract: Molecular docking is an important step in drug discovery, enabling the evaluation of receptor-ligand affinity while reducing experimental costs and increasing the number of possible tests. However, the high computational cost associated with molecular docking remains a limiting factor that can restrict both the experimental precision and the scale of the problems being addressed. To improve the future applicability of molecular docking, recent works have proposed the use of quantum algorithms based on Gaussian Boson Sampling quantum computers and also gate-based quantum computers. In this work, we propose the use of Quantum Circuit Evolution (QCE) for solving the molecular docking problem, a gate based and gradient-free quantum evolutionary method whose evolution is driven by the random application of unitary operations to a quantum circuit. The proposed algorithm demonstrated the ability to find the best solution to the problem in fewer steps than the methods presented in previous studies, exhibiting fast and stable convergence.

</details>


### [116] [Defeating Barren Plateaus with Task-Aligned Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12100)
*Ruipeng Xing,Yanan Li,Zhen Shang,Shengbin Wang,Yongjian Gu,Zhimin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，量子递归神经网络（QRNN）的可训练性关键不在于循环电路拓扑本身，而在于通过跨时间步的参数共享强制执行时间平移对称性。无参数共享时，梯度方差随序列长度指数衰减（贫瘠高原）；施加参数共享后，该依赖关系变为多项式，从而抑制贫瘠高原。数值模拟验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贫瘠高原（梯度指数级消失）是训练大规模量子神经网络的根本障碍，而QRNN作为处理序列数据的自然架构，其是否受此影响尚未明确。解决这一问题是推动量子递归模型可扩展训练的关键。

Method: 理论推导了QRNN中梯度方差的缩放律：针对有无参数共享两种情形，分别分析了梯度方差随序列长度的渐近行为；通过量子电路分析建立了时间平移对称性与梯度缩放之间的定量关系，并辅以数值模拟验证。

Result: 1) 无参数共享时，梯度方差随序列长度指数衰减，导致贫瘠高原；2) 跨时间步参数共享将梯度方差从指数依赖变为多项式依赖；3) 时间平移对称性是抑制贫瘠高原的根本机制。

Conclusion: 任务对齐的对称性（时间平移对称性）可以有效解决量子神经网络中表达性与可训练性之间的根本矛盾，为设计可训练的量子递归模型提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Barren plateaus -- the exponential vanishing of gradients -- are a fundamental obstacle to training scalable quantum neural networks. Whether they arise in quantum recurrent neural networks (QRNNs), a natural architecture for sequential data, remains a pressing question. Here we show that the decisive ingredient for trainability in QRNNs is not the recurrent circuit topology per se, but enforcing time-translation symmetry through parameter sharing across time steps. We prove that, without parameter sharing, QRNNs suffer from barren plateaus, with gradient variance decaying exponentially with sequence length. Imposing parameter sharing across time steps fundamentally alters this scaling, transforming it into a polynomial dependence and thereby suppressing the barren plateau. Numerical simulations corroborate these analytical predictions. By rigorously showing how time-translation symmetry suppresses barren plateaus and enhances learning capability in QRNNs, our work establishes task-aligned symmetry as a constructive resolution to the expressivity-trainability tension in quantum neural networks.

</details>


### [117] [Error correction on an array of superconducting qubits with defective components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12118)
*Julien M. Drouet,Xanda C. Kolesnikow,Campbell K. McLauchlan,Georgia M. Nixon,Seok-Hyung Lee,Dominic J. Williamson,Stephen D. Bartlett,Benjamin J. Brown,Robin Harper*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在120个超导量子比特的二维方形阵列上实现并比较了多种距离-5表面码缺陷处理策略，证明排除缺陷组件可将逻辑错误率降低2.8倍（1.62% vs 4.49%），并恢复了基于测量的逻辑门性能，展示了缺陷排除方法在固态量子计算规模化中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子计算架构需要大量耦合量子比特阵列，但制造过程不可避免会产生性能不均的量子比特和耦合器，其中一些因制造缺陷而表现不佳。量子纠错需要高性能组件，因此必须处理这些缺陷——要么通过调整量子纠错码来排除缺陷，要么通过后处理告知解码器来适应缺陷。

Method: 在120个超导量子比特的方形晶格阵列上实现了距离-5表面码，比较了三种策略：(1) 标准缺陷忽略方法；(2) 缺陷感知解码（后处理适应缺陷）；(3) 排除缺陷组件。通过记忆实验和稳定性实验评估逻辑错误率，并结合泄漏后选择技术进一步优化。

Result: 排除缺陷方法相比标准缺陷忽略方法，每轮逻辑错误率从4.49%降至1.62%，提升约2.8倍；缺陷感知解码仅带来微弱改进。稳定性实验中，排除缺陷实现了每轮6.3%的失败率抑制，而标准方法无任何抑制。结合泄漏后选择后，距离-5码在一个基上超越了最佳距离-3码的性能。

Conclusion: 排除缺陷组件是处理固态量子计算中制造缺陷的最有效策略，显著优于缺陷忽略和缺陷感知解码方法。该研究为缺陷排除方法在固态量子计算规模化中的必要性提供了原理性验证。

Abstract: A solid-state quantum-computing architecture will require the fabrication of arrays of many coupled qubits. It is inevitable that this process will produce qubits and couplers with varying performance, with some components underperforming due to imperfect fabrication. Quantum error-correction requires high-performing components and hence these defects must be dealt with, either by adapting the code to exclude the defects, or by informing the decoder to accommodate defects in post-processing. Here we implement and compare strategies to operate distance-5 surface codes on a quantum processor consisting of a square-lattice array of 120 superconducting qubits. We demonstrate a dramatic reduction in the probability of a logical error in a memory experiment by excluding underperforming components, compared with both a standard approach of ignoring defects, and a defect-aware decoding approach. We observe up to 2.8X improvement in logical errors per round when excluding defects compared with the standard defect-ignorant approach (1.62% compared to 4.49%). In contrast, defect-aware decoding gives only modest gains. Defects are also expected to be particularly harmful for measurement-based logical operations. Using a stability experiment we show that excluding defects resurrects measurement-based logic gate performance, observing a 6.3% per-round suppression of failure rate when excluding defects, compared to zero suppression otherwise. Furthermore, we show a further substantial decrease in logical errors when using leakage post-selection in combination with our defect exclusion strategies, resulting in a distance-5 code outperforming the best distance-3 in one basis. Our experiments therefore give a proof-of-principle demonstration of the essential utility of defect exclusion methods in the scale-up of solid-state quantum computing approaches.

</details>


### [118] [Categorical Tensor-Graph Semantics for Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12128)
*Naihong Hu,Ruining Li,Futao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在FHilb范畴中，从范畴张量图语义的视角系统研究了量子计算协议，将伯恩斯坦-瓦齐拉尼、西蒙、广义多伊奇-约萨（qutrit版）、广义单次Grover算法以及CNOT门、贝尔态、GHZ态、W态制备等核心量子过程重新表示为拓扑图结构，提供了一个可组合、可扩展的图论工具包，适用于自动化电路优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统希尔伯特空间形式化方法用高维矩阵运算掩盖了量子算法的结构本质，作者希望从范畴张量图语义的直观视角揭示量子算法的核心数学本质和运行机制。

Method: 在FHilb范畴中建立拓扑图框架，将量子算法的功能直接编码为图形骨架，利用拓扑变换蒸馏算法核心本质；通过互补Frobenius结构实现CNOT门，用图论方法追溯纠缠态的图形起源。

Result: 完成了Bernstein-Vazirani算法、Simon算法、qutrit广义Deutsch-Jozsa算法、广义单次Grover算法、CNOT门、Bell态、GHZ态及W态制备协议的完整图形化表示与简化。

Conclusion: 该工作桥接了张量范畴理论与实用量子算法设计，为跨量子硬件生态的自动化电路优化提供了可组合、可扩展的图论工具包。

Abstract: This paper systematically investigates quantum computing protocols from the intuitive perspective of categorical tensor-graph semantics within the category FHilb. While conventional Hilbert-space formalisms conceal the structural nature of quantum algorithms behind high-dimensional matrix operations, the topological framework developed herein directly encodes algorithmic functionalities to their graphical skeletons. We provide a comprehensive topological reinterpretation of the Bernstein-Vazirani and the Simon algorithms, demonstrating how topological transformations distill their core mathematical essence and clarify the operational mechanism of oracles. Going beyond standard qubit models, we formalize the qutrit-adapted generalized Deutsch-Jozsa algorithm as well as the generalized single-shot Grover algorithm, explicitly laying out their graphical representations. We further implement CNOT gates via complementary Frobenius structures, trace the diagrammatic genesis of quantum entanglement including Bell and GHZ states, and present a diagrammatic simplification for W-state preparation protocol. By bridging tensor category theory with practical quantum algorithmic design, this work furnishes composable, scalable diagrammatic toolkit essential for automated circuit optimization across the evolving quantum hardware ecosystem.

</details>


### [119] [Witnessing the Quantum Mpemba Effect with a Single Observable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12163)
*J. M. Z. Choquehuanca,P. A. C. Obando,M. S. Sarandy,F. M. de Paula*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于单一可观测量的通用框架，用于在马尔可夫开放量子系统中明确证伪Mpemba效应（即远离平衡的系统比接近平衡的系统更快弛豫的现象）。该框架通过定义“Mpemba可观测量”避免了完整的量子态层析，为量子态制备和热任务优化提供了实用工具，并以量子比特在广义振幅阻尼下的局部磁化为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应（远离平衡态的系统比接近平衡态的系统更快弛豫）违背了非平衡统计力学的直觉，现有方法缺乏一个通用且无需完整态层析的判定框架。核心动机是建立一种明确、可操作的判据来识别该现象，并降低实验验证的复杂度。

Method: 引入一个基于单一可观测量（称为Mpemba可观测量）的通用理论框架，专门针对马尔可夫开放量子系统。该可观测量被设计为能够作为Mpemba效应的签名，通过监测其时间演化而非重构整个量子态来判断效应是否发生。以局部磁化作为具体实例，并在广义振幅阻尼模型下的量子比特系统中进行理论演示。

Result: 成功展示了如何利用局部磁化这一单一可观测量来无歧义地见证Mpemba效应，验证了该方法在广义振幅阻尼下量子比特系统中的有效性。该框架避免了传统方法所需的完整量子态层析，降低了实验资源消耗。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子系统中Mpemba效应的实验观测提供了一种通用且高效的方案，有望推动量子态制备（如加速冷却）和热力学任务优化等实际应用。未来可进一步推广到更复杂的量子系统和非马尔可夫情形。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, in which a system farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one closer to it, defies the intuition of nonequilibrium statistical mechanics. Here, we introduce a general framework to unambiguously witness this phenomenon based on a single observable defined for Markovian open quantum systems. The Mpemba observable bypasses full state tomography, offering utility for state preparation and thermal task optimization. We illustrate our method using local magnetization as a Mpemba signature, applying it to a qubit under generalized amplitude damping.

</details>


### [120] [Axion like particles multi-parameter sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12164)
*Hassan Manshouri,Moslem Zarei,Mehdi Abdi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用量子计量技术约束类轴子粒子（ALPs）的质量与耦合常数，通过多探针检测策略规避量子Fisher信息矩阵的奇异性，在轴子质量≤10⁻³ eV范围内将耦合常数上限压低至10⁻⁷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类轴子粒子（ALPs）是理解暗物质和暗能量的关键，但其质量与耦合常数尚未被有效约束，现有方法灵敏度不足。

Method: 引入量子计量技术，利用ALPs介导的奇偶标量自旋-自旋相互作用，采用位置依赖的自旋传感器，并设计多探针检测策略以规避量子Fisher信息矩阵的奇异性。

Result: 在轴子质量m_a ≤ 10⁻³ eV范围内，可将耦合常数g^e_p g^n_p排除至10⁻⁷量级。

Conclusion: 该量子计量方案显著提升了对ALPs耦合常数的探测灵敏度，为暗物质与暗能量研究提供了新的实验途径。

Abstract: The search for the axion like particles (APLs)-one of the deepest puzzles in modern cosmology-may hold the key to understanding dark matter and dark energy. In this work, we introduce a setup taking advantage of the quantum metrology techniques to constrain the hypothetical mass and coupling constants of APLs by employing exotic pseudoscalar spin-spin interactions between fermions mediated by ALPs. A key advantage of our approach is the exploitation of position-dependent spin sensor results to the high sensitivity of the probe. To simultaneously investigate the axion mass and coupling constants, we invoke a multi-probe detection strategy. Through this strategy, we circumvent the singularity of the quantum Fisher information matrix as an ultimate upper bound on the sensitivity of any probe. For the axion masses in the range of $m_a {\le} 10^{-3} \text{eV}$, this setup can exclude values of axion coupling constants $g^e_pg^n_p$ down to $10^{-7}$.

</details>


### [121] [Anticipating Decoder Side-channel Attacks in Fault-tolerant Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12174)
*Shashvat Shukla,Dan E. Browne,Shin Nishio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种针对容错量子计算机的新型侧信道攻击：攻击者通过分析发送给解码器的综合症数据，可以推断量子计算机正在执行的逻辑电路（即"门指纹"）。研究聚焦于表面码中的Clifford+T计算，展示了不同逻辑操作如何产生独特的门指纹模式，并探讨了如何利用这些信息推断量子电路或算法。结论是解码系统可能成为侧信道攻击的向量，因此需要被安全保护或由可信方构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算成为应用技术，保护量子计算机免受信息安全攻击的需求日益增长。现有研究未关注解码器系统作为潜在侧信道攻击向量的问题。

Method: 定义"门指纹"概念——综合症数据中指示特定逻辑操作的模式；聚焦表面码中Clifford+T计算，展示逻辑操作产生门指纹的不同机制；分析如何利用门指纹信息推断量子计算机上运行的电路或算法。

Result: 成功识别了新型侧信道攻击向量；证明了逻辑操作在综合症数据中产生可区分的模式（门指纹）；表明解码器系统可被利用来推断量子计算内容。

Conclusion: 解码系统是侧信道攻击的潜在向量；为防止此类攻击，解码系统应被安全保护或由可信方构建。

Abstract: As quantum computing emerges as an applied technology, there is a growing need to protect quantum computers against information security attacks. This work identifies a new class of side-channel attacks against fault-tolerant quantum computers, in which the syndrome data that is sent to the decoder system is used to infer which computation (logical circuit) is taking place on the quantum computer. Our work introduces the concept of gate fingerprints, which describes those patterns present in syndrome data that indicate which logical operation took place on the quantum computer. We show different effects by which logical operations produce gate fingerprints by focusing on Clifford+T computation in the surface code. We then explore how gate fingerprint information can be used to make inferences about the circuits or algorithms run on a quantum computer. Our findings indicate that decoder systems can be a vector for side-channel attacks and thus to prevent this, decoder systems should either be secured or built by a trusted party.

</details>


### [122] [Nearly optimal polynomial approximations for the quantum singular value transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12190)
*Evan Rule*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了在区间[-1,1]上具有简单Chebyshev系数的奇偶阶跃函数多项式近似，证明了其误差界和近最优性（与理论最优误差仅差对数因子），并应用于量子相位估计、线性振幅放大、特征值阈值化等量子奇异值变换算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子奇异值变换（QSVT）等量子算法提供高效、易于数值实现的多项式近似，解决现有近似方法在数值实现复杂度或最优性方面的不足。

Method: 构造具有简单Chebyshev系数的奇偶阶跃函数多项式近似，推导严格误差界，证明其误差与理论最优误差之比随多项式阶数呈对数增长（近最优性）。

Result: 获得了近最优的多项式近似（误差乘性因子对数增长），且Chebyshev系数简单易于数值实现。

Conclusion: 该多项式近似可有效用于量子相位估计、线性振幅放大、特征值阈值化及量子奇异值变换等量子算法。

Abstract: We introduce polynomial approximations of the even and odd step functions on the interval $[-1,1]$ with simple Chebyshev coefficients, making their numerical implementation straightforward. We derive rigorous error bounds and demonstrate that these polynomials are nearly optimal in the sense that their error deviates from the theoretically optimal error by a multiplicative factor that grows logarithmically with the polynomial order. From these polynomials, we derive related nearly optimal polynomial approximations that can be used to perform quantum phase estimation, linear amplitude amplification, eigenvalue thresholding, and other quantum algorithms using the quantum singular value transform.

</details>


### [123] [Constant-Depth Multi-Product Formula for Trotter Error Mitigation in Near-Term Digital Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12225)
*Chan Bin Bark,Sangjin Lee,Kwang Jun Ahn,Sangmo Cheon,Moon Jip Park,Youngseok Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种恒定深度的多乘积公式（cd-MPF），通过经典后处理组合多个固定深度电路来压制Trotter误差，将误差标度从d⁻²提升至d⁻⁴，并指出在现有量子硬件噪声水平下，传统高阶公式的优势仅限于10⁻²以下绝对误差范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字量子模拟中，算法Trotter误差与物理噪声之间存在固有矛盾：高阶乘积公式可降低算法误差，但会显著增加电路深度，从而引入更多物理噪声。现有噪声水平下，这种权衡的可行优势被限制在极小的误差范围内，亟需一种不增加电路深度即可压制算法误差的方法。

Method: 引入恒定深度多乘积公式（cd-MPF），通过经典线性组合多个固定深度的量子电路输出，引入辅助参数α重塑Trotter误差项，在不增加电路深度的前提下消除领先阶(Δt)²算法误差，将误差随双量子比特电路深度d的标度从d⁻²提升至d⁻⁴。

Result: 基准测试表明，传统高阶公式在现有硬件噪声水平下的可行优势被限制在10⁻²绝对误差以下，而cd-MPF通过恒定深度实现了更陡峭的误差标度，可有效结合物理噪声抑制技术。

Conclusion: cd-MPF可作为近-term量子硬件上实现长时间量子动力学模拟的关键技术，结合物理噪声缓解方法有望突破当前噪声限制。

Abstract: Digital quantum simulation of many-body dynamics faces a tension between algorithmic Trotter error and physical noise that accumulates with circuit depth. Typical higher-order product formulas mitigate the algorithmic error at the cost of deeper circuits. Our benchmark shows that, within the limited physical error budget, the feasible advantage is confined to absolute errors well below the $10^{-2}$ scale, which vanishes under noise levels of current quantum hardware. In sharp contrast to the previous Trotter error mitigation methods, we introduce a constant-depth multi-product formula (cd-MPF) that suppresses the Trotter error by combining multiple circuits at fixed circuit depth. We identify an auxiliary parameter $α$, which reshapes the Trotter error terms while leaving the target evolution invariant. The classical linear combination of the measured expectation values cancels the leading $(Δt)^{2}$ algorithmic contribution and steepens the Trotter-error scaling with the two-qubit circuit depth $d$ from $d^{-2}$ to $d^{-4}$. Combined with physical-noise mitigation, our method can serve as a key ingredient for realizing long-time quantum dynamics simulation on near-term hardware.

</details>


### [124] [Nonequilibrium thermodynamics of the acoustoelectric quantum vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12228)
*Ryan O. Behunin,Andrew Shepherd,Francesco Intravaia,Matt Eichenfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了声电系统中声子与等离激元相互作用时，量子真空因载流子漂移速度超过声速而呈现热特性的现象，通过开放量子系统分析表明真空温度由漂移速度和声子波矢决定，实际参数下可达几开尔文，为研究量子真空效应提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子真空在特定运动学条件下呈现热特性的机制，并寻找能够实验观测该效应的可行物理平台。

Method: 采用开放量子系统分析方法，研究声电系统中漂移速度超过声速的载流子与声子、等离激元的相互作用，推导量子真空的有效温度表达式。

Result: 声电量子真空具有热特性，有效温度由漂移速度和声子波矢决定；在现实参数条件下可达数开尔文。

Conclusion: 声电系统为研究量子真空效应提供了一个有前景的实验平台。

Abstract: The quantum vacuum can assume thermal properties as a consequence of system kinematics, highlighting the nuance of our definition of particles in quantum field theory. Here, we explore this phenomenon in acoustoelectric systems, involving the interaction of phonons and plasmons, where the charge carriers drift at a constant velocity exceeding the speed of sound. Through an open quantum systems analysis, we show that the acoustoelectric quantum vacuum acquires a thermal character with a temperature defined by the drift velocity and the phonon wavevector. Realistic parameters yield effective temperatures of several Kelvin, establishing acoustoelectric systems as a promising platform for the investigation of quantum vacuum effects.

</details>


### [125] [A fault-tolerant quantum blockchain deployed on commercial telecommunications network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12249)
*Yongqiang Du,Chen-Xun Weng,Feng Xie,Ming-Yang Li,Mingxuan Zhang,Xin Hua,Xin An,Xiang Guan,Xin Liu,Zhenrong Zhang,Xi Xiao,Hua-Lei Yin,Kejin Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验演示了一种基于光子集成电路、部署于商用电信基础设施的全功能混合量子区块链架构，实现了近1/2的容错率（超越经典极限）、秒级共识达成以及约500 TPS的吞吐量，并通过食品溯源应用验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计算时代，区块链需要保持其密码学完整性和分布式容错韧性的核心优势。量子数字签名和量子拜占庭协议理论上可为区块链提供基础安全保证并容忍高达一半的恶意节点，但这类量子增强区块链的实际实现仍面临重大挑战。

Method: 提出并实验演示了一种混合量子区块链架构：基于光子集成电路（PIC）实现量子数字签名和量子拜占庭协议，并部署在商用经典电信基础设施上。

Result: 系统实现了近1/2的容错率（超越经典极限）、秒级共识时间，以及食品溯源应用中约500笔/秒的交易吞吐量。

Conclusion: 该工作为实用化量子区块链奠定了基础，能够在量子时代实现安全、可扩展、去中心化的信息处理。

Abstract: Popularized by the Bitcoin cryptocurrency, blockchain technology establishes a decentralized digital framework that utilizes cryptographic and consensus protocols to secure data against unauthorized modification. Consequently, blockchain has found broad adoption across diverse fields, including finance, data management, healthcare, and digital asset governance. In the quantum computing era, a paramount objective for blockchain is to preserve its foundational advantages of cryptographic integrity and decentralized fault-tolerant resilience. In principle, quantum digital signatures and quantum Byzantine agreement protocols offer foundational security guarantees and tolerate up to one-half of malicious nodes for blockchain. However, the practical realization of such a quantum-enhanced blockchain remains a significant and multifaceted challenge. Here, we propose and experimentally demonstrate a fully operational hybrid quantum blockchain architecture built on photonic integrated circuits and deployed over commercially available classical telecommunications infrastructure. The system achieves a fault tolerance of nearly one-half, surpassing the classical limit, while reaching consensus on a timescale of seconds. A deployed food traceability application validates the practicality of the proposed architecture, achieving a throughput of approximately 500 transactions per second. This work establishes a foundation for practical quantum blockchains, enabling secure, scalable, and decentralized information processing in the emerging quantum era.

</details>


### [126] [Experimental demonstration of scalable quantum blockchain with exponentially superior quantum communication complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12250)
*Feng Xie,Ming-Yang Li,Yongqiang Du,Chen-Xun Weng,Mingxuan Zhang,Xin Hua,Xiang Guan,Xin An,Jingzhe He,Xin Liu,Zhenrong Zhang,Xi Xiao,Hua-Lei Yin,Kejin Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了一种基于弱相干态的量子区块链协议，通过环形量子拜占庭协议在避免多体纠缠的情况下实现信息论安全，并在六节点光子集成网络平台上实现，量子通信资源消耗不到传统方案的4%，同时展示了每秒805.3笔交易且零失败的量子安全代币交换应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高容错量子区块链协议面临可扩展性挑战——区块链三难困境导致要么通信复杂度指数增长，要么需要实验上难以实现的多体纠缠，亟需一种既保持信息论安全又可扩展的量子区块链方案。

Method: 提出基于弱相干态的环形量子拜占庭协议，避免使用多体纠缠；在光子集成芯片平台上实现六节点网络，部署于商用电信基础设施。

Result: 量子通信资源消耗仅为传统方案的4%；量子安全代币交换应用实现吞吐量805.3 TPS且零故障。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了通向可扩展量子区块链的实用路径，在保持信息论安全的同时大幅降低资源需求，为分布式数字基础设施的量子安全升级提供了可行方案。

Abstract: To secure modern distributed digital infrastructures, quantum blockchains exploit quantum resources to achieve information-theoretic security and surpass the classical one-third fault-tolerance bound. However, existing high-fault-tolerant protocols face a fundamental scalability challenge: the blockchain trilemma imposes either exponential communication complexity or experimentally demanding multipartite entanglement. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a scalable quantum blockchain protocol based on weak coherent states that achieves an exponential reduction in quantum communication complexity. The protocol employs a circular quantum Byzantine agreement mechanism that preserves information-theoretic security while avoiding multipartite entanglement. We implement this protocol on a photonic integrated circuit platform, realizing a six-node network over commercially available telecommunication infrastructure. Compared with previous schemes, the protocol requires less than 4% of the quantum communication resources. Leveraging this advantage, we further demonstrate a quantum-secured token exchange application achieving a throughput of 805.3 transactions per second with zero failures. These results establish a practical pathway toward scalable quantum blockchain.

</details>


### [127] [Heisenberg Scaling in Many-Body Kinetic Uncertainty Relation via Quantum Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12264)
*Hayato Yunoki,Yoshihiko Hasegawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用量子反馈控制超辐射自旋系综实现计数可观测量海森堡标度（1/N²）精度的协议，通过多体动力学不确定性关系和反馈修正的均场方程从理论上证明了该结果，并用数值模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多体效应能否提升量子器件（如量子钟）的计数精度，特别是能否实现类似量子计量学中海森堡标度（1/N²）的计数精度标度，目前尚缺乏相关协议。

Method: 建立了一个通过量子反馈控制超辐射自旋系综的协议；利用多体动力学不确定性关系和反馈修正的均场方程进行理论分析；通过数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 成功实现了计数可观测量波动的1/N²海森堡标度；发现超辐射对活性的增强是瞬态的，需要反馈控制才能实现该标度；反馈可将集体耗散转化为实现海森堡标度计数的资源。

Conclusion: 反馈控制能够将集体耗散转化为资源，使得超辐射自旋系综的计数精度达到海森堡标度，为量子器件的精密测量提供了新途径。

Abstract: Precision is a central figure of merit for quantum devices, including quantum clocks whose performance is determined by the stability of counting events. Kinetic uncertainty relations set fundamental limits on the precision of such counting observables, showing that their fluctuations cannot be suppressed without increasing the activity of the system. While many-body effects offer a natural route to enhanced performance, it remains unclear how far they can enhance counting precision. In quantum metrology, Heisenberg scaling refers to the suppression of estimation variance as $1/N^2$ with the particle number $N$. This raises the question of whether the fluctuation of counting observables can exhibit an analogous Heisenberg-like $1/N^2$ scaling, but no protocol for achieving it has been established. We establish a protocol that achieves this scaling by applying quantum feedback to a superradiant spin ensemble. Because the superradiant enhancement of activity is transient, the scaling of counting precision becomes achievable only when it is controlled by feedback. We establish this result analytically through a many-body kinetic uncertainty relation and feedback-modified mean-field equations, and show it by numerical simulations. Our results demonstrate that feedback can turn collective dissipation into a resource for Heisenberg scaling of counting precision.

</details>


### [128] [Demonstration of tripartite cat states in two distinct classes of entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12317)
*May Chee Loke,Jonathan Schwinger,Kehui Yu,Yingshan Zhang,Amon M. Kasper,Tanjung Krisnanda,Yvonne Y. Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验实现了两种不等价的宏观三部分连续变量纠缠猫态（GHZ-cat态和W-cat态），通过一个辅助超导transmon对三个微波谐振腔进行条件控制，并验证了其纠缠分类特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真正的多部分纠缠态在宏观连续变量领域仍然难以实现，而多部分纠缠是量子计算、通信和计量学的重要资源。

Method: 利用超导transmon的三个能级，对三个微波谐振腔实现一对多的条件控制，在同一硬件设备上生成两种不同类别的三部分纠缠猫态，并采用高效子空间层析成像协议进行态重构。

Result: GHZ-cat态保真度0.83±0.02，W-cat态保真度0.70±0.02，验证了三种振荡器间的真正多部分纠缠；实验证明W-cat态中存在成对纠缠而GHZ-cat态中不存在，确认了两种纠缠类的本质差异。

Conclusion: 该按需生成和表征不等价三部分连续变量纠缠的技术，为未来宏观多体纠缠研究和基于多部分玻色子码的容错量子信息处理提供了有价值的实验平台。

Abstract: Entanglement is a cornerstone of quantum mechanics and an essential resource for quantum computation, communication, and metrology. While bipartite entanglement is extensively studied, genuine multipartite entangled states remain largely inaccessible in the macroscopic continuous-variable domain. Here, we experimentally realize macroscopic tripartite entangled cat states of the two distinct classes with fundamentally inequivalent properties, namely GHZ-cat and W-cat states, encoded across three microwave resonators coupled to an ancillary superconducting transmon. We develop one-to-all conditional controls on the three oscillators by fully utilizing three energy levels of the transmon to generate these states in a single piece of hardware and employ an efficient subspace tomography protocol for state reconstruction. We obtain GHZ-cat and W-cat states with fidelities $0.83\pm0.02$ and $0.70\pm0.02$, respectively, certifying genuine multipartite entanglement among the three oscillators. We further validate the distinct entanglement classes by experimentally demonstrating that pairwise entanglement is present in the W-cat state but absent in the GHZ-cat state. Our technique for the on-demand generation and characterization of inequivalent classes of tripartite continuous-variable entanglement provides a valuable testbed for future studies of macroscopic many-body entanglement and fault-tolerant quantum information processing with multipartite bosonic codes.

</details>


### [129] [Parallel Hadamard Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12344)
*Soichiro Imamura,Synge Todo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并行Hadamard测试，将多个Hadamard测试合并为单一电路类型，显著减少电路种类数量，并在固定每电路开销占主导时降低计算时间和财务成本，对Gram矩阵工作负载也能减少总采样次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hadamard测试在许多量子算法中需估计大量$\langle ψ|U|ψ\rangle$项，导致电路类型繁多、计算时间长、财务成本高，亟需一种统一高效的并行方案。

Method: 提出并行Hadamard测试，将多个Hadamard测试合并为单一电路类型；针对三类结构负载（任意酉算子集、前缀乘积数组、Gram矩阵元素）分别设计应用方案，并与传统方案进行成本比较。

Result: 并行Hadamard测试显著减少不同电路类型的数量；在固定每电路开销占主导时，能降低计算时间和财务成本；在Gram矩阵工作负载中，当典型非对角重叠较小时还能减少总采样次数。

Conclusion: 并行Hadamard测试是一种统一高效的量子测量方法，能有效降低算法实现的资源开销，尤其适合电路固定开销占主导或非对角重叠较小的场景。

Abstract: The Hadamard test is a fundamental building block widely used in many quantum computing algorithms. It estimates the real or imaginary part of $\langle ψ\vert U \vert ψ\rangle$, where $\vert ψ\rangle$ is a quantum state and $U$ is a unitary operator. In many algorithms, however, many such quantities must be estimated, leading to a large number of distinct circuit types, long computational times, and high financial costs. In this work, we propose the parallel Hadamard test, which combines multiple Hadamard tests into a single circuit type. We demonstrate how the parallel Hadamard test applies to three structural classes of workloads: arbitrary sets of unitary operators, prefix-product arrays, and Gram-matrix elements. For each class, we compare the cost of the parallel Hadamard test with that of the conventional one. Our unified approach significantly reduces the number of distinct circuit types, and can lower both computational time and financial costs in regimes where fixed per-circuit overheads dominate the total cost. In Gram-matrix workloads, it can also reduce the total number of shots when typical off-diagonal overlaps are small.

</details>


### [130] [Deterministic Minimum-Leakage Continuous-Variable Quantum Key Distribution with Phase-Conjugated Twin Beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12432)
*Zhenlin Zhao,Dawei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性的双模连续变量量子密钥分发协议，通过产生相位共轭孪生光束实现最小泄漏，消除了Alice端的预示步骤。在极大压缩极限下，该协议与已有预示协议每传输光学模的密钥率相同，但在有限压缩下，达到相同密钥率所需压缩度低约3 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除现有对称最小泄漏CV-QKD协议中Alice端的预示（heralding）步骤，实现确定性的状态制备，降低实验复杂度。

Method: Alice将两个反向压缩的高斯系综在平衡分束器上组合，将两个输出模（相位共轭孪生光束）同时发送给Bob；通过纠缠基源框架建立与预示协议的关联，并分析模式间相关的双模高斯攻击。

Result: 在极大压缩极限下，两种协议每传输光学模的密钥率相同；有限压缩下，相位共轭孪生光束协议达到相同密钥率所需的压缩度低约3 dB；相关攻击比独立攻击略有效，但优势在最小泄漏条件下有限。

Conclusion: 相位共轭孪生光束为实现对称最小泄漏CV-QKD提供了一条确定性的、实验上更具吸引力的路径。

Abstract: Minimum-leakage continuous-variable quantum key distribution suppresses Eve's Holevo information by engineering the signal ensemble at the state-preparation stage. Existing symmetric minimum-leakage protocols achieve this goal by heralding: Alice interferes two squeezed ensembles, measures one output mode, and sends the other to Bob. Here we propose a deterministic two-mode protocol that removes the Alice-side heralding step. Alice combines two oppositely squeezed Gaussian ensembles on a balanced beam splitter and transmits both output modes, which form phase-conjugated twin beams. We show that this protocol is related to the heralding protocol through a common entanglement-based source but corresponds to a different prepare-and-measure decomposition. In the very-large-squeezing limit, the two protocols give the same secret key rate per transmitted optical mode. For finite squeezing, however, the phase-conjugated twin-beam protocol requires approximately 3 dB less squeezing to achieve the same key rate. We further analyze correlated two-mode Gaussian attacks in which Eve injects ancillary modes with optimized inter-mode correlations. We find that the correlated attacks are slightly more efficient than independent attacks, but the advantage remains limited under the minimum-leakage condition. These results show that phase-conjugated twin beams provide a deterministic and experimentally appealing route to symmetric minimum-leakage CV-QKD.

</details>


### [131] [Q2NSViz: An Open-source Standalone Visualizer for Quantum Network Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12444)
*Francesco Mazza,Marcello Caleffi,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Q2NSViz是一个开源Python可视化工具，用于重放和检查量子网络仿真轨迹，通过重构物理拓扑、飞行中量子比特、纠缠关系等动态状态，帮助研究人员和教育工作者理解量子网络的非经典特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络的独特非经典特性使其仿真和直观理解 inherently difficult，现有工具缺乏对仿真执行过程的可视化回溯能力。

Method: 基于解耦的JSON/NDJSON轨迹合约、Qt-free重放引擎和交互式PyQt6界面，重构仿真网络状态的时间演化，包括物理拓扑、存储与飞行中量子比特、经典比特与数据包、测量结果及纠缠关系。

Result: 实现了将执行轨迹转化为可导航、可复现的可视化产物，暴露了物理连接性以及协议执行产生的动态纠缠诱导结构。

Conclusion: Q2NSViz作为Q2NS的独立伴侣工具，可被其他发射相同轨迹格式的仿真后端复用，为零编码的研究人员和教育工作者提供了缩小抽象协议逻辑与具体执行之间鸿沟的工具。

Abstract: The unique and non-classical features of quantum networks make their simulation and intuitive understanding inherently difficult. In this work, we present Q2NSViz, an open-source Python-based visualization tool for replaying and inspecting quantum-network simulation traces. Q2NSViz reconstructs the time evolution of the simulated network state, including physical topology, stored and in-flight qubits, classical bits and packets, measurements, and entanglement relationships. In this way, it exposes not only physical connectivity, but also the dynamic entanglement-induced structure produced, consumed, and transformed by protocol execution. Q2NSViz is built around a decoupled JSON/NDJSON trace contract, a Qt-free replay engine, and an interactive PyQt6 interface, making it a standalone companion to Q2NS and reusable by other simulation backends that emit the same trace format. By turning execution traces into navigable and reproducible visual artifacts, Q2NSViz provides a zero-coding tool for researchers and educators, narrowing the gap between abstract protocol logic and concrete execution.

</details>


### [132] [Strain-Induced Detuning of a Dressed Nitrogen-Vacancy Qubit: Effective Two-Level Theory and Its Validity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12453)
*Jihyeon Jeon,Donghun Lee,Seok-Kyun Son,Nojoon Myoung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了受应变影响的微波修饰金刚石NV色心量子比特的解析有效二能级模型，定量分析了横向应变如何破坏磁场噪声鲁棒性，并通过模拟ESR谱和导出有效性判据为实验设计提供了实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心在微波修饰下的理想二能级量子比特对静磁场一阶不敏感，但实际金刚石器件中残余横向晶格应变会混合自旋子能级，破坏这种磁场鲁棒性。需要理论模型定量描述应变效应及其对量子比特性能的影响。

Method: 从完整的三能级修饰哈密顿量出发，通过微扰消除远失谐旁观态，获得受应变修饰NV量子比特的解析有效二能级模型；导出修饰态分裂、自旋锁定混合角及纵向磁场耦合的闭式表达式；结合基于速率方程的光学读出模拟脉冲ESR谱；从本征值和旁观态权重导出精确有效性判据，并简化为两个实用准则。

Result: 横向应变会移动修饰态共振并倾斜自旋锁定轴，从而恢复有限的直流磁场响应，量化了磁场鲁棒性的损失。在模拟脉冲ESR谱中验证了这些特征。导出了两个实用判据：旁观态分支必须保持在名义上态之上，且其分支特异的混合必须保持很小。

Conclusion: 通过轴向场-横向应变平面的有效性相图总结了近似条件，为设计修饰NV传感实验提供了实用指南。

Abstract: The nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center in diamond can be operated as a microwave-dressed qubit. In the ideal two-level limit, its transition frequency is first-order insensitive to static magnetic fields, providing robustness against magnetic detuning noise. In practical diamond devices, however, residual transverse crystal strain mixes the $\ket{m_{s}=\pm1}$ spin sublevels and modifies the dressed qubit. In this study, we derive an analytical effective two-level model of a strained dressed NV qubit by perturbatively eliminating the far-detuned spectator state from the full three-level dressed Hamiltonian. We obtain closed-form expressions for the dressed-state splitting, the spin-locking mixing angle, and the longitudinal magnetic-field coupling. We show that transverse strain shifts the dressed-state resonance and tilts the spin-locking axis. These two effects restore a finite DC-field response and thereby quantify the loss of magnetic robustness. We demonstrate these features in simulated pulsed electron spin resonance spectra that incorporate rate-equation-based optical readout. We further derive exact validity criteria from the eigenvalues and spectator weights of the full three-level Hamiltonian. For practical use, we reduce these criteria to two controlled guidelines: the spectator-like branch must remain above the nominal upper dressed state, and its branch-specific admixture must remain small. A validity diagram over the axial-field--transverse-strain plane summarizes these approximate conditions and provides practical guidelines for designing dressed-NV sensing experiments.

</details>


### [133] [Sparse anisotropic positive maps for qutrit entanglement: exact indecomposability and PPT geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12470)
*Massimiliano F. Sacchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两参数稀疏双随机正映射族在qutrit上的性质，完整刻画了其正性、完全正性、可分解性相图，并构建了PPT纠缠边态及其暴露面，提供了研究正映射与纠缠检测的统一解析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正映射是检测纠缠（尤其是超越PPT判据的束缚纠缠）最直接的工具之一，但可解析处理的具体例子很少。本文旨在构建一个具有稀疏结构、可精确求解的两参数正映射族，以便在单一框架下系统地研究正性、完全正性、可分解性、PPT纠缠检测、最优性以及凸几何中的暴露面等概念。

Method: 引入并分析qutrit上由参数w和z独立调控两个相干通道的稀疏双随机正映射族W(w,z)。通过解析推导得到完整的相图：正性区域为[0,2/3]×[0,2/3]方形，完全正性区域为[0,1/3]×[0,1/3]方形，可分解性在w,z≥1/3的象限中于四分之一圆外丧失。在端点W(2/3,2/3)处构造了(5,5)秩型的四参数PPT边态族，并推导其解析检测区域。

Result: 完整确定了该映射族的正性、完全正性和可分解性相图；利用显式构造的PPT纠缠态证明了不可分解区域；证明了W(2/3,2/3)处的边态射线是PPT锥的暴露面；尽管W(2/3,2/3)不是最优映射，但给出了一个显式的最优精化映射，在该边态族上的检测区域严格更大。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个可解析处理的qutrit框架，其中正性、不可分解性、PPT纠缠、最优性和暴露凸几何等概念可以在单一体系中同时研究，为理解正映射和纠缠检测提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Positive but not completely positive maps provide one of the most direct ways to detect entanglement beyond the positive-partial-transpose (PPT) criterion. We introduce and analyze an exactly solvable two-parameter family of sparse bistochastic positive maps on qutrits, in which two coherence channels are independently tuned by parameters $w$ and $z$. The sparse structure makes the full phase diagram analytic: positivity holds exactly on the square $0\le w,z\le2/3$, complete positivity on the smaller square $0\le w,z\le1/3$, and decomposability is lost precisely outside a quarter circle in the corner $w,z\ge1/3$. The indecomposable region is certified by explicit PPT entangled state adapted to the same witness geometry. At the endpoint $W_*=W(2/3,2/3)$ we construct a four-parameter family of PPT edge states of rank type $(5,5)$, derive their analytic detection region, and show that the corresponding rays are exposed faces of the PPT cone. Finally, although $W_*$ is not optimal, we give an explicit optimal refinement whose detection region on this family is strictly larger. The result is an analytically tractable qutrit setting in which positivity, indecomposability, PPT entanglement, optimality, and exposed convex geometry can be studied in a single framework.

</details>


### [134] [Benchmarking loss functions for trainable quantum feature maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12487)
*Nguyen Dinh Quyen,Vu Tuan Hai,Quoc Chuong Nguyen,Le Bin Ho,Lan Nguyen Tran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了对数量似然损失、距离损失和测量损失三种损失函数在可训练量子特征映射中的优化动态、计算成本和分类性能，发现对数量似然损失在保持线性计算复杂度的同时实现了比测量损失更稳定的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定量子特征映射对复杂非线性分类任务表达力不足，而可训练量子特征映射虽能增强学习能力，但不同损失函数诱导的优化动态差异尚不明确，亟需系统研究。

Method: 将Log-Likelihood Loss应用于可训练量子特征映射，并与Distance Loss和Measurement Loss进行系统对比，通过大量数值实验分析三种损失函数的优化动态、计算成本和分类性能。

Result: Log-Likelihood Loss在保持线性计算复杂度的同时，优化稳定性优于Measurement Loss。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为量子核优化中平衡可训练性、计算效率和预测性能提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Many quantum machine learning models employ quantum feature maps to encode classical data into quantum states. While fixed feature maps often lack sufficient expressivity for complex nonlinear classification tasks, trainable quantum feature maps (TQFMs) enable adaptive quantum kernels with enhanced learning capability. Different loss functions can induce distinct optimization dynamics, yet their effects remain poorly understood. In this work, we apply the Log-Likelihood Loss function for TQFMs and provide a systematic comparison with Distance Loss and Measurement Loss. Through extensive numerical experiments, we compare their optimization dynamics, computational costs, and classification performance. Our results show that Log-Likelihood Loss consistently achieves more stable optimization than Measurement Loss while retaining linear computational complexity. The resulting benchmark offers practical guidance for balancing trainability, computational efficiency, and predictive performance in quantum kernel optimization.

</details>


### [135] [The Infinitesimal Structure of Quantum Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12559)
*Luca Barbieri-Viale*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将量子态嵌入到高阶对偶数代数中，构建了一个统一的几何框架，使得刘维尔-冯·诺依曼动力学的非线性矩阵对易子映射为平坦、线性的代数流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子态空间建立严谨统一的几何框架，解决现有框架中非线性动力学难以处理的问题。

Method: 通过构造从量子态空间到高阶对偶数代数 $\mathcal{Q}_N \equiv \mathbb{R}[\varepsilon]/(\varepsilon^{N^2-1})$ 的光滑正则嵌入，将每个量子态表示为非约化概形理论点，利用截断环族实现非线性对易子的全局线性化。

Result: 非线性矩阵对易子被全局映射为平坦、线性的代数流，幂零对偶数代数成为高维量子运动学的纯净几何景观。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架为高维量子运动学提供了一个线性化的几何基础，有望简化相关动力学分析。

Abstract: This paper establishes a rigorous, unified geometric framework for quantum state spaces by constructing smooth, regular embeddings into higher-order dual number algebras $\mathcal{Q}_N \equiv \mathbb{R}[\varepsilon]/(\varepsilon^{N^2-1})$, wherein every quantum state is faithfully represented as a non-reduced scheme-theoretic point. We show that under this unified family of truncated rings, the non-linear matrix commutators governing the Liouville-von Neumann dynamics map globally onto flat, linear, and rigid algebraic flows, establishing nilpotent dual algebras as a pristine geometric landscape for higher-dimensional quantum kinematics.

</details>


### [136] [The Quantum Polariton Hamiltonian that Reproduces the Same High-Harmonic Generation Spectra as the Classical Hamiltonian in Strong Laser Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12560)
*Nimrod Moiseyev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在红外泵浦光子数远大于发射紫外光子数的条件下，能否定义一个产生与Floquet理论相同高次谐波产生（HHG）谱的量子哈密顿量。关键假设是发射的高次谐波强度远小于红外激光强度，且第N次谐波发射源于吸收N个红外光子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否可以在量子框架下定义一个哈密顿量，使其产生的HHG谱与Floquet理论预测一致，从而为理解强场中的高次谐波过程提供新的理论视角。

Method: 基于两个关键假设进行理论推导：1）发射的高次谐波强度远小于红外激光强度；2）第N次谐波由吸收N个红外光子产生。在此条件下构建量子哈密顿量并与Floquet理论结果进行比较。

Result: 该研究证明了在特定条件下（红外光子数远大于紫外光子数，谐波强度远小于激光强度），可以定义出与Floquet理论预测一致的量子哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 在高次谐波强度远小于驱动激光强度的弱发射条件下，存在一个量子哈密顿量能够复现Floquet理论预测的HHG谱，这为强场物理中的高次谐波产生提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: This manuscript investigates the possibility of defining a quantum Hamiltonian that leads to the same high-harmonic generation (HHG) spectra as those predicted by Floquet theory in the regime where the number of infrared (IR) pump photons is much larger than the number of emitted UV photons. The key assumption underlying our derivation is that the intensity of the emitted high harmonics is many orders of magnitude smaller than that of the IR laser, and that the emission of the Nnt harmonic results from the absorption of N IR photons.

</details>


### [137] [Fast measurement-based generation of large-scale Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state with atomic nuclear-spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12565)
*Yan Lu,Xiao-Feng Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于类碱土原子核自旋的量子铁磁门（QFG），通过四量子比特量子相位门快速制备大规模GHZ态，利用Rydberg相互作用在6π/Ωₘ时间内实现高保真度操作。从三数据原子和一辅助原子的乘积态出发，通过含单个QFG、两个单比特门和辅助原子投影测量的粘合电路生成3量子比特GHZ态，重复该电路可扩展至9、27、81、243…量子比特的GHZ态。基于现有技术分析，243量子比特GHZ态可实现，更多量子比特可通过更高探测保真度实现纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模GHZ态对量子技术至关重要但制备困难，现有方法在可扩展性和保真度方面存在瓶颈，亟需提出高效的制备方案。

Method: 提出基于类碱土原子核自旋的QFG（四量子比特量子相位门），利用Rydberg相互作用实现快速高保真操作。设计粘合电路：一个QFG、两个单比特门和辅助原子投影测量，从三数据原子和一辅助原子的乘积态生成3量子比特GHZ态，通过重复该电路递归扩展至更多量子比特。

Result: QFG可在6π/Ωₘ时间内实现高保真度；通过粘合电路可递归生成3、9、27、81、243…量子比特GHZ态；基于现有技术分析，243量子比特GHZ态可实现，更多量子比特可通过更高探测保真度实现。

Conclusion: 提出的快速测量制备方案有效实现大规模GHZ态，具有良好可扩展性，在当前技术水平下243量子比特GHZ态可行。

Abstract: Large-scale Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger~(GHZ) state is useful for quantum technologies but difficult to be prepared. Here, we propose fast measurement-based preparation of large-scale GHZ states by a four-qubit quantum phase gate with nuclear-spin qubits of alkaline-earth-like atoms, which is named as quantum ferromagnetic gate~(QFG) due to its analogy to the alignment of molecular magnetic moments in a classical magnet. A high-fidelity Rydberg-mediated QFG can be realized in a time of $6π/Ω_{\text{m}}$ with $Ω_{\text{m}}$ the maximal Rydberg Rabi frequency. From a product state of three data atom and one ancilla atom, a gluing circuit with one QFG, two single-qubit gates, and a projective measurement of the ancilla can generate a 3-qubit GHZ state, and repetition of this gluing circuit can lead to 9, 27, 81, 243, $\cdots$-qubit GHZ states. Analyses based on currently available techniques show that a 243-qubit GHZ state is realizable, and more qubits can be entangled with higher detection fidelity.

</details>


### [138] [Forked Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Non-Markovian Open Quantum Dynamics and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12567)
*Zhao-Wei Wang,Kai-Yu Yuan,Feng-Hua Ren,Zhao-Ming Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分支物理信息神经网络（FPINN）的统一框架，通过梯度级解耦优化目标，实现了非马尔可夫量子系统的联合模拟与控制，在双量子比特Heisenberg XXX模型上展示了优于传统方法的保真度和脉冲平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统策略中量子系统的模拟与控制通常是解耦的，且现有PINN方法局限于马尔可夫环境。当应用于非马尔可夫系统时，标准PINN因耦合微分方程导致的多目标优化冲突而无法可靠收敛，亟需一种能够联合优化模拟与控制的统一方法。

Method: 在先前提出的分支PINN（FPINN）基础上引入专用控制分支，通过选择性梯度流在梯度层面解耦优化目标，将原本难以处理的多任务优化转化为良态问题，实现模拟与控制的联合优化。

Result: 在双量子比特Heisenberg XXX模型上的数值模拟表明：1）框架忠实再现了非马尔可夫动力学特征（退相干和信息回流）；2）在态制备任务中，FPINN的保真度优于梯度上升脉冲工程、截断随机基和标准PINN，且优势在环境更具耗散性和马尔可夫性时更明显；3）生成的脉冲更平滑，有利于实验实现。

Conclusion: 该框架为开放量子系统的模拟与控制提供了一种统一的端到端可微分范式，对量子计算、量子模拟和量子控制具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a pathway to reunify the simulation and control of quantum systems, in which these two tasks are typically decoupled in traditional strategies. However, most work remains confined to Markovian environments. When applied to non-Markovian systems, standard PINN architectures fail to converge reliably due to multi-objective optimization conflicts arising from the coupled differential equations. To address this fundamental limitation, we extend our previously proposed forked PINN (FPINN) by incorporating a dedicated control branch. By decoupling the optimization objectives at the gradient level via selective gradient flow, our method turns a previously intractable multi-task optimization into a well-conditioned one, allowing simulation and control to be optimized jointly without compromise. Numerical simulations on a two-qubit Heisenberg XXX model confirm that our framework faithfully reproduces the features of non-Markovian dynamics, including decoherence and information backflow. Taking a state-preparation task on the same model as an example, our FPINN achieves higher fidelity than gradient ascent pulse engineering, chopped random basis, and standard PINNs, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as the environment becomes more dissipative and more Markovian. The generated pulses are also noticeably smoother, which is advantageous for experimental implementation. Our framework thus provides a unified, end-to-end differentiable paradigm for simulation and control of open quantum systems, with potential implications for quantum computing, simulation, and control.

</details>


### [139] [Activating thermally charged quantum batteries in finite time: Thermodynamic trade-offs between correlations, work, and information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12573)
*Bhaskar Barman,Janine Splettstoesser,Henning Kirchberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时间依赖的搅拌协议，通过辅助激活器与量子电池的时变耦合，将热充电的被动量子电池激活为可提取有用能量的主动状态，并分析了净可提取能量的上限，结合投影测量可进一步增加能量提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热充电量子电池是一种稳定且易获取的能量资源，但其处于被动状态，无法通过幺正操作按需提取有用能量，因此需要一种激活机制使其变得主动可用。

Method: 引入时间依赖的搅拌协议，通过辅助激活器与电池的时变耦合产生关联，从而驱动电池进入主动状态；同时考虑搅拌过程能量成本、关联产生的熵成本及激活时间，推导净可提取能量的边界；结合对激活器的投影测量以增强能量提取。

Result: 给出了净可提取能量（净ergotropy）和功率输出的理论界限，并在波导QED实验设置中（谐振子电池+二能级系统）进行了性能表征。

Conclusion: 该搅拌协议能够有效激活热充电的被动量子电池，且通过测量辅助手段可进一步提升性能，为量子电池的按需能量提取提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Thermally charged quantum batteries provide a stable and easy-to-have energy resource, but remain passive and therefore do not allow useful energy extraction on demand via unitary operations. We introduce a time-dependent stirring protocol that activates a thermally charged quantum battery through a time-dependent coupling to an auxiliary activator, thereby generating correlations that drive the battery into an active state. Accounting explicitly for the energetic cost of the stirring process, the entropic cost of correlation generation and the activation time, we derive bounds on the maximal net extractable energy. Incorporating projective measurement on the activator and exploiting the information gained through the measurement further enhances the extractable energy. As an experimentally relevant example, we analyze a waveguide-QED setup where a harmonic-oscillator battery (waveguide) is stirred and monitored by a two-level system. We characterize the performance of the protocol in terms of net extractable energy (net ergotropy) and power output.

</details>


### [140] [Entropy Transport in Programmable Quantum Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12581)
*Radhika Joshi,Yuli V. Nazarov,Mohammad H. Ansari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了驱动量子比特结中熵传输的量子动力学控制机制，发现双量子比特结在更低驱动功率下实现了更强的熵传输，并揭示了共振驱动下的相干熵流贡献和负微分熵电导这两种非直观效应，为可编程量子熵流控制提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子逻辑架构作为可编程熵传输器件的能力，研究量子动力学（而非单纯的热库参数）如何控制物理熵流，并揭示驱动量子结中非经典的热输运现象。

Method: 对比分析两种简单量子架构：驱动单量子比特结和驱动双量子比特结，通过理论计算比较两者的熵传输效率和驱动功率需求。

Result: 双量子比特结相比单量子比特结显著增强熵传输效率，同时所需驱动功率大幅降低；发现了两种非直观效应：共振驱动下的相干熵流贡献和增加热偏压反而抑制熵流向探测库的负微分熵电导现象。

Conclusion: 量子逻辑架构可作为可编程熵传输器件，为驱动量子电路中的量子反馈控制、库保护和制冷研究提供了方向。

Abstract: We show that driven qubit junctions enable programmable control of physical entropy transport, with entropy conductance governed by quantum dynamics rather than by reservoir parameters alone. By comparing two simple quantum architectures -- a driven single-qubit junction and a driven two-qubit junction -- we find that the two-qubit junction enhances entropy transfer while requiring substantially lower driving power than its single-qubit counterpart. We further reveal two non-intuitive effects in both junctions: a sizable coherent contribution to the entropy current that emerges only under resonant driving, and negative differential entropy conductance, where increasing the thermal bias suppresses entropy flow into the probe reservoir. These results identify quantum logic architectures as programmable devices for entropy transport and suggest routes toward quantum feedback control, reservoir protection and refrigeration in driven quantum circuits.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum tests via inequalities for joint statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12591)
*Alfredo Luis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了违反即表明数据无法用联合概率分布描述的统计不等式，检验了量子准概率分布及噪声联合测量对这些不等式的违反情况，发现最大混合态乃至所有系统态均存在违反，表明这反映了量子力学统计结构本身的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能够判定数据是否可由真实联合概率分布描述的不等式，以揭示量子力学中非经典统计特征的根源。

Method: 推导统计不等式作为共同概率空间失败的见证者；检验量子准概率分布和噪声联合测量下的联合分布是否违反这些不等式。

Result: 最大混合态及所有系统态均违反这些不等式，违反现象不仅局限于特定量子态。

Conclusion: 这些不等式的违反并非单纯系统态的性质，而是量子力学统计结构本身的固有属性。

Abstract: In this work we derive statistical inequalities whose violation is equivalent to the impossibility of describing the data by a genuine joint probability distribution. So they are witnesses of the failure of a common probability space. We examine whether the most significant quantum quasidistributions violate these inequalities. We examine also whether these inequalitites are violated by the joint distributions derived form a noisy joint measurement. As a relevant example we find violations for the maximally mixed state, as well as cases where all system states violate them. This points to the idea that these results are more than a property of the system states, but are instead a property of the statistical structure of quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [142] [Constructing mode-resolved quantum optical models for emitters in photonic crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12603)
*Antonio Morales-Pérez,Iñaki García-Elcano,Chiara Devescovi,Maia G. Vergniory,Aitzol García-Etxarri,Alejandro González-Tudela*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构建量子光学晶格模型的方法，通过结合对称性约束的紧束缚构造与数值计算的能带结构及场分布，在保留光-物质耦合矢量特性的同时降低复杂度，实现了经典电磁模拟与量子多体/非马尔可夫建模之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光子晶体中光-物质相互作用建模存在两难：基于麦克斯韦方程组的电磁方法能提供真实矢量描述但难以整合量子多体和非微扰方法，而简化的量子光学晶格模型虽易处理但通常使用标量且空间无关的耦合，丢失了结构化光子环境的关键特征。

Method: 提出构造性框架：将对称性约束的紧束缚构造与数值计算的光子能带结构和场分布相结合，推导出最小化、对称性约束的晶格哈密顿量，既能重现目标光子色散，又保留模式分辨（位置和偏振依赖）的光-物质耦合结构。

Result: 该模型能在微扰区恢复基于格林函数的发射体动力学，同时提供超越发射体描述的非微扰量子动力学模拟。在二维光子晶体中验证表明，该框架能捕捉标量模型无法获得的偏振依赖定向发射，并支持非马尔可夫光-物质动力学及纠缠分析。

Conclusion: 该框架为经典电磁模拟工具与量子光学多体及非马尔可夫建模之间提供了实用的桥梁。

Abstract: Recent advances are enabling quantum emitters to interact with photonic crystals, whose electromagnetic modes exhibit complex dispersion relations, spatial mode structure, and polarization textures. However, modeling light-matter behavior in these systems faces a persistent trade-off: electromagnetic approaches based on Maxwell-equation solvers provide realistic vectorial descriptions but are difficult to integrate with quantum many-body and non-perturbative methods, whereas simplified quantum-optical lattice models are tractable but typically rely on scalar and spatially independent light-matter couplings that miss essential features of these structured photonic environments. Here, we introduce a constructive framework to derive quantum-optical lattice descriptions that overcome this trade-off. Combining symmetry-constrained tight-binding constructions with numerically computed photonic band structures and field profiles, our method yields minimal, symmetry-enforced lattice Hamiltonians that reproduce the target photonic dispersion while retaining the mode-resolved (position- and polarization-dependent) structure of the light-matter coupling. We show that these models recover Green's-function-based emitter dynamics in the perturbative regime, while providing access to non-perturbative quantum dynamical simulations beyond emitter-only descriptions. As a proof of principle, we apply the framework to a two-dimensional photonic crystal and show that it captures polarization-dependent directional emission inaccessible to scalar models, while enabling the analysis of non-Markovian light-matter dynamics and entanglement. Our results provide a practical bridge between classical electromagnetic simulation tools and quantum-optical many-body and non-Markovian modeling in photonic crystal settings.

</details>


### [143] [James-Stein estimation for quantum sensing schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12651)
*Luke Alexander Rhodes,Sean William Moore,Jacob A. Dunningham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在有限数据量子计量学中，当缺乏未知参数的先验分布时，James-Stein估计器如何能够在测量多个未知参数时提供显著优势，且无需任何先验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子计量协议中，数据分析步骤常被忽视，默认标准数据技术适用。然而在有限数据场景下，数据分析和成本函数选择至关重要，当没有可靠的先验分布时，贝叶斯分析无法直接应用，因此需要探索不依赖先验分布的替代估计方法。

Method: 将James-Stein估计器应用于简单量子计量方案中，用于在有限数据条件下测量多个未知参数，并与传统估计方法进行比较分析。

Result: James-Stein估计器在有限数据、多参数量子计量中表现出显著优势，无需任何先验分布即可获得更好的估计性能。

Conclusion: 在有限数据的量子计量中，当缺乏先验信息时，James-Stein估计器是一种有效的替代方案，能够弥补传统贝叶斯方法的不足，为量子计量协议的数据分析环节提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Quantum metrology protocols typically consist of four steps: state preparation, evolution, measurement, and data processing. Often, the first three steps are prioritised when designing a scheme as they contain all the quantum elements. The data analysis is generally considered an add-on with an implicit assumption that this step is well behaved and so standard data techniques can be applied. However, the situation can be more nuanced, such as when the available data are limited. In limited-data quantum metrology the choice of data analysis technique and cost function of the estimator is of great importance, and a reliable prior distribution of the unknown parameters is required for Bayesian analysis. An interesting question is what we should do when no such prior is available. In this work, we consider how the James-Stein estimator can give significant advantages when measuring multiple unknown parameters with limited data and, importantly, does not require any prior distribution. We demonstrate the advantage by applying this methodology to simple quantum metrology schemes.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum metrology with undetected mid-infrared photons for applied non-destructive testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12653)
*Paul Gattinger,Andreas W. Schell,Maja Buchegger,Markus Brandstetter,Ivan Zorin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于未探测光子量子非线性干涉仪的中红外光谱、显微和光学相干断层扫描技术，展示了其在非破坏性检测中的优势，并与经典系统进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用量子效应和光子关联（纠缠），通过易测量波段（可见/近红外）获取目标波段（中红外）信息，实现超低探针功率、室温操作和散粒噪声极限检测，用于日常非破坏性检测。

Method: 基于自发参量下转换（SPDC）源的量子非线性干涉仪，产生非简并关联信号光子和闲频光子，分别作为探测光子和探针光子，通过测量可见/近红外信号光来反演中红外信息。

Result: 成功应用该技术进行了中红外光谱、中红外显微成像和中红外光学相干断层扫描，并对系统进行了表征和与经典系统的基准对比。

Conclusion: 未探测光子量子技术在中红外波段具有重要应用前景，有望成为基于量子效应的常规非破坏性检测工具，并为该新技术的未来发展提供了展望。

Abstract: Metrology with undetected photons is an emerging technique that leverages quantum effects and photon correlations (entanglement) to retrieve valuable information in a target spectral range (e.g., mid-infrared, mid-IR) using measurements in an easily accessible domain (e.g., visible, near-IR). The underlying quantum process of spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) is utilized to generate non-degenerate correlated signal and idler photons to serve as detection and probing photons, respectively. Sensing with undetected photons enables important advantages, such as ultra-low probe powers, room-temperature operation, and shot-noise-limited detection. In this contribution, we apply a quantum nonlinear interferometer based on an SPDC source to perform applied mid-IR spectroscopy, mid-IR microscopy, and mid-IR optical coherence tomography (OCT) as among the most promising techniques for quantum-based routine non-destructive testing. Moreover, we characterize the system, benchmark it against classical systems, and provide a prospective outlook for this new technology.

</details>


### [145] [Entanglement and Optical Nonreciprocity in spontaneous Raman Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12676)
*Frank Schlawin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了拉曼散射的微观量子理论，揭示了其与纠缠光子产生的联系，并发现了斯托克斯和反斯托克斯边带之间的手性耦合与非互易放大效应，为拉曼量子光子学协议和量子增强拉曼光谱成像提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的研究表明散射光可以展现非经典关联，但拉曼散射的量子关联特性尚未被充分理解，需要建立连接拉曼散射与纠缠光子产生的统一理论框架。

Method: 发展了拉曼散射的微观理论，采用累积量展开方法分析散射边带的纠缠结构，并与传统非共振非线性波混合作对比。

Result: 发现拉曼散射在斯托克斯和反斯托克斯边带之间诱导出手性耦合，导致相干种子场的非互易放大；同时确定了区分拉曼介导关联与传统非线性波混合关联的特征信号。

Conclusion: 建立了拉曼基量子光子学协议的理论框架，为量子增强拉曼光谱和成像技术指明了发展路径。

Abstract: Inelastic light scattering is a central tool for sample characterization and label-free imaging across the physical and life sciences. Recent work has suggested that scattered light can also exhibit nonclassical correlations. Here, we develop a microscopic theory of Raman scattering that connects naturally to established descriptions of entangled-photon generation in three- and four-wave mixing, while capturing essential differences arising from the resonant, dissipative character of Raman scattering. Using a cumulant-expansion approach, we analyze the entanglement structure of the scattered sidebands and identify signatures that distinguish Raman-mediated correlations from those generated in conventional off-resonant nonlinear wave mixing. In particular, we show that Raman scattering induces chiral couplings between Stokes and anti-Stokes sidebands, leading to nonreciprocal amplification of coherent seed fields. These results establish a theoretical framework for Raman-based quantum photonic protocols and suggest routes toward quantum-enhanced Raman spectroscopy and imaging.

</details>


### [146] [Quantum PDE Solvers in Practice: Application-Driven Benchmarking of the Heat Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12688)
*Mahmoud Elkarargy,Abdelaziz Rahwan,Abdelrahman Elsayed,Forat Hatem*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的1D热方程基准测试，比较了11种量子PDE求解器在相同问题实例下的性能，涵盖了从态向量到含噪后端的多种设置，结果显示了各方法在精度、采样效率和噪声鲁棒性方面的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子PDE求解器难以评估，因为已有研究使用不同的离散化、输出模型、重构规则和硬件假设，缺乏统一公平的比较框架。

Method: 构建1D Dirichlet热方程的基准测试，在相同问题实例和读出合约下比较11种量子核方法（HHL、QSVT、QLS-Fourier、VQLS、QITE、var-QITE、AVQDS、Hamiltonian simulation、Schade-Hamiltonian、Schrödingerisation、QSM）。使用3种初始条件、4种网格尺寸（n=4~7量子比特）、CFL比r≈0.4、最终时间T=1，在态向量、理想采样（每步10^5次）和含噪Aer后端三种设置下评估。

Result: 态向量下，QSM和Schade-Hamiltonian达到浮点精度，Schrödingerisation约10^{-4}误差，QITE是平滑数据下最强的非变换方法。固定采样下，HHL退化为约0.79相对ℓ2误差。归一化失配分析显示22-29%的误差来自重构归一化。紧凑可观测量比全场重构所需采样数少1-3个数量级。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为选择量子PDE求解器提供了实用指南，揭示了不同方法在精度、采样效率和噪声鲁棒性方面的权衡。

Abstract: Quantum PDE solvers are difficult to evaluate in practice because published studies use different discretizations, output models, reconstruction rules, and hardware assumptions. We present a reproducible, application-driven benchmark for the 1-D Dirichlet heat equation that compares eleven kernels under the same problem instances and readout contract. The benchmark covers coherent linear solvers (HHL, QSVT, and QLS-Fourier), VQLS, imaginary-time methods (QITE, var-QITE, and AVQDS), real-time Hamiltonian simulation and unitary dilations (Hamiltonian simulation, Schade-Hamiltonian, and Schr"odingerisation), and the spectral quantum simulation method (QSM). We use three initial conditions, four grid sizes from $n=4$ to $7$ qubits ($N=16$ to $128$), a CFL-like ratio $r\approx0.4$, and final time $T=1$. Statevector, ideal-shot ($10^5$ shots per step), and noisy Aer backends separate algorithmic, sampling, and device-noise errors. On statevector, QSM and Schade-Hamiltonian reproduce the semi-discrete reference to floating-point precision, Schr"odingerisation reaches approximately $10^{-4}$ error, and QITE is the strongest non-transform method for smooth data. Under the fixed-shot setting, HHL degrades to approximately $0.79$ relative $\ell_2$ error, while several low-depth or postselected methods become readout-limited. A norm-mismatch ablation attributes 23--29% of the $n=7$ smooth-initial-condition error of Hamiltonian simulation, AVQDS, and QLS-Fourier to reconstruction normalization. Compact observables, including total thermal energy and individual Fourier-mode weights, require 1--3 orders of magnitude fewer shots than full-field reconstruction. The resulting public benchmark provides a practical guide for selecting quantum PDE solvers.

</details>


### [147] [Thermally Activated Long-Range Entanglement from Non-Abelian Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12710)
*Shuai Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在强非阿贝尔守恒律（如SU(2)自旋旋转对称性）约束下，热噪声反而能将局域热涨落转化为无界可操作资源，使自旋链在高温区间仍能产生随系统尺寸对数发散的蒸馏纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知中热噪声会抑制量子纠缠，本文旨在探究非阿贝尔守恒律能否逆转这一趋势，使热涨落成为纠缠资源。

Method: 理论分析有限范围SU(2)不变自旋链全局单态子空间，提出显式表示空间协议推导蒸馏纠缠下界；利用可精确求解的二聚链模型计算有限温度纠缠标度；通过非可积链的精确对角化进行数值验证。

Result: 得到蒸馏纠缠下界Y_N=½log₂N+O_β(1)；二聚链中零温无纠缠而任意T>0产生E_D=½log₂N+C(T)+o(1)，存在T_*~Δ/lnN的交叉标度；非可积链数值结果与预测标度一致。

Conclusion: 加热可在非阿贝尔守恒律限制热化时，激活跨宏观二分系统的尺寸发散蒸馏纠缠，即热噪声成为纠缠放大器。

Abstract: Thermal noise ordinarily suppresses quantum entanglement. We show that a strong non-Abelian conservation law can convert local thermal fluctuations into an unbounded operational resource. For a broad class of finite-range $SU(2)$-invariant spin chains restricted to the global-singlet sector, an explicit representation-space protocol yields $Y_N=\frac12\log_2 N+O_β(1)$, and hence $E_{\mathrm{D}}\geq Y_N$, throughout a finite high-temperature interval. Local thermal fluctuations produce subsystem spins $j\sim\sqrt N$, whose globally locked irreducible representations contain $\log_2(2j+1)\sim\frac12\log_2N$ ebits. An exactly solvable dimer chain exhibits a sharper effect: its zero-temperature state is unentangled across the cut, whereas every fixed $T>0$ produces $E_{\mathrm{D}}=\frac{1}{2}\log_2 N+C(T)+o(1)$, with crossover scale $T_*(N)\simΔ/\ln N$. Exact diagonalization of a nonintegrable chain is consistent with the predicted scaling. Thus heating can activate system-size-diverging distillable entanglement across a macroscopic bipartition when thermalization is confined by a non-Abelian conservation law.

</details>


### [148] [When Close Enough Is Not Enough: Autoregressive Drift in Quantum Circuit Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12780)
*Mehdi Saeedi,Eddie Richter,Paul Hartke*

Main category: quant-ph
