TL;DR: 本文研究了用于容错量子计算中量子电路优化的Transformer模型。在参数化电路（可后处理修正角度）上，混合方法实现了中位数保真度1.000；但在Clifford+T电路（离散门、不可后处理）上，精确等价率随目标长度急剧下降（从<=9门的88%降至>26门的近零）。归因于自回归漂移——从左到右解码中早期令牌发散不可逆级联。推理时多候选生成+等价验证将精确匹配率从7%提升至22.5%，数据扩增2.5倍推至39.5%，但长电路退化依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算中量子电路优化需要在保持精确功能等价的同时最小化昂贵的非Clifford资源（如T门）。作者希望探索Transformer模型能否解决这一具有挑战性的离散优化问题。

Method: 使用44.8M参数的编码器-解码器Transformer，采用结构化电路令牌化，在参数化电路（2-6量子比特）和Clifford+T电路（3-6量子比特）上评估。参数化电路采用混合方法（Transformer提供结构，经典优化调整角度）。

Result: 参数化电路上混合方法实现中位数保真度1.000。Clifford+T电路上模型学习有效语法和准确T门统计，但精确等价率从<=9门的88%降至>26门的近零。推理时搜索提升至22.5%，数据扩增2.5倍提升至39.5%，但长电路退化持续存在。

Conclusion: 自回归漂移限制了Transformer在需要精确离散正确性时的可靠性。近似输出可后处理时Transformer成功，精确等价要求下自回归漂移成为瓶颈。推理时搜索和数据缩放是有效杠杆，而训练侧微调和模型多样化无效。

Abstract: Quantum circuit optimization for fault-tolerant computing requires exact functional equivalence while minimizing expensive non-Clifford resources such as T gates. We study this problem using a compact 44.8M-parameter encoder-decoder transformer with structured circuit tokenization, evaluating on parameterized circuits (2-6 qubits) and Clifford+T circuits (3-6 qubits). On parameterized circuits, a hybrid approach -- structure from the transformer, angles from classical optimization -- achieves median fidelity 1.000 on 3-6 qubit circuits. On Clifford+T circuits, where all gates are discrete and no post-processing is possible, the model learns valid syntax and accurate T-Count statistics, yet exact equivalence degrades sharply with target length -- from 88% on circuits with <=9 gates to near zero beyond 26 gates.
  We trace this failure to autoregressive drift: early-token divergence cascading irrecoverably through left-to-right decoding. Two levers partially mitigate the drift: inference-time strategies that generate multiple candidates and select via equivalence verification raise exact-match rates from 7% to 22.5%, while scaling training data by 2.5x pushes them to 39.5%. Yet the degradation with target length persists -- even with more data, exact equivalence drops from 94% on short circuits to under 4% beyond 26 gates.
  The contrast between settings is our central finding: when approximate outputs can be rescued by post-processing, the transformer succeeds; when exact discrete correctness is required, autoregressive drift limits reliability, with both inference-time search and data scaling as effective levers while training-side fine-tuning and model-level diversification are not.

</details>


### [149] [A Variational Surrogate Approach to Finite-Horizon Quantum Control via Hardware-Efficient Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12802)
*Nahid Binandeh Dehaghani,Rafal Wisniewski,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于硬件高效拟设的变分量子框架，用于有限时域量子控制。该方法将控制目标重构为变分优化问题，通过硬件高效参数化量子电路替代传统时间相关控制场，以终端保真度最小化为目标，数值实验展示了在多比特态传输中的高保真度及可拓展性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子控制方法需要显式合成时间相关控制场或满足哈密顿量可达性约束，这些方法通常依赖于问题特定或物理启发的拟设，缺乏灵活性和对近量子设备的兼容性。本文旨在提出一种不依赖特定问题结构、且易于在近量子设备上实现的通用量子控制框架。

Method: 将有限时域量子控制目标重构为变分优化问题，使用硬件高效参数化量子电路（由单比特旋转门和纠缠门的交替层组成）作为终端演化的替代参数化，通过经典优化算法最小化终端保真度损失来优化电路参数，从而避免显式合成时间相关控制场。

Result: 在多比特态传输基准测试中实现了高保真度态传输，同时揭示了拟设表达力、优化复杂度和系统规模及电路深度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的变分量子控制框架提供了一种灵活、实现友好且与近量子设备兼容的方法，可在不依赖问题特定拟设的情况下实现有效的量子控制，但需在拟设表达力、优化复杂度和可拓展性之间进行权衡。

Abstract: We present a variational quantum framework for finite-horizon quantum control based on hardware-efficient ansätze. The objective is to steer a quantum system from a given initial state to a desired target state over a fixed time horizon by minimizing a terminal cost defined in terms of state fidelity. Instead of explicitly synthesizing time-dependent control fields or enforcing Hamiltonian reachability constraints, the proposed method reformulates the control objective as a variational optimization problem in which a hardware-efficient parameterized quantum circuit provides a surrogate parameterization of the terminal evolution. The circuit consists of alternating layers of single-qubit rotations and entangling gates, whose parameters are optimized using classical routines to minimize the terminal infidelity. This formulation avoids reliance on problem-specific or physics-inspired ansätze, providing a flexible and implementation-friendly approach compatible with near-term quantum devices. Numerical experiments on multi-qubit state-transfer benchmarks demonstrate high-fidelity state transfer while highlighting the trade-off between ansatz expressivity, optimization complexity, and scalability with respect to system size and circuit depth.

</details>


### [150] [The Resolution of the Identity as a Generator of Exact Integral Identities: A Coherent-State Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12822)
*Ghasem Asadi Cordshooli,Mehdi Mirzaee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新诠释了恒等分解，将Glauber相干态的完备性关系作为投影算子，系统推导出复高斯积分恒等式、离散Kronecker delta与连续Dirac delta局域化核，揭示了过完备与严格正交表示的结构差异，并指出了主恒等式中参数极限的非对易行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子力学教科书将恒等分解仅视为被动表示工具，本文旨在扭转这一视角，论证相干态完备性关系可直接作为精确复高斯积分恒等式的生成元，从而揭示希尔伯特空间完备性内蕴大量可解数学关系的统一图景。

Method: 将连续相干态完备性关系系统地投影到离散Fock基上，利用态守恒原理自然导出广义高斯积分关系；通过不同基投影分别得到离散Kronecker delta和连续Dirac delta局域化核，对比过完备与正交表示的差异；分析主恒等式中参数极限的非对易行为。

Result: 成功从相干态完备性关系出发，系统生成了高度推广的高斯积分恒等式；揭示了过完备表示（相干态）与严格正交表示（Fock基）在局域化核结构上的根本差异；发现了主恒等式中参数取极限时的非对易现象。

Conclusion: 该框架以透明直观的方式展示了希尔伯特空间完备性如何编码大量精确可解数学关系，仅需相干态基本性质和Dirac形式体系即可理解，为高级本科生和研究生教学提供了统一的视角。

Abstract: The resolution of the identity is typically utilized as a passive representation tool in standard quantum mechanics textbooks. In this paper, we reinterpret this traditional perspective by introducing the resolution of the identity associated with Glauber coherent states as a direct generator of exact complex Gaussian integral identities. By systematically projecting the continuous coherent-state completeness relation onto the discrete Fock basis, highly generalized Gaussian integral relations emerge naturally as a direct consequence of state preservation, rather than being introduced as independent mathematical postulates. We demonstrate that this overarching formal structure simultaneously dictates both discrete (Kronecker delta) and continuous (Dirac delta) localization kernels under appropriate basis projections, providing a sharp conceptual contrast between the structural features of overcomplete and strictly orthogonal representations. Crucially, we highlight an intriguing non-commuting behavior in the parameter limits of the master identity. Requiring only the elementary properties of coherent states and the Dirac formalism, this approach offers a transparent and visually intuitive illustration of how Hilbert-space completeness inherently encodes vast libraries of exact, solvable mathematical relations -- providing a unifying perspective suitable for advanced undergraduate and graduate instruction.

</details>


### [151] [Detecting Phishing in Ethereum Networks using Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12828)
*Sai Sakunthala Guddanti,Anupama Ray,Mrunal Arun Kumavat,Anil Prabhakar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出混合量子-经典集成框架，使用QSVM和VQC检测以太坊交易网络中的钓鱼攻击。QRAC编码相比ZZ特征图在QSVM和VQC上分别提升3%和13%，并在IBM Heron量子处理器上接近模拟器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QML方法在金融异常检测中虽优于统计方法，但落后于先进深度学习技术，因此需要弥合这一差距，发挥量子与经典计算的协同优势。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典集成框架，结合QSVM和VQC；采用级联QRAC编码方案与ZZ特征图对比；在模拟器和IBM Heron量子处理器上实验。

Result: QRAC编码相比ZZ特征图在VQC上提升13%，在QSVM上提升3%；集成框架有效提升检测效果；QSVM在假阴性率和召回率上表现最优；部分QML算法在IBM Heron上接近模拟器性能。

Conclusion: 混合量子-经典集成框架结合QRAC编码能有效提升金融异常检测性能，QML在量子硬件上展现出韧性，尽管存在硬件限制仍具应用潜力。

Abstract: This article explores the potential of Quantum Machine Learning (QML), specifically assessing a Quantum Support Vector Machine (QSVM) and a Variational Quantum Classifier (VQC) for detecting anomalies in real-world financial transaction data. While these QML methods outperform statistical methods, they fall short of cutting-edge deep learning techniques. To bridge this gap, we propose a hybrid quantum-classical ensemble framework that leverages the strengths of both domains. We demonstrate its effectiveness in detecting phishing in Ethereum transaction networks by combining complementary algorithms. The QSVM, whether used individually or in an ensemble, consistently delivered the lowest false negatives and higher recall rates, that are crucial for anomaly detection. To enhance individual models, we encoded the data using novel cascaded Quantum Random Access Coding (QRAC) schemes and compared it with the popular encoding ZZ feature map on both simulators and the IBM Heron quantum processor. For both QSVM and VQC, we consistently observed improvements (13% for QRAC-VQC and 3% for QRAC-QSVM) of QRAC over the ZZ feature map. Notably, certain QML algorithms exhibit remarkable resilience on the IBM Heron quantum processor, approaching simulator-level performance on devices with high quantum volume. This observation underscores the promise of QML despite hardware limitations.

</details>


### [152] [End-to-End Quantum Key Distribution Across Hybrid Fiber and Free-Space Links with All-Optical Encoding Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12837)
*Khen Cohen,Tomer Nahum,Michael Tzukran,Paz Or,Yehuda Pilnyak,Nitzan Livneh,Hagai Eisenberg,Yaron Oz,Haim Suchowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文演示了一种完整的光纤-自由空间混合量子密钥分发（QKD）链路，在单一端到端协议中桥接两种介质，通过全光学域的时隙-偏振编码转换，在90米户外自由空间链路上实现持续安全密钥生成，量子误码率（5.6-6.8%）远低于BB84安全阈值（11%），并验证了750米扩展链路的光子级操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来量子网络需要连接光纤（时隙编码）和自由空间（偏振编码）链路，但两者采用不同的光子编码方式，现有方案缺乏能够在单一协议内桥接两种介质且不破坏安全假设的实用接口。

Method: 采用诱骗态BB84协议（1550 nm），设计全光学域时隙-偏振（T2P）和偏振-时隙（P2T）转换器，无需测量或状态重建，转换器作为不可信量子信道的一部分；在90米户外自由空间链路上测试，覆盖强白天湍流到夜间稳定条件（折射率结构参数Cn^2跨越两个数量级），并验证750米扩展链路。

Result: 系统在所有大气条件下维持会话平均QBER为5.6-6.8%（低于11%安全阈值），实现持续安全密钥生成；全光学编码转换不破坏BB84协议安全假设；验证了从90米到750米自由空间链路的光子级操作可行性。

Conclusion: 全光学域安全光子编码转换可作为光纤与自由空间量子通信平台之间的实用接口，为未来量子网络应用提供了基础构建模块。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) promises information-theoretically secure communication, but future networks must bridge fiber and free-space links that naturally employ different photonic encodings, namely time-bin in fiber and polarization in free space. Here we demonstrate a complete hybrid fiber and free-space QKD link that bridges both media within a single end-to-end protocol, converting between the two encodings entirely in the optical domain. Using the decoy-state BB84 protocol operating at 1550 nm, we demonstrate continuous secure-key generation over a 90 m outdoor free-space link. The system operates across atmospheric conditions spanning more than two orders of magnitude in the refractive-index structure parameter Cn^2, from strong daytime turbulence to quiescent nighttime conditions, and we further validate photon-level operation over a 750 m free-space extension. Throughout, the link maintains a session-mean quantum bit error rate (QBER) of 5.6-6.8%, well below the 11% BB84 security threshold. The encoding conversion is performed entirely in the optical domain without measurement or state reconstruction, preserving the security assumptions of the BB84 protocol. Consequently, the time-bin-to-polarization (T2P) and polarization-to-time-bin (P2T) converters remain part of the untrusted quantum channel rather than trusted intermediate nodes. These results establish secure photonic encoding conversion as a practical interface between fiber and free-space quantum communication platforms, providing a building block for future quantum networks applications.

</details>


### [153] [Thermal Suppression of Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions in Finite-Dimensional Systems A Quasi-Hermitian Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12838)
*Jia-Chen Tang,Xu-Yang Hou,Ming-Zhang Wang,Hao Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在伪厄米框架下从第一性原理构建了混合态Loschmidt振幅，研究了有限维系统中热平衡态经突然淬火后的动力学量子相变（DQPTs）。通过一个N能级模型发现，温度通过热权重在能级间的重新分布控制DQPTs，当希尔伯特空间维度达到5时出现有限阈值温度，超过该温度后Loschmidt振幅失去所有实零点，DQPTs被完全抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对有限温度下DQPTs的理解尚不充分，缺乏从第一性原理出发的自洽理论框架来刻画混合态淬火动力学，特别是热效应对DQPTs的调控机制尚不清晰。

Method: 采用度规-平稳伪厄米框架，从第一性原理构造混合态Loschmidt振幅；建立包含两能级扇区与N-2个旁观态的N能级模型，通过热权重分布分析温度对DQPTs的调控作用。

Result: 温度通过热权重在能级间的重新分布控制DQPTs；当希尔伯特空间维度达到5时出现有限阈值温度，超过该温度后Loschmidt振幅失去所有实零点，DQPTs被完全抑制。

Conclusion: 热抑制机制为通过热占据控制动力学临界性提供了普适原理，准厄米框架为其严格推导提供了自洽基础。

Abstract: We investigate dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) in finite-dimensional systems prepared in thermal equilibrium states and subjected to a sudden quench. A mixed-state Loschmidt amplitude is constructed from first principles within a metric-stationary pseudo-Hermitian framework, providing a self-contained derivation of the finite-temperature quench dynamics. Applying this framework to an $N$-level model consisting of a two-level sector coupled to $N-2$ spectator states, we find that temperature controls the DQPTs through the redistribution of thermal weights among the eigenstates. This mechanism leads to a dimensionality-dependent threshold temperature that becomes finite when the Hilbert-space dimension reaches five, above which the Loschmidt amplitude loses all real zeros and the DQPTs are fully suppressed. The thermal suppression mechanism suggests a general principle for controlling dynamical criticality through thermal occupation, while the quasi-Hermitian framework provides the self-consistent foundation for its rigorous derivation.

</details>


### [154] [Time-independent counterdiabatic driving for emergent two-level subspaces in many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12848)
*S. Dengis,P. Schlagheck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明黎曼流形上的匀速测地线运动可直接导出时间无关的反绝热驱动，通过量子度量张量与反绝热哈密顿量的关系，使得反绝热哈密顿量的Hilbert-Schmidt范数恒定，进而对有效二能级系统实现全反绝热哈密顿量的时间无关性。在LZ模型、STIRAP和里德伯阻塞系统中验证了该方法，并讨论了多体系统中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统绝热协议需要时间依赖的辅助控制场，实现复杂；寻找一种能够通过固定振幅场实现高效量子态制备的方法，即时间无关的反绝热驱动。

Method: 利用量子态黎曼流形上的匀速测地线运动，结合量子度量张量与反绝热哈密顿量的关系，证明匀速测地线使反绝热哈密顿量的Hilbert-Schmidt范数恒定；对于有效二能级系统，进一步证明反绝热修正具有固定算子方向时，全反绝热哈密顿量时间无关。

Result: 在Landau-Zener模型、三能级STIRAP和集体驱动里德伯阻塞系统中实现了时间无关的反绝热驱动，在远短于传统绝热协议的时间尺度上实现了单位保真度态制备；同时指出多体系统中两能级约化仅为涌现性，有效子空间外的泄露限制了加速上限。

Conclusion: 时间无关反绝热驱动可在保持单位保真度的同时大幅缩短态制备时间，并将时间整形的辅助控制替换为固定振幅场，为实验实现提供了更简洁的方案，但在多体系统中存在加速上限的限制。

Abstract: We show that geodesic motion in the Riemannian manifold of quantum states provides a direct route to time-independent counterdiabatic driving. Using the relation between the counterdiabatic Hamiltonian and the quantum metric tensor, we prove that a constant-speed geodesic makes the Hilbert-Schmidt norm of the counterdiabatic Hamiltonian constant. For effective two-level systems whose counterdiabatic correction has a fixed operator direction, this further implies that the full counterdiabatic Hamiltonian itself is time independent. We illustrate this result with the Landau-Zener model, three-level Stimulated Raman adiabatic passage and a collectively driven Rydberg ensemble in the blockade regime. Limitations of this approach in realistic many-body systems are discussed, where the two-level reduction is only emergent and leakage out of the effective subspace bounds the achievable speedup. In all cases, time-independent counterdiabatic driving achieves unit-fidelity state preparation on timescales substantially shorter than conventional adiabatic protocols while replacing temporally shaped auxiliary controls by fixed-amplitude fields.

</details>


### [155] [Unitary Synthesis with Near-Optimal T-Count for Near-Clifford Unitaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12907)
*Wang Fang,Chris Heunen,Qisheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种通用酉合成方法，将任意n量子比特酉算子U实现为Clifford+T电路，其中T-count为$\widetilde{O}(2^n d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U))$，当$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$为常数时接近最优，显著改进了Tan (2025)的$\widetilde{O}(2^{4n/3})$上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有酉合成方法的T-count上界（如Tan的$\widetilde{O}(2^{4n/3})$）对大量酉算子不够紧，希望利用酉算子与Clifford群的距离来获得更紧的、与距离相关的上界。

Method: 基于酉算子U到Clifford群的Frobenius范数距离$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$，设计了一种合成方案，将U分解为Clifford+T电路，T-count与$2^n d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$成正比。

Result: 实现了T-count $\widetilde{O}(2^n d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U))$，当$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$为常数时近最优；对于$d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U) \ll 2^{n/3}$的酉算子，改进了Tan (2025)的$\widetilde{O}(2^{4n/3})$上界。

Conclusion: 该酉合成方法在T-count方面具有距离自适应性，对于接近Clifford群的酉算子效率显著提升，且在最坏情况下仍具有竞争力。

Abstract: We present an approach to unitary synthesis that implements an arbitrary $n$-qubit unitary operator $U$ by a Clifford+T circuit with T-count $\widetilde{O}(2^n d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U))$, where $d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$ is the Frobenius norm distance of $U$ to the Clifford group. The T-count is shown to be near-optimal when $d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U)$ is a constant. Our approach improves the previous best upper bound $\widetilde{O}(2^{4n/3})$ due to Tan (2025) for a large class of unitary operators $U$ as long as $d_F^{\mathcal{C}}(U) \ll 2^{n/3}$.

</details>


### [156] [Heisenberg-limited metrology in the presence of non-Markovian noise with finite control rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12913)
*Shravan Shravan,Tyler G. Thurtell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了非马尔可夫噪声下海森堡极限频率估计协议与量子芝诺效应之间的直接联系，将框架扩展到无限维环境，并严格分析了基于测量、主动恢复和动态去耦三类协议在有限控制频率下的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究表明利用无限快控制（如量子纠错）可以在非马尔可夫噪声下恢复海森堡极限频率估计，但这些控制与处理马尔可夫噪声的协议有本质差异，其底层物理机制尚不清晰。本文旨在阐明这些协议与量子芝诺效应的联系，并系统分析有限控制频率下各类协议的性能。

Method: 本文首先建立了非马尔可夫噪声下频率估计协议与量子芝诺效应之间的直接联系，并将框架从有限维环境扩展到无限维环境。随后界定了三类协议：纯测量协议、测量后主动恢复协议以及动态去耦协议，并在控制频率有限的条件下对每类协议的性能进行了严格分析。

Result: 纯测量协议可在主动恢复不可能实现的噪声模型中发挥作用，但其量子Fisher信息对控制频率的依赖呈二次方更差的关系。动态去耦协议在纯测量协议可实现的条件下总是可行的，且其量子Fisher信息对控制频率的依赖与主动恢复协议相同。数值模拟表明，动态去耦协议在微扰理论适用范围之外仍可能展现优势。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了非马尔可夫噪声下量子频率估计协议与量子芝诺效应的深层联系，为理解不同控制策略的性能权衡提供了统一框架，并为实际量子计量学中的协议设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recently, it has been shown that protocols utilizing infinitely fast controls, such as quantum error correction, can in principle restore Heisenberg-limited frequency estimation in the presence of a broad class of non-Markovian noise models arising from coupling to finite-dimensional environments. However, these controls differ substantially from those used to address Markovian noise, and their underlying physical mechanism remains unclear. In this work, we establish a direct connection between these protocols and the quantum Zeno effect, and extend the framework to infinite-dimensional environments. We delineate three types of constructions: (a) protocols that rely solely on measurements, (b) protocols using an active recovery after measurements and (c) protocols using dynamical decoupling and rigorously analyze the performance of each when controls can be applied at only a finite rate. While protocols relying purely on measurements can be engineered for noise models where active recoveries are fundamentally impossible, they result in the quantum Fisher information exhibiting a quadratically worse dependence on the control frequency. Surprisingly, while dynamical decoupling protocols are possible whenever protocols relying only on measurements can be engineered, the quantum Fisher information has the same dependence on control frequency as the active recovery protocols. Numerical simulations suggest that the improvement offered by dynamical decoupling may work in regimes beyond the perturbative setting where our rigorous theorems apply.

</details>


### [157] [Ancilla-Depth Phase Diagrams for Quantum Reference-Frame Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12947)
*Maxim V. Churilov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了带噪声的量子参考系作为统计实验的比较问题，发现有限维信道A和B（A可逆）之间所有带r维辅助测量的精确模拟等价于因子Γ=BA⁻¹的r-正性。通过对去极化信道的详细分析，给出了不同辅助维数下的相位边界和物理转换代价的封闭公式，并展示了该分离现象不仅限于去极化因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带噪声量子参考系作为统计实验的比较问题，特别是决策过程中可用的辅助记忆维度对比较结果的影响。

Method: 将两个有限维信道A和B（A可逆）之间带r维辅助测量的精确模拟等价为因子Γ=BA⁻¹的r-正性；通过去极化信道对D_a和D_b分析相位边界；推导受限水平r缺陷和物理后处理距离的封闭公式；构建转置-去极化结构展示分离的一般性。

Result: 对于去极化源和目标信道D_a和D_b，相位边界为D_a ⪰_r D_b ⟺ -1/(dr-1) ≤ b/a ≤ 1（a≠0）；物理转换代价为δ_phys(D_b|D_a)=(1-1/d²)dist(b, I_a)，其中I_a=conv{a, -a/(d²-1)}；所有k级测试都无法检测的最大物理转换成本为(d-k)/[d(d²-1)]。

Conclusion: 区分了受辅助限制的统计模拟与通过单个量子信道的实现之间的差异，并量化了这种差异。

Abstract: Comparing two noisy quantum reference frames as statistical experiments depends on the dimension of the ancillary memory available to the decision procedure. For finite-dimensional channels A and B with invertible A, we show that exact simulation of all measurements assisted by an r-dimensional ancilla is equivalent to r-positivity of the unique factor Gamma = BA^{-1}. The hierarchy can be realized by physical channel pairs: every unital, trace-preserving map that is k-positive but not (k+1)-positive embeds as the factor between the channels D_a and Gamma composed with D_a on an exact interval determined by the smallest Choi eigenvalue. For depolarizing source and target channels D_a and D_b, including negative and singular source parameters, the phase boundary is $\mathcal{D}_a \succeq_r \mathcal{D}_b \Longleftrightarrow -1/(dr-1) \leq b/a \leq 1$ for $a\neq 0$. We derive closed formulas for the restricted level-r deficiency and for the distance to every physical post-processing, $δ_{\mathrm{phys}}(\mathcal{D}_b\mid\mathcal{D}_a)=(1-1/d^2)\operatorname{dist}(b,I_a)$, where $I_a=\operatorname{conv}\{a,-a/(d^2-1)\}$. The largest physical conversion cost hidden from all tests through level k is $(d-k)/[d(d^2-1)]$. An untouched m-level spectator changes the first detecting external level from k+1 to $\lfloor k/m\rfloor+1$. A transpose--depolarizing construction shows that the separation is not confined to depolarizing factors. The results quantify the distinction between ancilla-restricted statistical simulation and implementation by a single quantum channel.

</details>


### [158] [Logical Entangling with Phantom Codes in Hypergraph Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12948)
*Keming He,Ziao Tang,Zetong Li,Ge Bai,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了幻影码（Phantom codes）在二元CSS超图乘积（HGP）码中的可行性，证明单纯形-重复码族是唯一满足幻影条件的HGP族，并在电路级噪声下展示了其在逻辑GHZ态制备和Trotterized多体量子模拟中的优势，特别适用于可重构中性原子阵列架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑纠缠门是容错量子计算中物理时空开销的主要来源。幻影码通过物理量子比特置换和泡利帧更新实现块内逻辑CNOT门，从而降低开销。关键在于这种机制能否与qLDPC码的低权重稳定子结构共存，这是实现低开销容错架构的核心问题。

Method: 在二元CSS超图乘积码框架内，通过严格数学分析确定满足幻影条件的码族；通过电路级噪声仿真评估该码族在逻辑GHZ态制备和Trotterized多体量子模拟中的性能，并与旋转表面码基线比较。

Result: 1）在自然等价意义下，单纯形-重复码族是唯一满足幻影条件的HGP码族；2）该码族保持低权重稳定子检查；3）在逻辑GHZ态制备和Trotterized多体量子模拟两个基准测试中，该码族相比旋转表面码基线具有具体优势。

Conclusion: 研究精确刻画了基于置换的逻辑纠缠门在HGP框架内对码设计的约束条件，展示了唯一可行码族的电路级优势，并为在可重构中性原子硬件上寻找具有更好渐近参数的幻影qLDPC码族以降低容错开销提供了指导。

Abstract: Logical entangling gates are a major source of physical spacetime overhead in fault-tolerant quantum computation. Phantom codes reduce this cost by implementing every ordered in-block logical CNOT through physical qubit permutations and Pauli-frame updates. Whether this mechanism can coexist with the low-weight stabilizer structure of qLDPC codes is a central question for low-overhead fault-tolerant architectures. We give a deterministic answer within binary CSS hypergraph product (HGP) codes. Up to natural equivalences, the simplex-repetition family is the unique HGP family satisfying the phantom condition. We then evaluate this family under circuit-level noise in logical GHZ-state preparation and Trotterized many-body quantum simulation. The codes retain low-weight stabilizer checks and yield concrete advantages over rotated surface-code baselines in both benchmarks. Reconfigurable neutral-atom arrays offer a natural setting for this approach, supporting nonlocal qLDPC operations while enabling in-block logical CNOTs without additional physical operations. Together, these results make precise how permutation-based logical entangling constrains code design within the HGP framework, demonstrate the circuit-level benefits of the unique family, and guide the search for phantom qLDPC families with better asymptotic parameters for low-overhead fault tolerance on neutral-atom hardware.

</details>


### [159] [Nonreciprocal Quantum Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12966)
*Wei-Bin Yan,Ying-Jie Zhang,Yun-Jie Xia,Heng Fan,Zhong-Xiao Man*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种非互易量子姆潘巴效应：在对称耦合两个同构储层的开放量子系统中，交换两储层参数（swap操作）可开关量子姆潘巴效应而不改变初始态。该非互易性由本征向量承载，而本征值保持不变。在Liouvillian例外点处，远态的弛豫从绕过最慢模式转变为避开临界慢化，开关对比度保持完整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子姆潘巴效应中的非互易性，即交换储层参数能否在不改变初始态的情况下控制该效应的开启与关闭，并揭示其背后的谱无关机制。

Method: 考虑一类广泛的开路量子系统，每个系统通过对称端口耦合到两个同构储层。通过交换两个储层的参数（称为swap操作）来改变Liouvillian算符，分析本征值和本征向量的变换规律，研究远态在最慢弛豫模式上的投影变化，并在Liouvillian例外点处检验该机制。

Result: Swap操作旋转了本征向量但固定了本征值，非互易性完全由本征向量承载。该操作改变了远态在最慢模式上的投影，从而切换是否绕过最慢弛豫通道。在Liouvillian例外点处，远态的弛豫从绕过最慢模式转变为避开临界慢化，且开关对比度保持不变。

Conclusion: 发现了非互易量子姆潘巴效应，其机制独立于谱，完全由本征向量决定，在Liouvillian例外点处展现出最纯粹的形式。

Abstract: We demonstrate a nonreciprocal quantum Mpemba effect. Consider a broad class of open quantum systems, each coupled to two isomorphic reservoirs through symmetric ports. Interchanging the parameters of the two reservoirs -- a discrete operation we call the swap -- turns the quantum Mpemba effect on or off without changing the initial states. The swap modifies the Liouvillian, yet a structural symmetry pins the eigenvalues while rotating only the eigenvectors. The nonreciprocity therefore leaves no trace in the spectrum and is carried entirely by the eigenvectors. Concretely, the swap alters the far state's projection onto the slowest mode, switching whether it bypasses the slowest relaxation channel. At a Liouvillian exceptional point, the far state's relaxation switches from bypassing the slowest mode to avoiding the critical slowing, with the on--off contrast intact. There the spectrum-independent mechanism takes its purest form.

</details>


### [160] [Maximal Classicalization of Finite-Group Quantum Reference-Frame Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12968)
*Maxim V. Churilov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于有限群G的酉表示U，表示完备性（包含所有不可约类型）是将量子后处理简化为经典卷积的精确条件，给出了最小辅助维度、条件期望距离的确切表达式，并揭示了量子-经典缺陷差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限量子参考系中群值失配诱导的随机酉信道，探讨量子后处理能否简化为经典卷积运算，以及何时量子后处理优于经典后处理。

Method: 通过等价性证明建立表示完备性与量子-经典约化等价的条件；使用Plancherel维度刻画条件期望距离；通过有限线性规划和决策见证表征律可识别性。

Result: 表示完备性是量子后处理简化为经典卷积的充要条件；给出了正交轨道和不变校准种子的最小辅助维度；对于不完备载体，得到了条件期望距离的精确表达式；对于不可约载体，给出了闭式缺陷；S3的忠实二维表示展示了相对于经典卷积10%的精确降级。

Conclusion: 表示完备性精确刻画了量子后处理可简化为经典卷积的场景，揭示了量子-经典之间的根本性差距，并为有限和无限紧群提供了系统性的分析框架。

Abstract: A finite quantum reference token with group-valued misalignment induces a random-unitary channel, but optimal degradation is generally an optimization over all quantum post-processings. For a unitary representation U of a finite group G, we prove that the following conditions are equivalent: U contains every irreducible type; one ancilla-assisted input has an orthonormal G-orbit; signed group measures embed isometrically into channels in diamond norm; and, for every pair of noise laws p,q, $\inf_{Λ\in\mathrm{CPTP}} \frac{1}{2}\|Φ_q^U-Λ\circΦ_p^U\|_\diamond =\min_{r\in\mathcal{P}(G)}\frac{1}{2}\|q-r*p\|_1$. Thus representation completeness is the exact carrier condition for universal reduction of quantum post-processing to classical convolution. We determine the minimum ancilla dimensions for an orthogonal orbit and for an invariant calibration seed. For an incomplete carrier, with visible Plancherel dimension S(U), we derive the exact conditional-expectation distance $\frac{1}{2}\|\operatorname{id}-Φ_u^U\|_\diamond=1-1/S(U)$ and an explicit quantum--classical deficiency gap. For irreducible carriers the deficiency is obtained in closed form; the faithful two-dimensional representation of $S_3$ yields an exact ten-percent reduction relative to classical convolution. We also characterize law identifiability through the conjugation representation, provide finite linear programs and decision witnesses, and establish both a finite-dimensional obstruction and stable visible-band reconstruction for infinite compact groups. Deterministic ancillary code reproduces the finite-group examples and numerical regression checks.

</details>


### [161] [An Agentic Formalization for Certified Quantum Neural Network Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12981)
*Mingrui Jing,Lei Zhang,Yusheng Zhao,Hongshun Yao,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Lean 4证明助手中形式化了量子神经网络(QNN)的核心理论，包括单量子比特QNN的精确刻画、量子相位处理定理、过参数化上界、直接和损失方差定律等，并发现八处非正式论证中的错误/模糊点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子神经网络的设计需要平衡表达能力和可训练性，但现有理论（QSP表达性分析和DLA可训练性分析）缺乏统一的、机器可验证的严格形式化基础，非正式论证中可能存在未被发现的错误。

Method: 在Lean 4证明助手中构建连接的表达性与可训练性形式化框架：证明单比特QNN的充要刻画、带资源计数的量子相位处理定理、用DLA维数界定量子Fisher信息秩的过参数化上界；通过去圆化二阶矩接口推导直接和损失方差定律；用参数化Casimir唯一性引擎处理可控、正交、matchgate族；单比特和乘积-Clifford系综闭合二设计假设。

Result: 建立了机器可验证的QNN理论形式化基础，发现并修正了八处非正式论证中未明确的错误或需澄清之处；证明了条件方差定律与DLA坐标下精确损失重构的冠冕定理。

Conclusion: 该工作为QNN理论提供了机器可验证的数学基础，是迈向AI辅助或自动化量子机器学习算法设计的重要一步。

Abstract: A central model in quantum machine learning is the quantum neural network (QNN), whose design requires balancing expressivity and trainability. Technically, expressivity is studied through circuit-function analysis, such as quantum signal processing, while trainability is analyzed using dynamical-Lie-algebra (DLA) methods. To support certified QNN design, we formalize these major components of QNN theory in a connected lean 4 development checked by a proof kernel, where every analytic input is either proved or exposed as a named hypothesis. On the expressivity side, we prove exact if-and-only-if characterizations of single-qubit QNNs, a resource-counted quantum phase processing theorem, and an overparameterization ceiling that bounds the quantum Fisher information rank by the DLA dimension. On the trainability side, we derive the direct-sum loss-variance law through a de-circularized second-moment interface. A parameterized Casimir-uniqueness engine discharges the required inputs for fully controllable, orthogonal, and matchgate circuit families, while single-qubit and product-Clifford ensembles close the two-design assumptions directly. A capstone theorem pairs the conditional variance law with exact loss reconstruction in DLA coordinates. The development record identifies eight corrections and clarifications that were not explicit in the informal arguments. We expect this work to provide a machine-checkable foundation for QNN theory and a step toward AI-assisted or automated design of quantum machine learning algorithms.

</details>


### [162] [The log log jam in Gaussian state tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12983)
*Sitan Chen,Weiyuan Gong,Qi Ye,Zhihan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续变量量子态层析中能量对样本复杂度的根本性限制，证明高斯测量必然导致loglogE依赖，而非高斯纠缠测量可消除该依赖，并给出了最优协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量系统的量子态层析在无物理约束下样本复杂度可能无界，即使是高斯态（具有有限维描述）也存在loglogE的样本复杂度依赖。此前文献未能确定此依赖是否为基本限制，本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: (1) 证明任何使用高斯测量（包括纠缠或自适应）的协议必然有loglogE依赖；(2) 给出自适应轮数与能量依赖之间的平滑权衡及匹配协议；(3) 利用高度纠缠的非高斯测量，以O(n²/ε²)样本学习n模纯高斯态，与E无关；(4) 基于Holevo-Helstrom单副本正则相位POVM的简单协议以O(1/ε²)样本学习单模纯高斯态，也与E无关。

Result: (1) 高斯测量存在根本性的loglogE依赖，回答了多个前期工作的开放问题；(2) 自适应与能量依赖存在平滑权衡；(3) 非高斯纠缠测量可完全消除能量依赖；(4) 单副本正则相位POVM可高效学习单模纯高斯态，无需能量依赖。

Conclusion: 本文阐明了能量在玻色子态层析中的核心作用，揭示了自适应、纠缠和魔法（非高斯性）在量子学习中的相互作用机制。

Abstract: Unlike in finite dimensions, quantum information in continuous-variable systems has the peculiar feature that without imposing physical constraints, the sample complexity of state tomography can be unbounded. Remarkably, this is even the case for state-of-the-art protocols for learning Gaussian states, which have finite-dimensional descriptions: the best known rates scale with $\log \log E$, where $E$ is the energy of the system. We prove this is not an artifact of existing analyses, but a fundamental limitation of the measurements used. We show: (1) Any protocol that uses Gaussian measurements, even entangled or adaptively chosen ones, must incur a $\log \log E$ dependence. This answers an open question posed by a number of previous works. (2) There is a smooth tradeoff between the number of rounds of adaptivity and the energy dependence, and we give a matching protocol achieving this interpolated rate. (3) With highly entangled, non-Gaussian measurements, one can learn $n$-mode pure Gaussian states with $O(n^2 / ε^2)$ samples, independent of $E$. This answers an open question posed by Chen et al. (4) A simple protocol based on the single-copy canonical phase POVM of Holevo and Helstrom learns single-mode pure Gaussian states with $O(1/ε^2)$ samples, again independent of $E$.
  Our results clarify the role of energy in bosonic state tomography and shed new light on the intriguing interplay between adaptivity, entanglement, and magic in quantum learning.

</details>


### [163] [A Noise-Aware Quantum Algorithm for Credit Valuation Adjustments on Real Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12990)
*Guillem Borràs Espert,Francisco Gómez Casanova,Luis de Pedro Sánchez,Senaida Hernández Santana,Pablo Serrano Molinero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的噪声感知量子工作流，用于信用估值调整（CVA）计算，引入了对比感知贝叶斯迭代量子幅值估计（CABIQAE）方法，在含噪量子硬件上相比噪声无关和噪声感知基线方法实现了更有效的幅值放大和更低的经典后处理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVA需要重复进行风险中性期望估计，是量子幅值估计的理想测试场景，但真实金融编码和含噪硬件下量子优势能否保持尚不明确。

Method: 构建了包含市场校准、离散化、Oracle构建、硬件执行和误差预算分析的端到端量子工作流；采用QCBM生成联合时间-市场分布，结合受控支付旋转编码；提出CABIQAE方法，将实验校准的Grover对比度损失融入贝叶斯推断和电路深度选择。

Result: CABIQAE在有限放大能力的当前设备上优于噪声无关基线方法，且经典后处理运行时间显著低于噪声感知BAE基线；完整CVA Oracle仍受限于电路深度和离散化分辨率。

Conclusion: 噪声感知的量子幅值估计算法在含噪硬件上能更有效利用有限量子资源，但完整CVA计算在当前设备上仍面临电路深度和精度瓶颈。

Abstract: Credit Valuation Adjustment (CVA) requires repeated risk-neutral expectation estimation, making it a natural test bed for quantum amplitude estimation, whose coherent amplification can in principle reduce Monte Carlo sampling cost. Whether this advantage survives realistic financial encoding and noisy hardware remains open. We develop an end-to-end, noise-aware quantum workflow for CVA, covering market calibration, discretisation, oracle construction, hardware execution and error-budget analysis. The model combines a correlated two-asset exposure with discount and default factors, encoded through a QCBM-based joint time-market distribution and controlled payoff rotations. We introduce contrast-aware Bayesian iterative quantum amplitude estimation (CABIQAE), which incorporates experimentally calibrated Grover-contrast loss into Bayesian inference and circuit-depth selection. Hardware-calibrated experiments show that CABIQAE exploits the limited amplification available on current devices more effectively than noise-agnostic alternatives and achieves a much lower classical post-processing runtime than the noise-aware BAE baseline. The analysis further decomposes the total CVA error into statistical, encoding, discretisation and hardware contributions. The full CVA oracle remains limited by circuit depth and discretisation resolution.

</details>


### [164] [Fixed-Boost Wigner Noise: Strict Trace-Distance Contraction without Quantum Degradability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12994)
*Maxim V. Churilov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于固定观测者洛伦兹 boosts，当动量存在不确定性时，Wigner 旋转会严格收缩所有成对的自旋态迹距离，但这种收缩无法通过量子信道后处理排序（即不产生退化信道）。对于自旋 1/2，刻画了 Wigner 角与横向动量方向生成的倒易对称信道锥，其中包含 Pauli 族 M_α。虽然 M_β 比 M_α 更严格地收缩所有迹距离，但唯一的线性后处理因子具有负的 Choi 特征值。通过优化找到最佳转换器的距离为 α(β-α)/(2-3α)，而反向缺陷为 β-α。该构造可通过窄波包极限实现，并给出显式扰动与有限层析界限。此外，所有非恒等成员无法嵌入时间齐次 Pauli 对角 Lindblad 半群。因此，自旋可区分性的全序并不决定量子统计后处理序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相对论性洛伦兹 boost 对自旋量子态可区分性的影响，特别是动量不确定性导致的 Wigner 旋转是否会在量子信息理论中产生有意义的信道排序，以及这种排序与量子统计后处理序之间的关系。

Method: 使用 Wigner 旋转描述洛伦兹 boost 对自旋的影响，引入动量不确定性的统计混合。对于自旋 1/2，通过倒易对称性刻画信道锥，求解最佳物理转换器的 diamond 距离优化问题。利用窄波包极限构造显式动量态，并给出扰动界和有限层析界。

Result: 1) 固定观测者 boost 下，动量不确定性导致的自旋信道严格收缩所有迹距离，但不产生退化信道。2) Pauli 族 M_α 中，M_β 比 M_α 更严格收缩所有迹距离，但唯一的线性后处理因子具有负 Choi 特征值。3) 最佳物理转换器的距离为 α(β-α)/(2-3α)，反向缺陷为 β-α。4) 所有非恒等成员无法嵌入时间齐次 Pauli 对角 Lindblad 半群。

Conclusion: 自旋态迹距离的严格全序（即所有可区分性指标均一致收缩）并不足以保证量子统计后处理序的存在。这表明在量子信息处理中，区分性序与后处理序是两种独立的概念，不能相互替代。

Abstract: A Lorentz boost acts on the canonical spin of a massive particle through a momentum-dependent Wigner rotation. We show that, for one fixed observer boost, reducing over an uncertain momentum can strictly contract every pairwise spin-state trace distance without producing a channel that is degradable from the less contracted one. For spin $1/2$, we first characterize the exact inversion-symmetric channel cone generated by a fixed Wigner angle and transverse momentum directions. Inside this cone lies the Pauli family $M_α=\operatorname{diag}(1-α,1-α,1-2α)$, $0\leqα<1/2$. For $0<α<β<1/2$, all trace distances between distinct spin states are strictly smaller after $M_β$ than after $M_α$, yet the unique linear post-processing factor has a negative normalized Choi eigenvalue. We solve the optimization over all physical converters exactly: $\frac{1}{2}\inf_{Λ\in\mathrm{CPTP}}\|Φ_β-Λ\circΦ_α\|_\diamond=\frac{α(β-α)}{2-3α}$, whereas the reverse deficiency is $β-α$. Thus the identity dominates the family, while all positive-noise members are pairwise incomparable under CPTP post-processing. The ideal construction is realized as the narrow-packet limit of pure, normalizable five-component momentum states, and explicit perturbation and finite-shot tomography bounds certify an open set of examples. Separately, every nonidentity member fails embedding in a time-homogeneous Pauli-diagonal Lindblad semigroup. Hence ordering all unassisted spin distinguishabilities does not determine the quantum statistical post-processing order.

</details>


### [165] [Expressibility and trainability of a two-dimensional pairwise quantum-circuit ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12996)
*Shuai Zhang,Wei Liu,Ji-Chong Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种受量子硬件2D连接结构启发的原生2D成对参数化量子电路（PQC）ansatz，并与代表性1D ansatz在16量子比特系统上比较了表达能力和可训练性。实验表明，2D ansatz在浅层（L=1,2）时KL散度最小，其二阶框架势更快逼近理论下界，且梯度方差在L=1-4时更小，但随着层数增加（L=5-6）差异逐渐缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PQC的ansatz设计多采用硬件无关或简化的1D链/环纠缠模式，而随着量子硬件发展，原生2D连接结构（如平面超导量子比特架构）变得越来越重要，因此需要设计与之匹配的ansatz并进行性能评估。

Method: 构造原生2D成对ansatz，在相同层深度下（尽管电路深度不同）与三种代表性1D ansatz在16量子比特系统上比较；使用KL散度衡量表达能力，二阶框架势衡量接近理论下界的速度，梯度方差（对Pauli-Z-string期望值关于第一个R_y角）衡量可训练性。

Result: 2D ansatz在L=1,2时KL散度最小；其二阶框架势在浅层时更快逼近理论下界；梯度方差在L=1-4时小于1D ansatz，L=5时差异缩小，L=6时四种ansatz统计上兼容。

Conclusion: 原生2D ansatz在浅层场景下相比1D ansatz具有更好的表达能力和可训练性，为适应量子硬件2D连接结构的变分量子算法设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Parameterized quantum circuits~(PQCs) constitute a central building block of variational quantum algorithms~(VQAs) and quantum machine learning~(QML) methods. Existing ansatz designs often adopt hardware-agnostic or simplified 1D chain/ring entanglement patterns. However, as quantum hardware continues to develop, native 2D connectivity patterns, such as planar superconducting-qubit architectures, are becoming increasingly important. Inspired by this hardware structure, we construct a native 2D pairwise ansatz and compare its expressibility and trainability with representative 1D ansatze at identical layer depths, despite their different circuit depths. For the fixed 16-qubit system, the 2D ansatz has the smallest KL divergence at $L=1$ and $2$, and its second-order frame potential approaches the theoretical lower bound more rapidly at shallow layer counts than the frame potentials of the three 1D ansatze. We also evaluate the gradient variance of the Pauli-$Z$-string expectation value $\langle Z_0\otimes\cdots\otimes Z_{15}\rangle$ with respect to the first $R_y$ angle. For this Pauli-$Z$ string and fixed parameter, the gradient variance is smaller for the 2D circuit at $L=1$--$4$. The differences narrow at $L=5$, and the four ansatze yield statistically compatible variances at $L=6$.

</details>


### [166] [Spin Chain Quantum Communication on a Trapped-Ion Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12999)
*Madhumita Sarkar,Trinity Pointon,Sougato Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在IonQ的捕获离子量子处理器上，通过数字模拟自旋哈密顿量，实验实现了工程化量子通信协议。结果表明，工程化耦合分布能显著提升量子态传输保真度，且利用哈密顿量对易结构的并行Trotter分解相比传统顺序实现可大幅降低电路深度和执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离量子比特间的高效通信是扩展量子处理器规模的核心挑战之一，尽管工程化自旋链协议在理论上已被广泛研究，但其实验实现仍然相对有限。

Method: 在IonQ的Forte 1/Forte Enterprise 1捕获离子量子处理器上，通过数字模拟自旋哈密顿量，结合精确数值模拟与量子硬件实验，对比了均匀最近邻耦合与工程化耦合分布，并利用哈密顿量对易结构实现并行Trotter分解。

Result: 工程化相互作用显著提升了量子态传输保真度；并行Trotter分解相比传统顺序实现，在更忠实再现目标动力学的同时，大幅降低了电路深度和执行时间。

Conclusion: 可编程量子处理器能够有效地实现量子通信协议，使基于哈密顿量的量子通信更接近实用量子技术。

Abstract: Efficient communication between distant qubits is one of the central challenges in scaling quantum processors. Although engineered spin chain protocols have been extensively investigated theoretically, their experimental realization has remained comparatively limited. Here, we experimentally realize engineered quantum communication protocols through digitally simulated spin Hamiltonian on IonQ's Forte 1/ Forte Enterprise 1 trapped-ion quantum processor. Combining exact numerical simulations with quantum hardware experiments, we benchmark uniform nearest-neighbour and engineered coupling profiles and demonstrate that engineered interactions significantly enhance the fidelity of quantum state transfer. We further show that exploiting the commutation structure of the spin Hamiltonian enables a parallel Trotter decomposition that more faithfully reproduces the target dynamics while substantially reducing the circuit depth and execution time compared to the conventional sequential implementations. Our results demonstrate that programmable quantum processors can effectively realize and efficiently implement quantum communication protocols, bringing Hamiltonian-based quantum communication closer to practical quantum technologies.

</details>
